Result: 在已知真值的实验模型数据集上验证了DKPL学习物理意义纳米结构的能力；在铁电材料中成功区分高/低特征畴壁角度；发现铒锰氧化物中head-to-head和tail-to-tail两种畴壁构型。

Conclusion: DKPL成功整合专家知识到自主显微镜实验中，为专家引导的自驱动实验室提供了新路径。该方法能学习物理意义的纳米结构，有效区分铁电畴壁特征角度，并发现多种畴壁构型。研究表明基于专家判断的成对学习可突破传统标量指标驱动学习的局限。

Abstract: Self-driving laboratories or autonomous experimentation are emerging as transformative platforms for accelerating scientific discovery. Bayesian optimization (BO) is among the most widely used machine learning frameworks for these purposes, but these BO-based frameworks rely on predefined scalar descriptors to guide experimentation. In many situations, the determination of an appropriate scalar descriptor can be challenging, and may fail to capture subtle yet scientifically important phenomena apparent to experts with interdisciplinary insight. To overcome this limitation, here we develop deep-kernel pairwise learning (DKPL), an approach for autonomous microscopy experiments which incorporates human expertise and interdisciplinary scientific knowledge into an active learning loop. Instead of relying on explicit scalar objectives, DKPL enables experts to directly evaluate which experimental output is more promising using interdisciplinary knowledge. DKPL then learns a latent utility function from these expert judgements to guide subsequent autonomous microscopy experiments. We demonstrate DKPL's performance in learning physically meaningful nanoscale structures while effectively prioritizing high-information measurement regions using an experimental model dataset with known ground truth. We further apply DKPL to analyze the character of ferroelectric domain walls, where we find DKPL capable of distinguishing between high and low characteristic domain-wall angles in bismuth ferrite, and able to discover both head-to-head and tail-to-tail domain-wall character in erbium manganite. This development establishes an approach to integrate expert knowledge into autonomous microscopy experiments and demonstrates a pathway toward expert-guided self-driving laboratories capable of addressing scientific problems beyond the limits of scalar-metrics-driven learning.

</details>


### [127] [On-Policy Consistency Training Improves LLM Safety with Minimal Capability Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21834)
*Andy Han,Kristina Fujimoto,Avidan Shah,Kiet Nguyen,Kai Xu,Chen Yueh-Han,Ilia Sucholutsky,Rico Angell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 OPCT（在线策略一致性训练），通过利用模型自身响应作为对比监督信号，解决了现有一致性训练方法泛化差和能力退化的问题，在三个模型家族上验证了在谄媚、越狱防御、安全意识三项安全指标上均优于基于 SFT 的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐模型存在谄媚、越狱漏洞、安全警告缺失等问题；现有对比一致性训练方法依赖离线生成的监督信号和监督微调（SFT），导致模型仅记忆表面形式泛化能力差且能力退化。

Method: 提出 On-Policy Consistency Training (OPCT)，利用模型自身对对比提示的响应作为监督信号，在线更新模型权重。

Result: OPCT 在三项安全指标上全面超越 SFT：谄媚率降至 8.1%（SFT 为 11.2%），自适应攻击下 jailbreak 防御成功率达 99%（SFT 为 87%），安全意识提升，且避免了 SFT 导致的 28 分 MATH-500 能力退化。

Conclusion: OPCT 在三项安全指标上均优于 SFT，且避免了能力退化（如 MATH-500 下降 28 分的问题），证明了一致性训练应采用在线策略而非离线 SFT 方法。

Abstract: Aligned models can misbehave in several ways: they are often sycophantic, fall victim to jailbreaks, or fail to include appropriate safety warnings. Consistency training is a promising new alignment paradigm to mitigate such failures by training invariants into the model using contrastive input pairs. Existing consistency training procedures generate the supervision signal once, offline, and use supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to update the model. Unfortunately, the resulting models tend to merely memorize the surface forms of the training distribution and thus generalize poorly and regress in their capabilities. We introduce On-Policy Consistency Training (OPCT), a new consistency training approach where the objective is computed over the model's own responses to prompts, supervised by itself conditioned on corresponding contrastive prompts. We evaluate OPCT on three safety axes: sycophancy, jailbreaking, and safety awareness. Across three model families, OPCT outperforms its SFT counterpart on all safety desiderata. It nearly halves the sycophancy rate relative to baseline (8.1% vs. 15.4%, compared to 11.2% for SFT). Under an adaptive per-target attacker, OPCT holds jailbreak defense success near 99% on held-out jailbreak behaviors, whereas SFT achieves 87% on average. On safety awareness, OPCT outperforms SFT in two out of three models, and matches it on the other. OPCT also largely avoids the capability regressions that SFT induces, such as a 28-point drop on MATH-500. Our results suggest that consistency training is best implemented as OPCT rather than as SFT, especially when generalization beyond the training distribution is desired.

</details>


### [128] [Energy-Gated Attention: Spectral Salience as an Inductive Bias for Transformer Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21842)
*Athanasios Zeris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出能量门控注意力(EGA)，通过谱能量加权token重要性，在极低参数开销下显著改善语言建模性能，发现能量阈值与实词比例高度吻合，提示语言建模与湍流动力学存在深层结构相似性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer注意力平等对待所有token，与湍流流体动力学中相干结构携带不成比例能量的规律形成类比。假设：信息密度高的token（如形态边界、句法核心、话语标记）应比背景token获得更多注意力。

Method: 提出Energy-Gated Attention (EGA)：通过单个可学习线性投影计算嵌入场的谱能量，识别主导谱模式，据此对value聚合进行门控。实验部分系统消融三种小波族（固定Morlet、Daubechies db2/db4、参数化Morlet）。

Result: TinyShakespeare验证损失提升+0.103（仅12,480额外参数，<0.26%开销，无额外计算成本）；Penn Treebank提升+0.101，证明跨数据集泛化性；学习阈值收敛至tau≈0.35，对应约36% token携带高于平均谱能量。

Conclusion: EGA通过极低开销（<0.26%参数增量）实现显著性能提升，验证了"信息密集token应获得更多注意力"的假设。关键发现：（1）最优能量方向是数据自适应的、非正弦的，固定小波基次优；（2）学习到的能量阈值tau≈0.35恰好对应英语文本中实词的分布比例（约36%），表明该方法捕获了稳定的语言学属性；（3）未来方向：学习小波包分解。

Abstract: Standard transformer attention computes pairwise similarity between queries and keys, treating all tokens as equally salient regardless of their intrinsic informational content. In turbulent fluid dynamics, coherent structures -- the energetically dominant, spatially organized patterns that persist amid background chaos -- carry a disproportionate fraction of total energy and govern all transport. We propose that tokens play an analogous role in transformer attention: informationally dense positions (morphological boundaries, syntactic heads, discourse markers) concentrate spectral energy and should attract proportionally more attention than background tokens (function words, repeated patterns, low-information filler). We propose Energy-Gated Attention (EGA): a simple modification that gates value aggregation by the spectral energy of key token embeddings, computed by a single learned linear projection that discovers the dominant spectral mode of the embedding field. On TinyShakespeare, EGA achieves +0.103 validation loss improvement with only 12,480 additional parameters (<0.26% overhead) and no measurable computational cost. The result is consistent on Penn Treebank (+0.101), demonstrating dataset independence. A systematic ablation across three wavelet families (fixed Morlet, Daubechies db2/db4, and a parametric Morlet) establishes that fixed structured bases are suboptimal -- the optimal energy direction is data-adaptive and non-sinusoidal -- while identifying learned wavelet packets as a promising open direction. The learned energy threshold converges to tau ~= 0.35 independently of initialization, corresponding to the fraction (~36%) of tokens carrying above-average spectral energy in English text, a stable linguistic property consistent with the fraction of content words in running English text.

</details>


### [129] [Geometry-Adaptive Explainer for Faithful Dictionary-Based Interpretability under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21849)
*Sungjun Lim,Heedong Kim,Andrew Lee,Kyungwoo Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对字典类解释器在分布偏移下的忠实性问题，提出几何对齐方法GAE，通过重对齐解释器字典与OOD活跃子空间，在无需梯度更新和标签数据的条件下，有效提升了模型解释在分布外的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 字典类解释器（如稀疏自编码器、转换器）是机制可解释性的主要工具，但其在分布偏移下的忠实性缺乏系统研究。分布偏移会旋转模型活跃使用的子空间，导致在分布内训练的字典与OOD活跃子空间产生错位。

Method: 提出Geometry-Adaptive Explainer (GAE)，在不更新梯度的情况下，仅利用无标签OOD激活数据将解释器字典与OOD活跃子空间进行几何对齐，同时保持原始特征结构。

Result: 理论证明GAE的额外损失以二阶矩偏移的二次方为界；实验表明GAE在多个模型和OOD设置下的因果忠实性匹配或超越所有需训练的基线方法。

Conclusion: GAE通过几何对齐策略有效解决了分布偏移下的忠实性问题，仅需无标签OOD数据且无需梯度更新，即可实现与甚至超越需训练的基线方法的因果忠实性表现。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability aims to explain a model's behavior by identifying causally responsible internal structures. Dictionary-based explainers such as sparse autoencoders and transcoders are a primary tool, but their faithfulness under out-of-distribution (OOD) shift has received little systematic attention. We show that distribution shift rotates the subspace that the model actively uses, misaligning the explainer's dictionary trained on in-distribution (ID) activations. We formalize this misalignment as the faithfulness gap, a geometric distance between the ID dictionary and the OOD-active subspace, and show that it controls OOD faithfulness degradation. To reduce this gap, we propose the Geometry-Adaptive Explainer (GAE), which realigns the explainer's dictionary with the OOD-active subspace while preserving the original feature structure. This requires only unlabeled OOD activations and no gradient updates. We prove that GAE improves over the unadapted ID explainer, with excess loss bounded quadratically by the second-moment shift. Empirically, GAE even matches or surpasses all training-based baselines in causal faithfulness across multiple models and OOD settings.

</details>


### [130] [The Illusion of Reasoning: Exposing Evasive Data Contamination in LLMs via Zero-CoT Truncation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21856)
*Yifan Lan,Yuanpu Cao,Hanyu Wang,Lu Lin,Jinghui Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对LLM评估中的数据污染问题，特别是难以检测的规避性污染，本文发现模型生成的推理步骤会主动掩盖其底层记忆化现象。基于此发现，提出ZCP方法，通过截断思维链过程并比较原始基准与扰动数据集上的零CoT性能来检测污染，同时引入污染置信度指标量化污染程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据污染破坏了LLM能力的客观评估，且恶意模型发布者采用规避性污染策略（如释义基准数据）来规避现有检测方法，人为提升排行榜表现。

Method: ZCP通过截断整个思维链(CoT)过程来暴露潜在的捷径映射，并比较模型在原始基准测试和等构扰动参考数据集上的零CoT性能，以隔离记忆与模型内在问题解决能力。同时引入"污染置信度"指标来量化污染的可能性和严重程度。

Result: 实验表明ZCP在已识别的污染模型和特别微调的污染模型上均能稳健检测直接和规避性数据污染。

Conclusion: ZCP能够鲁棒地检测直接污染和规避性污染，为LLM评估提供了有效的黑盒检测工具。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated impressive reasoning abilities across a wide range of tasks, but data contamination undermines the objective evaluation of these capabilities. This problem is further exacerbated by malicious model publishers who use evasive, or indirect, contamination strategies, such as paraphrasing benchmark data to evade existing detection methods and artificially boost leaderboard performance. Current approaches struggle to reliably detect such stealthy contamination. In this work, we uncover a critical phenomenon: a model's generated reasoning steps actively mask its underlying memorization. Inspired by this, we propose the Zero-CoT Probe (ZCP), a novel black-box detection method that deliberately truncates the entire Chain-of-Thought (CoT) process to expose latent shortcut mappings. To further isolate memorization from the model's intrinsic problem-solving capabilities, ZCP compares the model's zero-CoT performance on the original benchmark against an isomorphically perturbed reference dataset. Furthermore, we introduce Contamination Confidence, a metric that quantifies both the likelihood and severity of contamination, moving beyond simple binary classifications. Extensive experiments on both previously identified contaminated models and specially fine-tuned contaminated models demonstrate that ZCP robustly detects both direct and evasive data contamination. The code for ZCP is accessible at https://github.com/Yifan-Lan/zero-cot-probe.

</details>


### [131] [When to Switch, Not Just What: Transition Quality Prediction in Clash Royale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21868)
*Heeyun Heo,Huy Kang Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现竞争游戏中策略切换频率与胜率负相关，提出TQP三阶段管道解决现有推荐系统的零切换成本假设问题，通过PersonaGate、TimingGate和ScoreFusion组件以及SwitchGap评估指标，有效提升策略推荐质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玩家在竞争游戏中频繁在失败后切换策略，但分析92万余场匹配记录发现切换频率与胜率呈负相关。现有推荐系统存在"零切换成本假设"问题——仅评估策略的期望质量，忽视切换的行为成本和个体差异。这导致推荐系统无法有效帮助低胜率玩家。

Method: 将策略推荐重新表述为转换级决策问题，实现为TQP（转换质量预测器）三阶段管道（Who -> When -> What）。包含三个核心组件：PersonaGate用于抑制那些战略一致性经验上与更好结果相关的玩家的推荐；TimingGate使用子类型和状态匹配的基线来识别切换可能产生净收益的时刻；ScoreFusion通过结合可采纳性信号与预测的转换质量（delta WR）来排名候选策略。此外引入SwitchGap评估指标，衡量策略的判别质量。

Result: 完整管道在5.4%推荐率下实现+10.4个百分点的SwitchGap。尽管损失触发的切换者是表现最差的群体，但他们从子类型条件化指导中获得最大收益。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了策略推荐中的切换成本问题，在5.4%推荐率下实现+10.4个百分点SwitchGap，且损失触发的切换者（表现最差的群体）从子类型条件化指导中获益最大，证明了该框架的实际价值。

Abstract: In competitive games, players frequently switch strategies after losing streaks, yet our analysis of 926,334 match records from 34,619 Clash Royale players reveals a counterintuitive pattern: switching frequency is inversely associated with the win rate, with effects that vary substantially across players and situational contexts. We attribute this to a limitation common in many prior recommendation systems, which evaluate strategies by expected quality while overlooking the behavioral cost of switching and individual differences in switching propensity. We refer to this implicit premise as the Zero Switching Cost Assumption. To address this, we reformulate strategy recommendation as a transition-level decision problem and instantiate it as TQP (Transition Quality Predictor), a three-stage pipeline structured as Who -> When -> What. PersonaGate suppresses recommendations for players whose strategic consistency is empirically associated with superior outcomes. TimingGate identifies moments when switching is likely to yield a net benefit over staying, using a subtype- and state-matched baseline to control for natural win-rate recovery. ScoreFusion ranks candidate strategies by combining an adoptability signal with predicted transition quality (delta WR). We further introduce SwitchGap, an evaluation metric that measures a policy's discriminative quality without treating observed player choices as optimal ground truth. This property is particularly important because the most frequent switchers record the lowest win rates. The full pipeline achieves a SwitchGap of +10.4 percentage points at a recommendation rate of 5.4%, and loss-triggered switchers, despite being the lowest-performing group, benefit the most from subtype-conditioned guidance.

</details>


### [132] [Noise Schedule Design for Diffusion Models: An Optimal Control Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21911)
*Seo Taek Kong,Weina Wang,R. Srikant*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper proposes an optimal control framework for designing noise schedules in diffusion models, using Fisher information as the state variable. The approach provides theoretical guarantees for Õ(d/n) sampling error with practical schedules (including extensions of exponential/sigmoid) and demonstrates empirical improvements on image generation benchmarks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: While existing theory proves Õ(d/n) sampling error bounds are achievable for diffusion models, these results only hold for specific noise schedules that do not include the exponential and sigmoid schedules used in practice. This gap between theory and practice motivates the need for a principled framework to analyze and design noise schedules.

Method: The authors recast the noise schedule design as an optimal control problem where Fisher information serves as the state variable (evolving via ODE), and the noise schedule acts as the control input. The objective is a functional involving Fisher information that bounds the KL sampling error. Sufficient conditions for achieving Õ(d/n) sampling error are derived, with closed-form noise schedule expressions obtained under parametric assumptions on the data distribution.

Result: The optimal control formulation yields sufficient conditions on noise schedules guaranteeing Õ(d/n) sampling error (where d is data dimension, n is discretization steps). Under parametric assumptions on the data distribution, closed-form expressions are derived that generalize standard empirical schedules (exponential, sigmoid). Systematic parameter tuning produces new schedules achieving superior FID scores on image generation benchmarks.

Conclusion: The framework provides a principled approach that achieves both theoretical tractability (noise schedules with proven Õ(d/n) error bounds, including schedules that match practical use) and empirical performance (new tuned schedules achieving superior FID scores on image generation benchmarks).

Abstract: We develop a principled framework for analyzing and designing noise schedules in diffusion models. We show that one can recast this design problem as an optimal control problem, whose state is the Fisher information of the diffusion process which evolves according to an ODE and the control input is the noise schedule. The objective of the optimal control problem is a functional involving the Fisher information, which is shown to be an upper bound on the Kullback-Leibler sampling error. By solving this optimal control problem, we obtain sufficient conditions on noise schedules under which state-of-the-art $\tilde{\mathcal{O}} (d/n)$ sampling error is achievable, where $d$ is the data dimension and $n$ is the number of discretization steps. While existing theoretical work also prove that $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(d/n)$ sampling error bounds are achievable, these results hold for specific noise schedules, which do not include the schedules used in practice. Under a further parametric assumption on the data distribution, we show that one can obtain closed-form expressions for the noise schedules. These noise schedules generalize standard empirical schedules such as exponential and sigmoid schedules by allowing additional parameters that can be tuned. Systematically tuning the parameters of these schedules yields new schedules that achieve superior FID scores on image generation benchmarks.

</details>


### [133] [CausalGuard: Conformal Inference under Graph Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21928)
*Vikash Singh,Weicong Chen,Debargha Ganguly,Yanyan Zhang,Nengbo Wang,Sreehari Sankar,Mohsen Hariri,Alexander Nemecek,Chaoda Song,Shouren Wang,Biyao Zhang,Van Yang,Erman Ayday,Jing Ma,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CausalGuard addresses treatment effect estimation under unknown causal graphs by integrating LLM priors, conditional-independence pruning, and BIC-based reweighting into a conformal framework, providing finite-sample coverage guarantees while improving efficiency over graph-agnostic methods.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Graph-agnostic conformal methods require large padding to regain coverage; graph-based adjustment sets are sensitive to misspecification, leading to under-coverage in causal effect estimation.

Method: Proposes candidate DAGs from LLM-derived edge priors, prunes them via conditional-independence tests, reweights by BIC, then calibrates using structure-weighted conformal framework on aggregated graph-conditional doubly robust pseudo-outcomes with composite nonconformity scores.

Result: Achieves marginal coverage above 90% nominal level; reduces interval width compared to graph-agnostic conformal baselines; suppresses invalid collider adjustments; remains stable under misspecified priors.

Conclusion: CausalGuard achieves nominal coverage and reduced interval width across five benchmarks, effectively suppresses invalid collider adjustments, and remains stable under misspecified priors when data supports the retained candidate set.

Abstract: Estimating treatment effects from observational data requires choosing an adjustment set, but valid adjustment depends on an unknown causal graph. Graph misspecification can cause under-coverage, while graph-agnostic conformal wrappers may regain nominal coverage only through large padding. We introduce CausalGuard, a structure-weighted conformal framework that calibrates after aggregating graph-conditional doubly robust pseudo-outcomes. Candidate DAGs are proposed from an LLM-derived edge prior, pruned by conditional-independence tests, and reweighted by Bayesian Information Criterion. A composite nonconformity score then calibrates the posterior-weighted pseudo-outcome. CausalGuard provides distribution-free finite-sample marginal coverage for this aggregated pseudo-outcome; under causal identification, overlap, conditional-mean nuisance stability, and concentration on target-aligned valid adjustment strategies, its conditional mean converges to the true Conditional Average Treatment Effect. Across five benchmarks, CausalGuard attains mean coverage above the nominal 90% level for the directly evaluable target and reduces width when graph-agnostic conformal baselines require large padding. Stress tests show that CausalGuard suppresses invalid collider adjustment and remains stable under misspecified priors when the retained candidate set is data-supported.

</details>


### [134] [Optimal Guarantees for Auditing Rényi Differentially Private Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21938)
*Benjamin D. Kim,Lav R. Varshney,Daniel Alabi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于假设检验的黑盒审计框架，用于验证机器学习算法的Rényi差分隐私(RDP)保证。该方法使用Donsker-Varadhan变分估计器直接估计邻居执行间的Rényi散度，提供了非渐近置信区间，并证明了样本复杂度的最优下界（对数因子内最优）。在MNIST和CIFAR-10数据集上对DP-SGD的实验表明，该方法在小和中等等Rényi阶数上显著优于现有最优黑盒方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing black-box auditing methods for Rényi differential privacy (RDP) are limited in accuracy, particularly at small and moderate Rényi orders where precise auditing is most challenging; there is a need for optimal sample complexity guarantees.

Method: Hypothesis testing framework using class-restricted Donsker-Varadhan (DV) variational estimator to directly estimate Rényi divergence between neighboring executions; non-asymptotic confidence interval analysis separating statistical estimation error from algorithmic privacy leakage.

Result: Established first optimal (up to logarithmic factors) sample complexity guarantees for auditing RDP via DV estimators; empirical results on DP-SGD across MNIST and CIFAR-10 show significant improvements over prior black-box methods, especially at challenging small and moderate Rényi orders.

Conclusion: First optimal audit framework for RDP via DV estimators with logarithmic-optimal sample complexity; empirical evaluation demonstrates substantial improvements over state-of-the-art black-box methods, particularly at small and moderate Rényi orders where auditing is most challenging.

Abstract: We study black-box auditing for machine learning algorithms that claim R \ 'enyi differential privacy (RDP) guarantees. We introduce an auditing framework, based on hypothesis testing, that directly estimates Rényi divergence between neighboring executions using the Donsker-Varadhan (DV) variational estimator. Our analysis yields explicit and non-asymptotic confidence intervals for RDP auditing via class-restricted DV estimators, separating statistical estimation error from algorithmic privacy leakage. We prove matching minimax lower bounds showing that, up to logarithmic factors, our sample-complexity guarantees are information-theoretically optimal, thereby establishing the first optimal guarantees for auditing RDP via DV estimators. Empirically, we instantiate our framework for auditing DP-SGD in a fully black-box setting. Across MNIST and CIFAR-10, and over a wide range of privacy regimes, our auditors produce a strong overall improvement on empirical RDP lower bounds compared to prior state-of-the-art black-box methods especially at small and moderate Rényi orders where accurate auditing is most challenging.

</details>


### [135] [SCI-Defense: Defending Manipulation Attacks from Generative Engine Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21948)
*Xucheng Yu,Haibo Jin,Huimin Zeng,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCI-Defense是一个针对GEO攻击的三组件防御框架，通过结合困惑度检测(PPL)、语义完整性评分(SIS)和候选间检测(ICD)来评估四个操控维度(权威归因、叙事目的性、比较声明、时间声明)。在600个Amazon产品和600个MS MARCO段落上的实验表明，该框架在产品描述场景下表现优异，但对Web段落存在局限。现有防御手段对语义操控攻击无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-based ranking systems are vulnerable to Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) attacks, where adversaries inject semantic signals into product descriptions to artificially boost rankings.

Method: SCI-Defense combines Perplexity detection (PPL), Semantic Integrity Scoring (SIS), and Inter-Candidate Detection (ICD). SIS evaluates four manipulation dimensions: Authority Attribution (AA), Narrative Purposiveness (NP), Comparative Claims (CA), and Temporal Claims (TC).

Result: On 600 Amazon products (6 categories): Precision=1.000, FPR=0.000; Recall=1.000/0.952/0.830 against String/Reasoning/Review attacks. On 600 MS MARCO passages: String attacks blocked perfectly, Review attacks near-zero recall. Existing defenses (PPL-only, SafetyClf, paraphrasing) achieve zero recall against semantic manipulation.

Conclusion: New attacks (Specification Amplification and Use-Case Saturation) expose semantic relevance manipulation as a structural defense blind spot, indicating future research directions.

Abstract: LLM-based ranking systems are vulnerable to Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) attacks, where adversaries inject semantic signals into product descriptions to artificially boost rankings. We propose SCI-Defense, a three-component defense framework combining Perplexity detection (PPL), Semantic Integrity Scoring (SIS), and Inter-Candidate Detection (ICD). SIS evaluates four manipulation dimensions: Authority Attribution (AA), Narrative Purposiveness (NP), Comparative Claims (CA), and Temporal Claims (TC). Evaluated on 600 Amazon product descriptions across 6 categories, SCI-Defense achieves Precision=1.000 and FPR=0.000, with Recall of 1.000, 0.952, and 0.830 against String, Reasoning, and Review attacks respectively. On 600 MS MARCO web passages, String attacks are blocked with perfect recall while Review attacks yield near-zero recall, as web passages lack the persuasion-oriented signals that SIS targets in product descriptions. We demonstrate that existing defenses -- PPL-only filters, SafetyClf content classifiers, and paraphrasing -- achieve zero recall against semantic manipulation attacks. We further demonstrate new attacks such as Specification Amplification and Use-Case Saturation can expose semantic relevance manipulation as a structural defense blind spot that suggests directions for future research.

</details>


### [136] [Dynamic Mixture of Latent Memories for Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21951)
*Dianzhi Yu,Vireo Zhang,Hongru Wang,Yanyu Chen,Minda Hu,Wanghan Xu,Siki Chen,Philip Torr,Zhenfei Yin,Irwin King*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MoLEM框架，通过动态专家混合架构将多个专家作为独立记忆载体，路由器根据键值匹配选择专家并注入潜在记忆到推理过程。基础模型保持冻结以避免灾难性遗忘，在数学、科学和代码领域的持续学习实验中相比基线提升10.40%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在根本缺陷：更新模型参数的方法会导致灾难性遗忘，而依赖外部记忆的方法无法真正增强模型内在能力。智能体的自我进化需要在变化的任务序列中持续积累新知识，同时保留已有能力。

Method: 提出MoLEM框架，采用动态专家混合(MoE)架构生成潜在记忆。路由器通过键值匹配选择和加权专家，聚合后的潜在记忆注入推理过程。基础推理模型完全冻结，所有经验知识内化到附加模块中。每个训练阶段配备轻量级自编码器用于推理时路由选择，未匹配阶段输入则回退到预训练模型。

Result: 经过完整的持续学习序列后，方法相比Vanilla预训练基线平均准确率提升10.40%。在不同的训练顺序下，竞品方法均无法持续超越基线，而本方法表现稳定。

Conclusion: MoLEM通过冻结基础模型+额外模块的方式，有效解决了灾难性遗忘问题，在数学、科学和代码领域的持续学习任务中平均准确率提升10.40%，且性能不受训练顺序影响，优于现有方法。

Abstract: Achieving self-evolution in intelligent agents requires the continual accumulation of new knowledge across changing task sequences without forgetting previously acquired abilities. Existing approaches either internalize knowledge by updating model parameters, which induces catastrophic forgetting, or rely on external memory, which fails to genuinely enhance the model's intrinsic capabilities. We propose MoLEM, a generative mixture of latent memory framework based on a dynamic mixture-of-experts (MoE). We treat multiple experts as independent carriers to generate memory. A router selects and weights experts through key-query matching, and the aggregated latent memory is injected into the reasoning process. The base model for reasoning remains entirely frozen, with all experiential knowledge internalized into the additional modules, avoiding catastrophic forgetting. For continual learning, each training stage is paired with a lightweight autoencoder that selects the appropriate routing group at inference, and inputs that match no stage fall back to the pretrained model. Experiments train the framework on continual-learning sequences spanning math, science, and code domains. After training, we evaluate the framework on the corresponding test sets to measure task learning and competence preservation across continual adaptation stages. After the full continual-learning sequence, our method improves the average accuracy by 10.40% over the Vanilla pretrained baseline, while none of the competing methods consistently exceed this baseline across different training orders.

</details>


### [137] [ChronoMedicalWorld: A Medical World Model for Learning Patient Trajectories from Longitudinal Care Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21963)
*Jiangyuan Wang,Xuyong Chen,Junwei He,Xu Xu,Shasha Xie,Fuman Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CMWM是一种动作条件隐变量世界模型框架，用于从纵向护理数据学习患者轨迹。采用联合嵌入状态编码器与整合结构化/文本干预的宽动作编码器，通过循环隐转换模块和六项损失函数（含生理感知先验）训练。在2,232例CKD患者队列中，eGFR预测MAE达7.384，相比GPT-5.5基线降低7.28%，框架可推广至任意慢性病。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢性病护理需要预测患者在多年时间跨度内、指定干预下的生理演变。现有电子健康记录(EHR)模型多为判别式，大语言模型在重复干预下会出现漂移问题，难以满足长期临床模拟需求。

Method: CMWM采用联合嵌入状态编码器与宽动作编码器（整合结构化干预指标与自由文本通信嵌入）处理纵向护理数据；通过循环隐变量转换模块在六项损失函数下训练（含下一个观测监督、隐空间预测、SIGReg正则化及三个生理感知形状先验：斜率、连续性、大跳惩罚）；采用闭环滚动前缀协议使训练与部署条件一致。

Result: 在2,232例肾内科患者队列中，CMWM实现了动态50%历史滚动测试MAE=7.384、RMSE=10.256，优于调优GPT-5.5结构化提示基线的7.964和11.069（MAE降低7.28%，RMSE降低7.35%），提升主要来自患者-健康教练对话部分。

Conclusion: CMWM提出了一种通用的慢性病长期预测框架，通过动作条件隐变量世界模型实现了相比GPT-5.5 baseline更优的预测精度（MAE降低7.28%，RMSE降低7.35%），且框架不限于CKD，可推广至任何具有周期性临床状态和干预的慢性病场景。

Abstract: Long-horizon clinical simulation -- predicting how a patient's physiology evolves over years under specified interventions -- is central to chronic-disease care, yet existing electronic health record (EHR) models are predominantly discriminative, and general-purpose large language models drift under repeated interventions. We propose the \textbf{ChronoMedicalWorld Model (CMWM)}, an action-conditioned latent world-model framework for learning patient trajectories from longitudinal care data. CMWM couples a joint-embedding state encoder with a wide action encoder that admits both structured intervention indicators and free-text communication embeddings, and trains a recurrent latent transition module under a six-term objective: next-observation supervision, next-latent prediction, SIGReg latent regularisation, and three physiology-aware shape priors (slope, continuity, large-jump penalty). A closed-loop rollout-prefix protocol matches training to deployment, so the model is optimised against the same multi-step error it exhibits at inference. As a concrete case study, we instantiate CMWM for annual estimated glomerular filtration rate (eGFR) trajectory forecasting in chronic kidney disease (CKD). On a 2{,}232-patient nephrology cohort, the CKD instantiation achieves a dynamic-50\% history rollout test mean absolute error (MAE) of 7.384 and root-mean-square error (RMSE) of 10.256, against 7.964 and 11.069 for a tuned GPT-5.5 structured-prompting baseline ($-7.28\%$ MAE, $-7.35\%$ RMSE), with the gain dominated by the dialogue portion of patient--health-coach communication. The framework is not CKD-specific: its architecture, loss design, and training protocol apply to any chronic condition that can be cast as periodic clinical state interleaved with structured and conversational interventions.

</details>


### [138] [An Improved Adaptive PID Optimizer with Enhanced Convergence and Stability for Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21968)
*Saurabh Saini,Kapil Ahuja,Thomas Wick,Saurav Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper proposes IAdaPID-ADG, a novel optimizer that integrates AMSGrad's non-increasing effective learning rate and DiffGrad's gradient difference-based modulation factor into AdaPID to address convergence and stability issues. Evaluated on multiple datasets, it significantly outperforms existing optimizers.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing optimizers have two key drawbacks: noisy/varying gradients and overshoot phenomenon. While Adam addresses the first issue and PID optimizers address the second, AdaPID tackles both but still suffers from convergence and stability issues inherited from Adam.

Method: The method combines two key extensions of Adam into AdaPID: (1) Non-increasing effective learning rate from AMSGrad to address convergence issues, and (2) Gradient difference-based modulation factor from DiffGrad to address stability issues. The resulting IAdaPID-ADG optimizer tackles both limitations within a unified framework.

Result: IAdaPID-ADG substantially outperforms all competing optimizers on benchmark datasets (MNIST, CIFAR10) and real-world datasets (IARC, AnnoCerv). Ablation study on MNIST demonstrates the individual contribution of each added component.

Conclusion: IAdaPID-ADG optimizer is proposed, which integrates non-increasing effective learning rate (from AMSGrad) and gradient difference-based modulation factor (from DiffGrad) into AdaPID framework. Experimental results on multiple datasets demonstrate substantial improvements over competing optimizers, with ablation study confirming the contribution of each added component.

Abstract: Optimization is essential in deep learning. The foundational method upon which most optimizers are built is momentum-based stochastic gradient descent. However, it suffers from two key drawbacks. First, it has noisy and varying gradients, and second, it has an overshoot phenomenon. To address noisy gradients, Adam was proposed, which remains the most widely used adaptive optimizer. To address the overshoot phenomenon, a control-theory-based PID optimizer was proposed. To tackle both the limitations within a single framework, several variants of Adaptive PID (AdaPID) have recently been proposed.
  Although AdaPID performs well, it still inherits two critical drawbacks from Adam, namely convergence and stability issues. In this work, we address both these limitations. To fix the convergence issue, we uniquely integrate the idea of using a non-increasing effective learning rate into AdaPID (originally proposed in AMSGrad, an extension of Adam). To fix the stability issue, we innovatively integrate a gradient difference based modulation factor into AdaPID (originally proposed in DiffGrad, another extension of Adam). Combining both these ideas in AdaPID, results in our novel IAdaPID-ADG optimizer.
  We evaluate our proposed optimizer on multiple datasets, including benchmark datasets (MNIST and CIFAR10) and real-world datasets (IARC and AnnoCerv). The IAdaPID-ADG substantially outperforms all competing optimizers. Additionally, we perform an ablation study on the MNIST dataset to demonstrate the contribution of each added component.

</details>


### [139] [Reasoning through Verifiable Forecast Actions: Consistency-Grounded RL for Financial LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21975)
*Jialin Chen,Aosong Feng,Harshit Verma,Siyi Gu,Haiwen Wang,Ali Maatouk,Yixuan He,Yifeng Gao,Leandros Tassiulas,Rex Ying*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StockR1通过引入可验证的预测行动机制，将语言推理与时序预测有机结合，解决了传统方法中定性推理与定量预测脱节的问题。在10年金融数据上的实验表明，该方法显著优于时间序列基线和通用LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融市场具有极端非平稳性、低信噪比和强依赖外部信息（新闻、公司基本面、宏观经济信号）的特点。现有方法要么将时间序列抽象为文本，要么将预测与语言推理解耦，导致定性推理与定量结果之间存在根本性错配。

Method: 提出StockR1，一个时间序列增强的LLM，通过可验证的预测行动统一股票预测和金融推理。基于工具调用设计，模型首先输出预测行动（结构化且可解释的市场展望表示），然后基于此行动调用时间序列解码器生成分布式的未来轨迹。使用强化学习优化整个流程，奖励联合反映答案有效性、预测准确性和生成行动与观察到的时序动态之间的一致性，奖励还根据样本级不确定性标量重新加权。

Result: 在大规模10年基准测试中进行金融问答和股票预测评估，持续优于时间序列基线和通用LLM，推理准确率分别提升17.7%（4B模型）和25.9%（8B模型）。

Conclusion: Structuring forecast actions establishes a powerful synergy between language reasoning and temporal prediction, enabling LLMs to reason through verifiable, interpretable, and numerically grounded decisions。

Abstract: Financial markets are characterized by extreme non-stationarity, low signal-to-noise ratios, and strong dependence on external information such as news, company fundamentals, and macroeconomic signals. Yet, existing approaches either abstract time-series into text or decouple forecasting from language-based reasoning, leading to a fundamental mismatch between qualitative reasoning and quantitative outcomes. To address this, we introduce StockR1, a time-series-enhanced LLM that unifies stock forecasting and financial reasoning through a verifiable forecast action. Based on a tool-call design, the model first emits a forecast action, which is a structured and interpretable representation of its qualitative market outlook. It then invokes a time-series decoder conditioned on this action to generate distributional future trajectories, leading to more informed question answering and financial reasoning. We optimize the full pipeline with reinforcement learning, where rewards jointly reflect answer validity, forecast accuracy, and consistency between generated actions and observed time-series dynamics. In addition, rewards are reweighted by a sample-level uncertainty scalar, encouraging the model to accommodate varying uncertainty in market dynamics. We evaluate StockR1 on financial question answering and stock forecasting over a large-scale 10-year benchmark. Our method consistently outperforms time-series baselines and general-purpose LLMs, improving reasoning accuracy by 17.7% (4B) and 25.9% (8B). These findings demonstrate that structuring the forecast actions establishes a powerful synergy between language reasoning and temporal prediction, enabling LLMs to reason through verifiable, interpretable, and numerically grounded decisions.

</details>


### [140] [Check Your LLM's Secret Dictionary! Five Lines of Code Reveal What Your LLM Learned (Including What It Shouldn't Have)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22005)
*Hisashi Miyashita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过对lm_head权重矩阵进行SVD分析，无需模型推理即可揭示transformer语言模型中的可解释语义子空间，发现不同模型具有差异化的子空间结构，提出VCS和WPS指标用于子空间一致性和异常token检测，证明伦理问题子空间源于预训练且难以通过对齐移除，建议将SVD分析纳入发布前安全审计流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模型解释性方法依赖大量推理计算和输入分析，本文提出仅需5行PyTorch代码、无需模型推理的权重分析方法，旨在从模型权重本身直接揭示语义子空间，实现高效的安全审计。

Method: 对transformer语言模型的lm_head权重矩阵进行奇异值分解(SVD)，通过分析左奇异向量识别与相应奇异方向对齐时最易被选择的词汇token，检查这些聚类以揭示模型的训练数据组成和整理策略；引入VCS（词汇聚类得分）量化子空间一致性，WPS（加权投影得分）作为静态异常token检测器。

Result: GPT-OSS-120B表现出功能分化子空间的递阶层次结构；Gemma-2-2B以十九世纪前英语拼写为主，形成阶梯式聚类结构，有助于提高输出可控性；Qwen2.5-1.5B具有广泛多语言覆盖，但存在伦理问题子空间；伦理问题子空间源于预训练，无法通过后训练对齐移除；WPS成功检测到GPT-OSS-120B中著名的shokubutsu-hyakka-tsu异常token。

Conclusion: 提出lm_head SVD分析应作为标准发布前安全审计步骤，并建议将其应用于SVD引导的tokenizer优化和更可控的LLM设计；证明了该方法可在无推理条件下检测异常token，为模型安全审计提供了高效工具。

Abstract: We show that singular value decomposition of the lm_head} weight matrix of a transformer-based large language model -- requiring only five lines of PyTorch and no model inference -- reveals interpretable semantic subspaces directly from the model weights. Each left singular vector identifies the vocabulary tokens most readily selected when the hidden state aligns with the corresponding singular direction; inspecting these clusters exposes the model's training data composition and curation philosophy.
  Analysing GPT-OSS-120B, Gemma-2-2B, and Qwen2.5-1.5B, we find that singular value spectra and vocabulary cluster structures differ systematically across models: GPT exhibits a graduated hierarchy of functionally differentiated subspaces; Gemma is dominated by pre-nineteenth-century English orthography, forming a stepwise clustering structure that may contribute to high output controllability; and Qwen exhibits broad multilingual coverage alongside subspaces whose vocabulary the authors have determined to be ethically inappropriate for direct publication.
  Base-instruct comparison reveals that ethically concerning subspaces originate in pretraining and are not removed by post-training alignment. We introduce the Vocabulary Cluster Score (VCS) to quantify subspace coherence, and the Weighted Projection Score (WPS) as a static glitch token detector; applying WPS to GPT-OSS-120B recovers shokubutsu-hyakka-tsu (ID 137606), a well-known glitch token widely reported in the CJK language community, without any model inference. We propose a taxonomy of root causes for problematic vocabulary content and call for lm_head} SVD analysis to be adopted as a standard pre-release safety auditing step. Our findings further suggest directions toward SVD-guided tokenizer optimisation and more controllable LLM design.

</details>


### [141] [LABO: LLM-Accelerated Bayesian Optimization through Broad Exploration and Selective Experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22054)
*Zhuo Chen,Xinzhe Yuan,Jianshu Zhang,Jinzong Dong,Ruichen Zhou,Yingchun Niu,Tianhang Zhou,Yu Yang Fredrik Liu,Yuqiang Li,Nanyang Ye,Qinying Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LABO addresses inefficient LLM integration in Bayesian optimization by using a gating mechanism to strategically allocate cheap LLM evaluations for exploration and expensive real experiments for high-uncertainty regions, achieving superior sample efficiency with both theoretical and empirical validation.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing LLM-augmented Bayesian optimization approaches embed LLMs directly into sampling or surrogate modeling pipelines without fully exploiting the significantly lower evaluation cost of LLMs compared to real-world experiments, leading to inefficiency.

Method: LABO employs a gating criterion to dynamically balance reliance on LLM predictions versus actual experiments—using inexpensive LLM evaluations for broad search space exploration and reserving costly real experiments only for high-uncertainty regions. A cumulative regret bound is provided for theoretical grounding.

Result: LABO achieves more sample-efficient optimization by leveraging the complementary strengths of LLM predictions (low cost, broad exploration) and actual experiments (high accuracy, targeted uncertainty reduction). Empirical results across diverse scientific tasks demonstrate consistent superiority over existing methods under identical experimental budgets.

Conclusion: LABO provides a practical and theoretically grounded approach for integrating LLMs into scientific discovery workflows, consistently outperforming existing methods under identical experimental budgets.

Abstract: The high cost and data scarcity in scientific exploration have motivated the use of large language models (LLMs) as knowledge-driven components in Bayesian optimization (BO). However, existing approaches typically embed LLMs directly into the sampling or surrogate modeling pipeline, without fully leveraging their significantly lower evaluation cost compared to real-world experiments. To address this limitation, we propose LLM-Accelerated Bayesian Optimization (LABO), a framework that combines LLM predictions with experimental observations within a single BO loop. LABO employs a gating criterion to dynamically balance the reliance on LLM predictions versus actual experiments. By leveraging inexpensive LLM evaluations to broadly explore the search space and reserving costly real experiments only for regions with high uncertainty, LABO achieves more sample-efficient optimization. We provide a theoretical analysis with a cumulative regret bound that formalizes this efficiency gain. Empirical results across diverse scientific tasks demonstrate that LABO consistently outperforms existing methods under identical experimental budgets. Our results suggest that LABO offers a practical and theoretically grounded approach for integrating LLMs into scientific discovery workflows.

</details>


### [142] [Prototype-Guided Classification Sub-Task Decoupling Framework: Enhancing Generalization and Interpretability for Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22055)
*Xianhao Song,Yuang Zhang,Yuqi She,Liping Wang,Xuemin Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PDFTime proposes a prototype-guided multi-stage decision framework for time series classification, replacing the traditional direct feature-to-label mapping with decoupled similarity-based reasoning. The method achieves SOTA results on major TSC benchmarks, demonstrating that breaking the black-box mapping paradigm leads to better accuracy and interpretability.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing TSC methods adopt direct feature-to-label classification paradigm (linear projection after global pooling), which conflates feature extraction and decision logic into an inseparable mapping. This limits both accuracy and interpretability of the models.

Method: PDFTime leverages learned prototypes to approximate class-conditional feature distributions in latent space, enabling progressive discrimination through classification sub-tasks of varying granularity. This reformulates TSC as a decoupled, multi-stage similarity-based reasoning process.

Result: PDFTime achieves state-of-the-art performance across UEA and UCR benchmarks, securing top-1 accuracy on 80 out of 128 datasets in the UCR archive, significantly outperforming recent strong baselines in both consistency and generalization.

Conclusion: PDFTime establishes a new paradigm for time series classification by demonstrating that decoupled, multi-stage similarity-based reasoning outperforms traditional direct feature-to-label mapping. The framework achieves top-1 accuracy on 62.5% (80/128) of UCR datasets, demonstrating superior consistency and generalization capabilities.

Abstract: Time Series Classification (TSC) is a long-standing research problem that has gained increasing attention in recent years with the rapid growth of large-scale temporal data. Despite substantial progress enabled by deep learning, designing TSC models that are both accurate and interpretable remains a challenging task. Many existing approaches adopt a direct feature-to-label classification paradigm, by collapsing high-dimensional temporal embeddings into class logits via a single linear projection (often after global pooling), the paradigm conflates feature extraction and decision logic into an inseparable mapping.
  To address these limitations, we propose PDFTime, a prototype-guided framework that reformulates time series classification as a multi-stage decision process. Instead of direct feature-to-label mapping, PDFTime leverages learned prototypes to approximate class-conditional feature distributions in the latent space, enabling progressive discrimination through classification sub-tasks of varying granularity. To our knowledge, PDFTime is the first framework to reformulate time series classification as a decoupled, multi-stage similarity-based reasoning process, breaking the long-standing paradigm of direct, black-box feature-to-label mapping. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that PDFTime achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance across UEA and UCR benchmarks. Notably, it secures the top-$1$ accuracy on 80 out of 128 datasets in the UCR archive, significantly outperforming recent strong baselines in both consistency and generalization.

</details>


### [143] [From Reasoning Chains to Verifiable Subproblems: Curriculum Reinforcement Learning Enables Credit Assignment for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22074)
*Xitai Jiang,Zihan Tang,Wenze Lin,Yang Yue,Shenzhi Wang,Gao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCRL通过子问题课程和细粒度信用分配解决RLVR在难题上效率低下的问题，将部分进展转化为可验证学习信号，在7个数学推理基准上实现显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于结果的RLVR在难题上效率低下，原因在于：(1) 正确答案的rollout稀少；(2) 样本级信用分配无法利用失败尝试中的部分进展；(3) 难题处于梯度死区难以学习。

Method: SCRL的核心创新包括：(1) 子问题课程构建：从参考答案链中提取可验证的子问题，以原始问题作为最终子问题；(2) 子问题级归一化：对每个子问题位置独立归一化奖励，将归一化后的优势分配给对应的答案片段，实现细粒度的信用分配；(3) 将部分进展转化为可验证的学习信号，无需外部评分标准或奖励模型。

Result: 在7个数学推理基准上，SCRL优于强基线方法：Qwen3-4B-Base上平均准确率提升+4.1分，Qwen3-14B-Base上提升+1.9分；在AIME24、AIME25和IMO-Bench上，Qwen3-4B-Base的pass@1提升+3.7分，pass@64提升+4.6分，表明在难题上探索能力更强；难度越高，相对收益越大。

Conclusion: SCRL通过子问题课程学习将难题从梯度死区中解放出来，在7个数学推理基准上显著优于基线方法，特别是在AIME和IMO等难题上表现突出。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) has shown strong promise for LLM reasoning, but outcome-based RLVR remains inefficient on hard problems because correct final-answer rollouts are rare and sample-level credit assignment cannot use partial progress in failed attempts. We introduce SCRL (Subproblem Curriculum Reinforcement Learning), a curriculum RL framework that derives verifiable subproblems from reference reasoning chains and fixes the final subproblem as the original problem. This turns partial progress on hard problems into verifiable learning signals. Algorithmically, SCRL uses subproblem-level normalization, which normalizes rewards independently at each subproblem position and assigns the resulting advantages to the corresponding answer spans, enabling finer-grained credit assignment without external rubrics or reward models. Our analysis shows that subproblem curricula lift hard problems out of gradient dead zones, with larger relative gains as the original problem becomes harder. Across seven mathematical reasoning benchmarks, SCRL outperforms strong curriculum-learning baselines, improving average accuracy over GRPO by +4.1 points on Qwen3-4B-Base and +1.9 points on Qwen3-14B-Base. On AIME24, AIME25, and IMO-Bench, SCRL further improves pass@1 by +3.7 points and pass@64 by +4.6 points on Qwen3-4B-Base, indicating better exploration on hard reasoning problems.

</details>


### [144] [Can Breath Biomarkers Causally Influence Blood Glucose? Investigating VOC-Mediated Modulation in Diabetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22075)
*Varsha Sharma,Prasanta K. Guha,Avik Ghose*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种基于挥发性有机化合物和生活方式变量的非侵入性、数据驱动框架，用于识别糖尿病风险个体。通过因果推断估计VOCs对血糖的影响，并结合机器学习分类器和高斯混合模型聚类分析，实现对高风险人群的分类和分层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 糖尿病是全球健康负担，早期检测对及时干预至关重要，迫切需要非侵入性的糖尿病风险识别工具。

Method: 使用因果推断技术估计丙酮、异丙醇、异戊二烯和乙醇等挥发性有机化合物对血糖水平的影响；设计分类器区分糖尿病和非糖尿病患者；采用高斯混合模型识别人群自然聚类；建立"灰色地带"人群的风险排序系统。

Result: 特定挥发性有机化合物对血糖水平表现出强烈因果影响，机器学习模型可可靠地区分和分层高风险个体。

Conclusion: 本研究开发的因果可解释分析框架在糖尿病非侵入性早期筛查中具有应用潜力，特定挥发性有机化合物对血糖水平具有显著因果影响，机器学习模型可可靠地分类和分层高风险人群。

Abstract: Diabetes is a global health burden, and early detection is critical for timely intervention. This study explores a non-invasive, data-driven framework to identify individuals at risk of diabetes using Volatile Organic Compounds (VOCs) and lifestyle variables. We use causal inference techniques to estimate the impact of VOCs such as acetone, isopropanol, isoprene, and ethanol on blood glucose levels. Additionally, we designed a classifier to distinguish diabetics from non-diabetics using non-invasive markers. We created a risk-based ranking system for individuals in the "gray zone," and identified natural clusters in the population using Gaussian Mixture Model. Our results suggest that specific VOCs exhibit a strong causal influence on glucose levels and that machine learning models can reliably classify and stratify individuals at high risk. This integrated causal-explainable analysis can support the development of tool for non-invasive early screening of diabetes.

</details>


### [145] [Aerodynamic force reconstruction using physics-informed Gaussian processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22111)
*Gledson Rodrigo Tondo,Igor Kavrakov,Guido Morgenthal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: A probabilistic physics-informed ML method for reconstructing aerodynamic loads from noisy structural responses, demonstrated on a bridge case study, achieving good accuracy without overfitting or regularization.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional aerodynamic load models rely on simplifications that introduce accuracy-limiting assumptions, and their validation is challenging with noisy/incomplete data.

Method: Probabilistic physics-informed machine learning approach that leverages noisy structural dynamic response measurements to reconstruct underlying aerodynamic loads, incorporating physical constraints to avoid overfitting without requiring regularization.

Result: Strong agreement between predicted and true loads demonstrated via RMSE, magnitude, phase angle, and peak value metrics on the Great Belt East Bridge (simulated under linear unsteady assumption).

Conclusion: A physics-informed ML framework that eliminates overfitting and regularization needs, enabling heterogeneous multi-fidelity data fusion for accurate aerodynamic load reconstruction on the Great Belt East Bridge.

Abstract: Accurate modeling of aerodynamic loads is essential for understanding and predicting the responses of complex structural systems. However, these models often rely on simplifications of the true physical forces, introducing assumptions that can limit their accuracy. Validating such models becomes particularly challenging in the presence of noisy or incomplete data. To address this, we introduce a probabilistic physics-informed machine learning approach designed to reconstruct the underlying aerodynamic loads from noisy measurements of structural dynamic responses. The model avoids overfitting, eliminates the need for regularization schemes, and allows for the use of heterogeneous and multi-fidelity data during the training process. The efficacy of the approach is demonstrated through the reconstruction of aerodynamic loads on the Great Belt East Bridge, simulated under a linear unsteady assumption. Results show a strong agreement between true and predicted loads, particularly related to root mean squared errors, magnitude, phase angle and peak values of the signals. The method for load reconstructing holds broad applicability, such as modeling validation, future load estimation, and structural damage prognosis.

</details>


### [146] [Short-Term-to-Long-Term Memory Transfer for Knowledge Graphs under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22142)
*Taewoon Kim,Vincent François-Lavet,Michael Cochez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究将知识图谱记忆中的短期到长期信息转移建模为神经符号化基于价值的决策问题，使用per-item Q-learning设计。在RoomKG基准测试中，学习到的转移策略优于符号和神经基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Reinforcement learning under partial observability requires explicit modeling of short-term-to-long-term transfer of symbolic observations in memory-based approaches, which most existing methods do not address.

Method: Per-item Q-learning design with shared parameters and temporal-difference updates over matched items across consecutive steps, casting the knowledge-graph memory transfer as a neuro-symbolic value-based decision problem.

Result: On RoomKG benchmark at memory capacity 128, learned transfer decisions outperform symbolic baselines (with temporal annotations) and history-based LSTM/Transformer baselines.

Conclusion: A lightweight local short-term-only transfer policy performs best, keeping navigation- and query-relevant facts while discarding lower-value candidate facts, enabling explicit and interpretable memory decisions under constraints.

Abstract: Reinforcement learning under partial observability requires deciding what information to retain, yet most memory-based approaches do not explicitly model short-term-to-long-term transfer of symbolic observations. We study this transfer process in a temporal knowledge-graph memory setting and cast it as a neuro-symbolic value-based decision problem: for each observed triple, the agent chooses whether to keep or drop it before long-term insertion. To handle variable-sized short-term buffers, we use a per-item Q-learning design with shared parameters and a practical temporal-difference update over matched items across consecutive steps. On the RoomKG benchmark at long-term memory capacity 128, learned transfer decisions outperform symbolic and neural baselines, including symbolic baselines with temporal annotations and history-based LSTM/Transformer baselines. Across transfer-policy ablations, a lightweight local short-term-only variant performs best, and step-level behavior shows that the policy keeps navigation- and query-relevant facts while discarding lower-value candidate facts, supporting explicit and interpretable memory decisions under memory constraints.

</details>


### [147] [Algebraic Machine Learning for Small-to-Medium Datasets Is Competitive against Strong Standard Baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22155)
*David Mendez,Fernando Martin-Maroto,Gonzalo G. de Polavieja*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了基于代数结构子直接分解的AML框架，发现其在50-2000训练样本的小规模图像数据集上优于交叉验证的CNN等基线方法，在表格数据上与XGBoost、LightGBM等方法相当。AML使用通用代数归纳偏置而非模态特定偏置，且无需交叉验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号方法通常被认为在现实监督学习任务中无法与强大的现代学习器竞争。

Method: AML通过代数结构的子直接分解（subdirect decomposition）进行学习，而非数值优化。研究者在图像和表格分类任务上针对不同训练集规模评估AML，并与标准基线方法进行比较。

Result: 在小规模图像数据集（50-2000训练样本）上，仅使用训练数据的AML优于包括CNN在内的一系列交叉验证基线方法；在同等规模表格数据上，XGBoost整体表现最佳，但AML与LightGBM和随机森林等方法相当。

Conclusion: AML通过通用的代数归纳偏置而非特定模态的归纳偏置实现竞争性性能，且由于没有任务依赖的超参数需要调优，因此无需交叉验证。

Abstract: Symbolic methods are generally not considered competitive with strong modern learners on realistic supervised tasks. We evaluate Algebraic Machine Learning (AML), a framework that learns through subdirect decomposition of algebraic structure rather than numerical optimization, against standard baselines on image and tabular classification across varying training-set sizes. We find that AML trained only on training data without using validation or cross-validation outperforms a family of cross-validated baseline methods including CNNs on small to medium image datasets (50--2000 training examples). On tabular datasets in the same size range, XGBoost is overall the best performing method, but AML is nonetheless comparable to methods incorporating task-specific biases such as LightGBM and random forests. AML achieves this competitive performance across two very different types of datasets using a generic algebraic inductive bias, rather than the modality-specific biases built into standard baselines like CNNs for images or XGBoost for tabular data, and requires no cross validation because it has no task-dependent hyperparameters to tune.

</details>


### [148] [One-Way Policy Optimization for Self-Evolving LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22156)
*Shuo Yang,Jinda Lu,Kexin Huang,Chiyu Ma,Shaohang Wei,Yuyang Liu,Guoyin Wang,Jingren Zhou,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OWPO方法，通过将优化方向与更新幅度解耦来解决RLVR中稀疏二元验证器奖励导致的训练效率低下和优化不稳定问题。该方法通过验证器决定更新方向、参考策略仅调整更新幅度的设计，采用非对称重加权机制（加速对齐劣质偏差、增益锁定优质偏差），并结合迭代参考更新产生"棘轮效应"。实验结果表明OWPO显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法对偏差的惩罚不加区分，当策略尝试超越参考策略时可能偏离验证器确定的方向，从而抑制性能提升。

Method: 提出非对称重加权机制：对劣质偏差（策略落后于参考）执行"加速对齐"，对优质偏差（策略超越参考）执行"增益锁定"；结合迭代参考更新产生"棘轮效应"持续巩固收益。

Result: OWPO在实验中超越DAPO、OPD、MOPD等强基线，无需外部参考模型即可实现持续自我进化。

Conclusion: OWPO通过非对称重加权机制和迭代参考更新策略，在无需外部参考模型的情况下实现了持续自我进化，在多个强基线上取得了性能突破。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become a promising paradigm for scaling reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, the sparsity of binary verifier rewards often leads to low efficiency and optimization instability. To stabilize training, existing methods typically impose token-level constraints relative to a reference policy. We identify that such constraints penalize deviations indiscriminately; this can flip verifier-determined direction when the policy attempts to outperform the reference, thereby suppressing gains. To resolve this, we propose One-Way Policy Optimization (OWPO), a method based on the principle of decoupling optimization direction from update magnitude. In OWPO, the verifier dictates the update direction, while the reference policy serves only to adjust the magnitude. Specifically, OWPO applies asymmetric reweighting: it performs Accelerated Alignment for inferior deviations (where the policy lags behind the reference) and Gain Locking for superior deviations (where the policy surpasses the reference). Furthermore, by incorporating iterative reference updates, OWPO creates a ``Ratchet Effect'' that continuously consolidates gains. Experimental results demonstrate that OWPO outperforms strong baselines, including DAPO, OPD, and MOPD, breaking the bottleneck of fixed priors to enable continuous self-evolution without reliance on external reference models.

</details>


### [149] [Maestro: Reinforcement Learning to Orchestrate Hierarchical Model-Skill Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22177)
*Jinyang Wu,Guocheng Zhai,Ruihan Jin,Yuhao Shen,Zhengxi Lu,Fan Zhang,Haoran Luo,Zheng Lian,Zhengqi Wen,Jianhua Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Maestro是一个基于强化学习的多模态智能体编排框架，通过轻量级策略动态组合冻结的专家模型和技能库，在10个多模态基准上以4B编排器超越GPT-5和Gemini-2.5-Pro，且具有良好的泛化性和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前框架依赖单一大型语言模型和固定逻辑，无法利用不同模型在不同领域的互补优势，导致性能受限。

Method: 将异构多模态任务重新定义为层级模型-技能注册表上的序列决策过程；训练轻量级策略策略来动态组合冻结的专家模型和两级技能库，在每一步决定是否调用外部专家、选择哪个模型-技能对以及何时终止；采用基于结果的强化学习进行优化，无需步骤级监督信号。

Result: 使用仅4B编排器达到70.1%平均准确率，超越GPT-5（69.3%）和Gemini-2.5-Pro（68.7%）；学习到的协调策略具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: Maestro通过4B规模的编排器实现了70.1%的平均准确率，超越GPT-5和Gemini-2.5-Pro；且无需重新训练即可泛化到新的专家模型和技能，并在保持低延迟高计算效率的同时，扩展注册表后能在挑战性基准上达到59.5%，优于所有闭源基线。

Abstract: The proliferation of large language models (LLMs) and modular skills has endowed autonomous agents with increasingly powerful capabilities. Existing frameworks typically rely on monolithic LLMs and fixed logic to interface with these skills. This gives rise to a critical bottleneck: different LLMs offer distinct advantages across diverse domains, yet current frameworks fail to exploit the complementary strengths of models and skills, thereby limiting their performance on downstream tasks. In this paper, we present Maestro (Multimodal Agent for Expert-Skill Targeted Reinforced Orchestration), a Reinforcement Learning (RL)-driven orchestration framework that reframes heterogeneous multimodal tasks as a sequential decision-making process over a hierarchical model-skill registry. Rather than consolidating all knowledge into a single model, Maestro trains a lightweight policy to dynamically compose ensembles of frozen expert models and a two-tier skill library, deciding at each step whether to invoke an external expert, which model-skill pair to select, and when to terminate. The policy is optimized via outcome-based RL, requiring no step-level supervision. We evaluate Maestro across ten representative multimodal benchmarks spanning mathematical reasoning, chart understanding, high-resolution perception, and domain-specific analysis. With only a 4B orchestrator, Maestro achieves an average accuracy of 70.1%, surpassing both GPT-5 (69.3%) and Gemini-2.5-Pro (68.7%). Crucially, the learned coordination policy generalizes to unseen models and skills without retraining: augmenting the registry with out-of-domain experts yields a 59.5% average on four challenging benchmarks, outperforming all closed-source baselines. Maestro further maintains high computational efficiency with low latency. The source code is available at https://github.com/jinyangwu/Maestro.

</details>


### [150] [Beyond Euclidean Proximity: Repairing Latent World Models with Horizon-Matched Trajectory Reachability Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22164)
*Liangyu Li,Shengzhi Wang,Qingwen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了轨迹可达性指标（TRM），用于解决潜在世界模型中终端成本接口的决策相关信息暴露问题。TRM通过从轨迹结构中学习成对排序头来改进规划器看到的终端候选排序，而非修改底层模型。在TwoRoom中从7%提升至97%，在PLDM上从32.7%提升至84.0%，并通过机制分析解释了为什么XY坐标虽然只占不到1%的潜在MSE，却携带了大部分候选质量信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前潜在MPC通过预测终端与目标潜在状态之间的欧几里得距离对候选序列进行排名，但这假设原始潜在距离正确地权衡了与可达性相关的变量。然而，在复杂环境中，原始潜在距离可能无法正确捕捉与任务相关的信息，导致规划器做出错误决策。

Method: 提出轨迹可达性指标（TRM），一种用于固定潜在世界模型的事后终端排序方法。TRM从记录的运动轨迹结构中训练一个小型的成对头部，作为替代或混合成本函数使用，而保持编码器、动态模型、采样器、优化器和评估清单不变。关键设计选择是 horizon-aware 监督：指标在广泛的、平衡的时间分离上训练，以匹配长-horizon终端候选排序问题。

Result: 在TwoRoom基准上，使用LeWorldModel（LeWM）的原始潜在规划达到7.0%成功率，而完整horizon TRM达到97.0%；打乱时间标签的对照实验保持在0.0%。相同方法将PLDM基线从32.7%提高到84.0%（三个种子）。使用100,000配对预算的短horizon TRM变体仅达到35.0%。在PushT上，TRM风格的任务状态指标改善了SCSA排序和选定最终距离。XY位置可线性解码（R²=0.998），但原始潜在MSE错误排序候选；XY探针行空间占终端目标潜在MSE不到1%但携带大部分候选质量信号。

Conclusion: TRM作为规划器端修复方法，能够有效替代或增强原始潜在距离。在TwoRoom中，XY坐标探针行空间仅占终端目标潜在MSE的不到1%，却携带了大部分候选质量信号；SCSA审计显示TRM改进了规划器看到的排序和选定端点。在PushT上，TRM风格的任务状态指标比闭环成功率更干净地改善了SCSA排序和选定最终距离，这为连续操作中的辅助混合成本提供了依据。关键发现是，当终端可达性指标应该替换或增强原始潜在接近度时，TRM和审计可以解释何时以及为何这样做。

Abstract: Latent world models can contain the state needed for control, yet their terminal-cost interface can expose the planner to the wrong decision-relevant information. In common latent MPC, candidate sequences are ranked by Euclidean distance between predicted terminal and goal latent states; this assumes that raw latent distance weights reachability-relevant variables correctly. We propose trajectory reachability metrics (TRM), a post-hoc terminal-ranking method for fixed latent world models. TRM trains a small pairwise head from logged trajectory structure and uses it as a replacement or hybrid cost; the encoder, dynamics, sampler, optimizer, and evaluation manifests remain fixed. The key design choice is horizon-aware supervision: the metric is trained on broad, balanced temporal separations to match the long-horizon terminal candidate ranking problem. On a hard TwoRoom benchmark, raw latent planning with LeWorldModel (LeWM) reaches 7.0% success, while full-horizon TRM reaches 97.0%; shuffled temporal-label controls stay at 0.0%. The same recipe improves a PLDM baseline from 32.7% to 84.0% across three seeds, and a short-horizon TRM variant reaches only 35.0% with the 100,000 pair budget. In TwoRoom, we provide mechanistic evidence for why TRM works: XY position is linearly decodable (R^2=0.998), yet raw latent MSE misranks candidates; the XY-probe rowspace accounts for less than 1% of terminal-goal latent MSE but carries most candidate-quality signal; and SCSA audits show that TRM improves the ordering and selected endpoint seen by the planner. On PushT go50/go75, TRM-style task-state metrics improve SCSA ranking and selected final distance more cleanly than closed-loop success, motivating auxiliary hybrid costs in continuous manipulation. TRM is the planner-facing repair, and audits explain when terminal reachability metrics should replace or augment raw latent proximity.

</details>


### [151] [Survive or Collapse: The Asymmetric Roles of Data Gating and Reward Grounding in Self-Play RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22217)
*Sophia Xiao Pu,Zhaotian Weng,Chengzhi Liu,Jayanth Srinivasa,Gaowen Liu,William Yang Wang,Xin Eric Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 自我博弈强化学习的稳定性主要由数据级门控决定，而非奖励设计。严格门控是稳定性的充分必要条件，且存在"接地提议者悖论"——接地提议者反而加速崩溃。通过连续严格性参数可观察到两阶段相变现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我博弈强化学习虽能显著提升推理能力，但广泛存在训练崩溃和不稳定问题，现有研究主要从奖励设计角度应对，而崩溃的根本机制尚不清晰。

Method: 通过受控实验在Python输出预测任务和确定性DSL孪生任务上进行测试，后者可消除预训练先验、输出歧义和执行器噪声的干扰。

Result: 发现数据级门控与奖励信号具有不对称性：严格门控在所有奖励变体（包括无真值访问的自洽奖励）下均能维持训练稳定，而移除门控后任何奖励变体都无法保证稳定。揭示了"接地提议者悖论"：接地提议者（具真值访问权）在与自洽求解器配对时反而加速崩溃，因其将训练集中在清洁任务上，形成通往虚假自洽吸引子的最快路径。此外，引入连续严格性参数ε揭示两阶段相变：低ε下训练指标先解耦，而验证准确率在ε更高时才下降。

Conclusion: 数据级门控（而非奖励校准）是决定自博弈稳定性的关键约束条件。

Abstract: Self-play reinforcement learning trains language models on their own generated tasks, co-evolving a proposer and solver without human labels. Recent systems report strong reasoning gains, but collapse and instability are widely observed and poorly understood. The dominant response treats this as a reward-design problem. We argue instead that self-play stability is governed by two distinct levers: a data-level gate that decides which proposer-generated tasks enter the training pool, and the reward signal that updates the policy on tasks already admitted. Through controlled experiments on a Python output-prediction task and a deterministic-DSL twin task that strips pretraining priors, output ambiguity, and executor noise, we find the two levers are asymmetric. A strict gate is sufficient for stability under every reward variant we test, including a self-consistency reward with no access to ground truth; while no reward variant is sufficient once the gate is removed. This asymmetry exposes a counter-intuitive coupling we call the Grounded Proposer Paradox: a proposer with ground-truth access accelerates collapse faster than an ungrounded one when paired with a self-consistency solver, by concentrating training on clean tasks that form the fastest path to a spurious self-consistent attractor. Replacing the binary gate with a continuous strictness parameter $\varepsilon$ further reveals a two-stage phase transition: training-side metrics decouple at low $\varepsilon$, while validation accuracy holds until $\varepsilon$ is much higher. Data-level gating, not reward calibration, is the binding constraint on self-play stability.

</details>


### [152] [Boundary-targeted Membership Inference Attacks on Safety Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22373)
*Anthony Hughes,Alexander Goldberg,Prince Jha,Adam Perer,Nikolaos Aletras,Niloofar Mireshghallah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了对安全分类器进行成员推断攻击的新方法，通过识别训练数据中模型低置信度样本，攻击者能以3.5倍效率推断敏感训练数据成员身份，并证明差分隐私等噪声策略可有效防御此类攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全分类器虽必要，但其训练数据包含敏感内容（自残、心理健康讨论），存在隐私隐患。现有成员推断攻击方法效果有限，需探索新的攻击向量。

Method: 提出基于边界-targeting的样本选择策略，识别低置信度样本以放大训练集成员推断信号；对比实验包括最新MIA基线方法。

Result: 攻击者可在5%假阳性率下恢复19%的情感支持检测分类器训练对话，较最新MIA方法提升3.5倍；基于内容的过滤防护无效，但噪声策略可有效防御。

Conclusion: 研究证明边界样本（低置信度样本）的过滤可有效防御MIA攻击，同时现有噪声策略（如差分隐私）能缓解此类攻击的敏感性。

Abstract: Safety classifiers are essential safeguards within generative AI systems, filtering harmful content or identifying at-risk users when interacting with large language models. Despite their necessity, these models are trained on sensitive datasets including discussions of self-harm and mental health, raising important, yet poorly understood, privacy concerns. Membership inference attacks (MIAs) allow adversaries to infer membership of examples used to train models. In this work, we hypothesize that identifying the examples on which the classifier is least confident are informative for an adversary to infer membership. This reflects a localized failure of generalization, where the model relies on memorization to resolve ambiguity in the training set. To investigate this, we introduce a new boundary-targeted selection strategy that identifies low confidence examples that amplify the signal of an examples membership within a training set. Our experimental results show that an adversary can recover 19\% of the conversations a safety classifier flagged as indicating user distress, at a 5\% false-positive rate, on a classifier fine-tuned for detecting a user who may require emotional support. This is $3.5$ times more than attacking using state-of-the-art MIA methods alone. Finally, we characterize the boundary laying examples and show that content-based filtering is ineffective for protection, and existing noise strategies can effectively mitigate susceptibility of these examples.

</details>


### [153] [IKNO: Infinite-order Kernel Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22182)
*Pengyuan Zhu,Ivor W. Tsang,Yueming Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IKNO（无限阶核神经算子）通过引入无限阶核积分来克服现有神经算子表达能力受限的问题。该方法具有解析的有限近似形式，包含两种变体：IKNO-Vanilla基于Kronecker特征分解实现完整核预解式，IKNO-TP采用张量积结构组合各轴预解式。实验表明该方法在多种基准数据集上达到最优性能，具有良好的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子主要依赖一阶核积分近似，这严重限制了模型的表达能力。

Method: 提出两种互补的无限阶神经算子构造方法：IKNO-Vanilla通过对偶网格上的完整核预解式实现全局信息聚合，IKNO-TP通过组合每轴预解式实现张量积算子。两种变体均通过快速计算方案在保持高计算效率的同时实现出色的全局信息聚合。

Result: 在时间依赖和时间独立的基准测试上，包括大规模工业数据集，IKNO在几乎所有基准数据集上均取得了显著改进，始终保持最先进的精度，同时能够扩展到非常大的点云规模。

Conclusion: IKNO通过无限阶核积分显著提升了神经算子的表达能力，同时通过快速计算方案保持了高计算效率，在多种基准数据集上实现了最先进的性能，并具备处理大规模点云数据的可扩展性。

Abstract: Neural operators have achieved significant success in modern scientific computing due to their flexibility and strong generalization capabilities. Existing models, however, primarily rely on first-order kernel integral approximations, which severely limit their expressivity. To address this, we propose the Infinite-order Kernel Neural Operator (IKNO), which constructs neural operators via infinite-order kernel integrals and admits an elegant closed-form finite approximation. We develop two complementary infinite-order neural operator constructions: IKNO-Vanilla, which applies the full-kernel resolvent on the product grid via Kronecker eigendecomposition, and IKNO-TP, an alternative tensor-product operator that composes per-axis resolvents. Furthermore, we develop fast computation schemes for both variants of IKNO, which achieve outstanding global information aggregation while maintaining high computational efficiency. Empirically, we evaluate our IKNO on both time-dependent and time-independent benchmarks with arbitrary input shapes, including large-scale industrial datasets. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the IKNO method consistently achieves the SOTA accuracy with significant improvements on nearly all benchmark datasets while maintaining scalability to very large point clouds.

</details>


### [154] [From Sequential Nodes to GPU Batches: Parallel Branch and Bound for Optimal $k$-Sparse GLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22188)
*Jiachang Liu,Andrea Lodi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种简单、通用的模块化CPU-GPU框架，用于在GPU上批量处理分支定界节点，解决离散变量优化问题。通过填充和自定义内核处理不规则数据结构，实现显著加速并可扩展至Rashomon集收集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前GPU加速成功未有效转移至离散变量、组合结构和非线性目标的问题，主要挑战包括分支定界中异构节点的顺序处理以及CPU与GPU之间频繁的数据移动

Method: 提出模块化CPU-GPU框架，采用批量处理多个分支定界节点，使用填充和轻量级自定义内核处理不规则节点数据结构

Result: 实现一到两个数量级的加速，且在挑战性实例上达到零最优性间隙

Conclusion: 该框架可扩展用于收集完整的Rashomon集，支持下游统计分析如变量重要性分析和模型选择

Abstract: GPUs have significantly accelerated first-order methods for large-scale optimization, especially in continuous optimization. However, this success has not transferred cleanly to problems with discrete variables, combinatorial structure, and nonlinear objectives, such as certifying optimal solutions for cardinality-constrained generalized linear models. Major challenges include the sequential processing of heterogeneous nodes in branch and bound (BnB) and frequent data movement between the CPU and GPU. We propose a simple, generic, and modular CPU--GPU framework that processes multiple BnB nodes in batches on GPUs. The framework is built around a small set of GPU-efficient routines and uses padding together with lightweight custom kernels to handle irregular node data structures. Experiments show one to two orders of magnitude speedups and zero optimality gap on challenging instances. The framework can also be extended to collect the entire Rashomon set, enabling downstream statistical analysis such as variable-importance analysis and model selection under secondary user-specific measures (e.g., AUC in classification).

</details>


### [155] [Structured-Sparse Attention for Entity Tracking with Subquadratic Sequence Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22476)
*Hangyue Zhao,Paul Caillon,Erwan Fagnou,Alexandre Allauzen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对长序列实体跟踪任务，提出利用注意力权重的局部块对角结构对 resolvent 算子进行块级近似评估，将计算复杂度从二次降至亚二次（$O(n^{4/3}d)$），在保持精度的同时实现 12-29% 的推理加速（最高 2.4 倍），但受注意力头数量约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实体跟踪任务需要维护和更新长序列中的实体与属性潜在状态。现有任务特定的注意力算子虽能将深层 Transformer 压缩为少量层（通过单层内多跳状态传播实现），但其密集评估的计算成本仍然很高。

Method: 利用注意力权重的块对角局部性结构，对 resolvent 算子进行块级分解评估：保持块内交互的精确性，将跨块交互路由至约简系统。从而将评估复杂度降至 $O(n^{4/3}d)$（当 $d \approx n$ 时为 $O(n^{7/3})$）。

Result: 在受控跟踪基准测试中，该方法达到与密集算手相当的精度，墙钟时间减少 12-29%；在相同精确匹配精度下，比紧凑密集 Transformer 快最多 2.4 倍。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持精度的前提下显著降低了计算成本，为长序列实体跟踪任务提供了一种高效的实际解决方案，但存在注意力头数量与可追踪属性数量的匹配约束。

局限：当可同时演化的属性数量超过注意力头数量时，性能会崩溃。

Abstract: Entity tracking requires maintaining and updating latent states for entities and attributes over long sequences. Recent task-specific attention operators can compress deep Transformer stacks into a few layers by performing multi-hop state propagation within a single layer, but their dense evaluation remains expensive. We show that in this setting, learned attention is strongly structured: most mass concentrates in local block-diagonal neighborhoods with a light cross-block residue. Exploiting this, we derive a blockwise evaluation of a resolvent-style operator that keeps within-block interactions exact and routes cross-block interactions through a reduced system. The resulting evaluation is subquadratic in sequence length $O(n^{4/3}d)$ (and $O(n^{7/3})$ when $d\approx n$). On controlled tracking benchmarks, our method matches the dense operator's accuracy while reducing wall-clock time by $12-29\%$ under a standardized measurement protocol, and is up to $2.4 \times$ faster than a compact dense Transformer at comparable exact-match accuracy. We further provide ablations over block size and model capacity, and identify a limitation: performance collapses when the number of simultaneously evolving properties exceeds the number of attention heads.

</details>


### [156] [Bandit Convex Optimization with Gradient Prediction Adaptivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22191)
*Shuche Wang,Adarsh Barik,Vincent Y. F. Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究赌博式凸优化中乐观梯度预测的价值。主要贡献：1）证明单点反馈下存在不可避免的Ω(√T)下界，梯度估计方差从根本上限制预测收益；2）提出TP-VR-OPT算法，通过方差缩减技术使梯度方差与预测误差而非梯度范数成比例；3）在两点反馈下达到O(√(d·E[S_T]))的遗憾上界，并在信息论意义上证明最优至√d因子；4）开发自适应变体并扩展至非平稳环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究背景是赌博式凸优化(BCO)这一基础在线学习框架，仅能观测已选决策点的损失函数值。核心研究问题是：乐观梯度预测是否能以预测自适应的方式改善最坏情况遗憾。当前问题在于，即使有梯度预测mt，现有方法仍难以有效利用预测误差来提升性能。该研究旨在建立 regret bounds that scale with cumulative prediction error S_T。

Method: 1. 信息论分析：建立单点反馈下Ω(√T)的下界，揭示即使预测误差S_T=o(T)也无法克服方差估计的根本限制。2. 算法设计：提出TP-VR-OPT算法，采用新颖的方差缩减梯度估计器，其方差与预测误差而非梯度范数成比例。3. 自适应扩展：开发无需预知E[S_T]或时间范围T的自适应变体。4. 非平稳环境扩展：将框架推广至时变环境，建立同时适应预测误差和比较路径长度的动态遗憾保证。

Result: 核心理论结果包括：1）上界：TP-VR-OPT在两点反馈下实现遗憾上界O(√(d·E[S_T]))；2）下界：建立了信息论最优下界Ω(√E[S_T])，表明该算法在√d因子内是最优的；3）单点反馈不可逾越性：即使S_T=o(T)，仍存在不可避免的Ω(√T)下界；4）动态遗憾：非平稳环境下可同时适应预测误差和比较器路径长度。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了两点反馈下可实现最优至√d因子的预测自适应遗憾上界，并提供了动态遗憾保证。核心贡献在于：1）通过方差缩减梯度估计器将方差与预测误差挂钩；2）建立了信息论下界揭示最优可达到的性能极限；3）开发了无需先验知识的自适应变体；4）将框架推广至非平稳环境。

Abstract: Bandit convex optimization (BCO) is a fundamental online learning framework with partial feedback, where the learner observes only the loss incurred at the chosen decision point in each round. In this work, we investigate whether optimistic gradient predictions can improve worst-case regret guarantees in a prediction-adaptive manner. Specifically, given gradient predictions $m_t$, we seek regret bounds that scale with the cumulative prediction error $S_T=\sum_{t=1}^T \|\nabla f_t(x_t)-m_t\|^2.$ We first establish a negative result: under the single-point feedback protocol, an unavoidable $Ω(\sqrt{T})$ regret lower bound persists even when $S_T=o(T)$, showing that the variance of gradient estimation fundamentally obscures the benefit of accurate predictions. To overcome this barrier, we propose \emph{Two-Point Variance-Reduced Optimistic Gradient Descent} (TP-VR-OPT) for the two-point feedback setting. The key idea is a novel variance-reduced gradient estimator whose variance scales with the prediction error rather than the gradient norm. This yields a regret bound of $O\big(\sqrt{d\,\mathbb{E}[S_T]}\big),$ where $d$ is the decision dimension. Complementing this result, we establish an information-theoretic lower bound that scales as $Ω(\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[S_T]})$, providing a fundamental characterization of the best achievable prediction-adaptive regret and showing that TP-VR-OPT is optimal up to a factor of $\sqrt d$. We further develop adaptive variants that eliminate the need for prior knowledge of $\mathbb{E}[S_T]$ or the horizon $T$, and extend our framework to non-stationary environments, establishing dynamic regret guarantees that adapt simultaneously to the cumulative prediction error and the comparator path length.

</details>
