### [157] [Reinforced Graph of Thoughts: RL-Driven Adaptive Prompting for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22195)
*Manuel Noah Riesen,Peter Alfred von Niederhäusern*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RGoT通过强化学习将Graph of Thoughts范式自动化，使LLM能够自适应生成图操作结构，无需人工手动定义操作图，提升了GoT的灵活性和通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GoT范式需要人工手动定义操作图，要求研究者深入了解问题的解法，导致图结构僵硬且缺乏适应性，难以应对不同复杂度的任务。

Method: 提出RGoT方法，利用强化学习从人类定义的集合中自适应生成图操作。该方法将图结构的构建过程自动化，使LLM能够根据任务复杂度动态调整思考图的结构。

Result: 实验结果表明，在特定约束条件下，可以自动化地为任务复杂度自适应地构建图操作。

Conclusion: RGoT通过RL自动化生成图操作，为GoT范式提供了一种自适应的问题解决框架，在保持可控性的同时提升了适应性。

Abstract: Graph of Thoughts (GoT), a generalized form of recent prompting paradigms for large language models (LLMs), has been shown to be useful for elaborate problem solving. By executing a graph of operations, thoughts of the LLM are structured as an arbitrary graph, forming the actual graph of thoughts. Originally, the graph of operations is defined manually, which requires in-depth knowledge about the solution of the problem to solve. Such a static graph of operations is rigid and therefore lacks adaptability. We propose Reinforced Graph of Thoughts (RGoT), an automated approach to the GoT prompting paradigm that leverages reinforcement learning (RL) to adaptively generate a graph of operations from a human-defined set. Results indicate that, under certain constraints, it is possible to construct graphs of operations adaptively to the task's complexity in an automated way.

</details>


### [158] [A Tutorial on Diffusion Theory: From Differential Equations to Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22586)
*Jiayi Fu,Yuxia Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本教程从微分方程角度系统梳理了扩散模型的理论框架，证明前向过程可用SDE和ODE两种形式描述，逆时间采样对应 reverse SDE 和 reverse probability-flow ODE，核心由边际 score function ∇log p_t(x) 驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提供一个统一的理论框架，从微分方程角度系统性地理解扩散模型的数学本质，建立前向过程与反向采样之间的对应关系。

Method: 从条件高斯前向过程出发，推导出ODE和SDE两种表示；通过对数据分布边缘化得到边际前向方程；通过逆时间动力学推导 reverse SDE 和 reverse probability-flow ODE，揭示 score function 在其中的核心作用。

Result: 证明了标准噪声预测目标等价于 score matching（仅差一个与模型参数无关的常数）；建立了DDPM/DDIM与连续时间 reverse SDE/ODE 采样之间的对应关系；分析了 DPM-Solver 等采样方法及引导采样策略。

Conclusion: 扩散模型可以统一在SDE/ODE框架下理解，DDPM和DDIM分别对应离散 reverse-SDE 和 reverse-ODE 采样，两者在训练目标上本质一致。

Abstract: This tutorial develops diffusion models from the viewpoint of differential equations. We begin with the conditional Gaussian forward process and show that this path admits both an ordinary differential equation (ODE) representation and a stochastic differential equation (SDE) representation. Averaging the conditional process over the data distribution then yields marginalized forward ODE and SDE formulations that transport the data distribution $p_0=p_{\mathrm{data}}$ to a Gaussian prior $p_1=\mathcal{N}(0,I)$. We next derive the corresponding reverse-time dynamics, namely the reverse SDE and the reverse probability-flow ODE, both of which are governed by the marginal score $\grad\log p_t(x)$. This leads to a training objective for score estimation and shows that the standard noise-prediction objective is equivalent to score matching up to an additive constant independent of the model parameters. We then discuss sampling methods for the learned reverse dynamics, including DPM-Solver, as well as guided sampling through classifier guidance and classifier-free guidance. Finally, we compare DDPM and DDIM with the reverse SDE/ODE framework and show that they share the same training objective, while DDPM sampling corresponds to discrete reverse-SDE sampling and DDIM sampling corresponds to reverse-ODE sampling.

</details>


### [159] [Two is better than one: A Collapse-free Multi-Reward RLIF Training Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22620)
*Shourov Joarder,Diganta Sikdar,Ahsan Habib Akash,Binod Bhattarai,Prashnna Gyawali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多奖励内部反馈强化学习框架，通过答案级聚类投票奖励和完成级Token自确定性奖励的组合，以及KL-Cov正则化，在无外部真值监督条件下实现稳定的LLM长程推理能力提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLIF方法依赖单一内部奖励信号，存在奖励黑客、熵崩溃和推理结构退化等问题。

Method: 提出多奖励RLIF框架，包含答案级聚类投票奖励和完成级Token自确定性奖励；采用GDPO归一化平衡奖励尺度差异；引入KL-Cov正则化针对性惩罚低熵Token分布以维持探索性。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成基准测试中，本方法相比先前无监督RL方法提升了稳定性和鲁棒性，性能接近监督式RLVR方法。

Conclusion: 多奖励内部反馈结合KL-Cov正则化可实现稳定的无监督长程推理，性能接近监督式RLVR方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has substantially improved the reasoning ability of LLMs, but often depends on external supervision from human annotations or gold-standard solutions. Reinforcement learning from internal feedback (RLIF) has recently emerged as a scalable unsupervised alternative, using signals extracted from the model itself. However, existing RLIF methods typically rely on a single internal reward, which can lead to reward hacking, entropy collapse, and degraded reasoning structure. We propose a multi-reward RLIF framework that decomposes the training signal into two complementary components: an answer-level reward based on cluster voting and a completion-level reward based on token-wise self-certainty. To combine these signals robustly, we apply GDPO-based normalization to reduce reward-scale imbalance. We further introduce KL-Cov regularization, which targets low-entropy token distributions responsible for disproportionate entropy reduction, preserving exploration and preventing late-stage collapse. Across mathematical reasoning and code-generation benchmarks, our method improves stability and robustness over prior unsupervised RL approaches, while achieving performance close to supervised RLVR methods. These results show that complementary internal rewards, combined with targeted regularization, can support stable long-horizon reasoning without relying on external ground-truth supervision. Code will be released soon.

</details>


### [160] [Can Transformers Learn to Verify During Backtracking Search?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22221)
*Yin Jun Phua,Tony Ribeiro,Tuan Nguyen,Katsumi Inoue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Identified two failure modes in decoder-only transformers trained on cumulative search traces (scattered retrieval and history entanglement) and proposed SSA, a structural attention mask fix that isolates decisions to current state without retraining.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Decoder-only transformers trained on cumulative traces fail the optimal continue-or-backtrack prediction requirement because two trajectories reaching the same state should admit the same viable continuations. The model fails through: (1) scattered retrieval—state features scattered across many positions, and (2) history entanglement—the predictor conditioning on trajectory rather than state.

Method: Localization rewrites decision blocks to expose state features locally, addressing scattered retrieval. Selective State Attention (SSA) uses a fixed attention mask to enforce state-based decisions structurally, without modifying training data, objective, or parameters.

Result: On same-state pairs differing only in prior history, SSA emits identical decisions while cumulative-trained causal baseline does not. Tested on 3-SAT, graph coloring, Blocks World, and backtracking parsing.

Conclusion: SSA enables models to make identical decisions on same-state pairs differing only in history, without requiring retraining. The work opens inference-time context clearing as a candidate approach for isolation without retraining.

Abstract: Backtracking search underlies classical constraint solvers, planners, and theorem provers. Recent transformer-based reasoning systems explore search trees over their own intermediate steps. A common training recipe fits an autoregressive next-token loss on offline solver traces. The model's input at each step is a cumulative trace of all prior decisions. The optimal continue-or-backtrack predictor depends only on the current search state, since two trajectories reaching the same state admit the same viable continuations. We show that decoder-only transformers trained on cumulative traces fail this requirement in two ways: the trace can scatter state features across many positions (scattered retrieval), and the predictor can condition on the trajectory rather than the state (history entanglement). We address scattered retrieval with localization, a trace-level fix that rewrites each decision block to expose state features locally. We address history entanglement with Selective State Attention (SSA), a fixed attention mask that enforces state-based decisions structurally without modifying training data, objective, or parameters. We focus on reactive verification, after propagation has exposed a contradiction. We test SSA on 3-SAT, graph coloring, Blocks World, and backtracking parsing. On same-state pairs that differ only in prior history, SSA emits identical decisions while a cumulative-trained causal baseline does not. Our contribution is a diagnostic of transformer behavior on serialized trajectory data, paired with a structural fix. Pretrained language models that search over their own reasoning steps may face the same failure. Our analysis opens up inference-time context clearing as a candidate way to apply the same isolation without retraining.

</details>


### [161] [The Double Dilemma in Multi-Task Radiology Report Generation: A Gradient Dynamics Analysis and Solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22635)
*Erjian Zhang,Yatong Hao,Liejun Wang,Zhiqing Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对医学影像报告生成多任务学习中线性标量化策略的局限性，从随机微分方程(SDE)梯度动态角度分析其"双重困境"机制，提出CAME-Grad优化器，通过冲突规避方向修正和幅度增强能量注入解决漂移项偏移与扩散项衰减问题，实现理论最优方向与任务归纳偏置的动态平衡，显著提升多任务医学报告生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于架构设计的医学影像报告生成多任务学习方法存在局限，线性标量化策略无法有效平衡判别性临床监督的硬约束与报告生成的光滑性要求。

Method: 提出基于SDE框架分析梯度动态的CAME-Grad优化器，通过冲突规避方向修正和幅度增强能量注入确保几何有效性并避免局部最优，结合自适应梯度融合机制建立理论最优方向与任务特定归纳偏置的动态平衡。

Result: 实验表明CAME-Grad作为通用即插即用优化器，在8种不同RRG方法中带来实质性且一致的改进，在MIMIC-CXR上临床疗效平均提升2.3%，在IU X-Ray上提升1.9%。

Conclusion: CAME-Grad作为通用即插即用优化器，在8种不同的RRG方法中带来实质性且一致的改进，在MIMIC-CXR上平均提升2.3%，在IU X-Ray上平均提升1.9%。

Abstract: While multi-task learning based automatic radiology report generation (RRG) is widely adopted to ensure clinical consistency, most focus on architectural designs yet remain limited to coarse linear scalarization strategies. These strategies cannot effectively balance the hard constraints of discriminative clinical supervision with the smoothness requirements of report generation. To address these problems, we analyze the failure mechanism of linear scalarization from the perspective of gradient dynamics, utilizing the stochastic differential equation (SDE) framework to characterize it as a "Double Dilemma" of drift term deviation and diffusion term decay. Based on this, we propose a backbone-agnostic optimizer named Conflict-Averse Magnitude-Enhanced Gradient Descent (CAME-Grad). Through conflict-averse direction rectification and magnitude-enhanced energy injection, the algorithm not only ensures geometric validity, but also avoids local optimal solutions. Then, the adaptive gradient fusion mechanism is used to establish a dynamic balance between the theoretical optimal direction and the task-specific inductive bias. Experiments show that as a universal plug-and-play optimizer, CAME-Grad brings substantial and consistent improvements across eight diverse RRG methods, elevating overall clinical efficacy performance by an average of 2.3\% on MIMIC-CXR and 1.9\% on IU X-Ray. Our code is available at https://github.com/vpsg-research/CAME-Grad.

</details>


### [162] [ARC-STAR: Auditable Post-Hoc Correction for PDE Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22222)
*Chengze Li,Lingwei Wei,Li Sun,Hongbo Lv,Jie Yang,Hongrong Zhang,Kening Zheng,Wei-Chieh Huang,Enze Ma,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ARC-STAR提出了一种针对PDE基础模型的冻结-宿主后验校正框架，通过全局校正器、局部块级精修器和预算感知路由三阶段，显著提升预训练求解器在陌生流动场景下的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDE foundation models drift on unfamiliar flows with spatially concentrated errors; existing approaches (retraining destabilizes network, uniform correction ignores spatial distribution) are inadequate.

Method: Three-stage correction: (1) global corrector removes broad solver bias, (2) blockwise local refiner cleans post-global residual, (3) label-free scoring routes deployment compute to high-risk regions under budget constraints.

Result: Across five flow benchmarks (ten regime cells): global stage reduces raw host error by 91-99%; local stage further reduces post-global residual by up to 94.4%; only method achieving ≥36x velocity rollout error reduction over raw Poseidon on every cell.

Conclusion: ARC-STAR is the only method achieving at least 36x error reduction over raw Poseidon across all ten regime cells; framework is frozen-host (no fine-tuning), auditable (separately trained stages), and budget-aware (blockwise compute routing).

Abstract: Partial differential equation (PDE) foundation models are pretrained networks that forecast how physical fields like velocity and pressure evolve from a single reusable solver. On unfamiliar flows their predictions drift step by step, errors concentrate in a few regions, yet retraining destabilizes the network and uniform post-hoc correction overlooks this spatial concentration. To address this, we propose a frozen-solver post-hoc correction framework, Adaptive Risk-Calibrated Spatial Triage for Auditable Refinement (ARC-STAR). ARC-STAR organizes correction into three stages: a global corrector removes broad solver bias, a blockwise local refiner cleans the post-global residual, and, at deployment, a label-free score routes refinement to high-risk blocks under a compute budget. The framework is designed to be (i) frozen-host, preserving the pretrained solver without fine-tuning; (ii) auditable, with global and local stages trained and evaluated separately for measurable contributions; and (iii) budget-aware, using a blockwise interface that either refines the full field or routes limited compute to high-risk regions. Across five flow benchmarks spanning ten regime cells, ARC-STAR is the only method that cuts velocity rollout error by at least 36x over raw Poseidon on every cell. The global stage reduces raw host error by 91-99%, and the local stage further reduces the remaining post-global residual by up to 94.4%. Our code implementation is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/arc_star.

</details>


### [163] [How Many Different Outputs Can a Transformer Generate?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22223)
*Maxime Meyer,Mario Michelessa,Caroline Chaux,Vincent Y. F. Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究transformer架构特征与序列输出容量之间的关系，证明了可访问序列长度随prompt长度线性增长、超临界阈值后指数衰减等理论性质，并提供了经验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何仅利用transformer架构的少量特征来精确预测其可输出的不同序列数量，包括定性和定量两个维度。

Method: 通过形式化证明提供理论分析框架，核心包括：(i) 可访问序列最大长度随prompt长度线性增长；(ii) 超过临界阈值后，可访问序列比例呈指数衰减；(iii) prompt长度与可访问序列长度的线性系数存在理论上限。实证验证采用跨架构、跨模型规模的对比实验。

Result: 理论方面：可访问序列最大长度与prompt长度呈线性关系；超临界阈值后序列比例指数衰减；线性系数存在理论上界。实证方面：所提上界在跨架构、跨模型规模实验中紧致（误差因子小于10）。

Conclusion: 即使在无界上下文和计算时间条件下，这些结果依然成立。该理论框架统一解释了transformer在复制、填充等简单序列任务上观察到的实证失败现象。

Abstract: We study how we can leverage only a handful of characteristics of a transformer's architecture to closely predict the number of different sequences it can output, both qualitatively and quantitatively. We provide an upper bound depending on the length of the prompt, which we show empirically to be tight up to a factor less than 10, across architectures and model sizes. Our analysis also provides a theoretical explanation for previously observed empirical failures of transformers on simple sequence tasks, such as copying and cramming. Formally, we prove that (i) the maximal length of accessible sequences (those that the transformer can output for some prompt) grows linearly with the prompt length, (ii) beyond a critical threshold, the proportion of accessible sequences decays exponentially with sequence length, and (iii) the linear coefficient relating prompt length to accessible sequence length admits a theoretical upper bound. Notably, these results hold even with unbounded context and computation time.

</details>


### [164] [Holomorphic Neural ODEs with Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks for Interpretable Discovery of Complex Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22235)
*Bhaskar Ranjan Karn,Dinesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Holomorphic KAN-ODE框架，用KAN替代MLP并引入Cauchy-Riemann方程正则化以保持全纯结构，实现参数效率提升16倍（280参数），在六个复动力系统中达到R²>0.95，成功识别所有符号族，并在噪声鲁棒性和迁移学习方面显著优于MLP基准，同时提供可解释的符号方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Complex dynamical systems (e.g., z² + c) exhibit fractal boundaries with extreme sensitivity to initial conditions. Existing MLPs within Neural ODEs lack complex-analytic priors, violate Cauchy-Riemann conditions, and function as opaque black-box approximators incapable of yielding governing equations.

Method: Replaced MLP with KAN (Kolmogorov-Arnold Network) using learnable B-spline activations on network edges; incorporated Cauchy-Riemann equations as differentiable regularization to preserve holomorphic structure; applied automatic spline-to-formula fitting for symbolic equation extraction.

Result: On six complex dynamical systems: velocity-field R² > 0.95; correctly identified all six governing symbolic families; Julia set fractal boundary reconstruction up to 98.0% agreement; only 4% MSE degradation under 10% noise vs 15.2× for MLPs; 90.4% improvement in transfer learning from quadratic to cubic dynamics.

Conclusion: KANs are established as a parameter-efficient (16× fewer parameters), interpretable alternative to MLPs for physics-informed discovery of holomorphic dynamics, uniquely providing symbolic equations, enforced holomorphic structure, and superior noise resilience.

Abstract: Complex dynamical systems governed by holomorphic maps such as $z^2 + c$ exhibit fractal boundaries with extreme sensitivity to initial conditions. Accurately modelling these structures from data requires methods that respect the underlying complex-analytic geometry, yet Multi-Layer Perceptrons (MLPs) within Neural Ordinary Differential Equations (Neural ODEs) lack complex-analytic priors, violate the Cauchy--Riemann conditions, and function as opaque approximators incapable of yielding governing equations. We introduce Holomorphic KAN-ODE, a framework that replaces the MLP with a Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (KAN) whose learnable B-spline activations reside on network edges, and incorporates Cauchy--Riemann equations as a differentiable regularization to preserve holomorphic structure. We evaluate on six families of complex dynamical systems spanning polynomial and transcendental classes. With only 280 parameters ($16\times$ fewer than the MLP baseline), the network achieves velocity-field $R^2 > 0.95$ on all six systems, correctly identifies all six governing symbolic families through automatic spline-to-formula fitting, and reconstructs Julia set fractal boundaries with up to 98.0\% agreement. Crucially, the model exhibits only 4\% MSE degradation under 10\% observation noise versus $15.2\times$ for MLPs, and achieves 90.4\% improvement in transfer learning from quadratic to cubic dynamics. While the MLP attains lower pointwise reconstruction error due to its larger capacity, the KAN uniquely provides interpretable symbolic equations, enforced holomorphic structure, and superior noise resilience, capabilities that are entirely absent in black-box architectures. These results establish KANs as a parameter-efficient, interpretable alternative to MLPs for physics-informed discovery of holomorphic dynamics.

</details>


### [165] [Vector Policy Optimization: Training for Diversity Improves Test-Time Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22817)
*Ryan Bahlous-Boldi,Isha Puri,Idan Shenfeld,Akarsh Kumar,Mehul Damani,Sebastian Risi,Omar Khattab,Zhang-Wei Hong,Pulkit Agrawal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VPO是一种替代GRPO的RL算法，通过优化向量值奖励而非标量奖励来训练LLM产生多样化解决方案，在推理时搜索和进化搜索任务上显著优于传统标量RL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM后训练的标准范式优化预定义的标量奖励，导致LLM产生低熵响应分布，难以在推理时搜索中展示所需的多样性。

Method: VPO是一种RL算法，利用实践中通常是向量值的奖励（如代码生成中的每个测试用例正确性，或多个不同的用户角色），显式训练策略来预测多样化的下游奖励函数，产生多样化的解决方案。本质上是GRPO优势估计器的替代方案，训练LLM输出一组解决方案，其中各个解决方案专门针对向量奖励空间中的不同权衡。

Result: 在四个任务上，VPO在测试时搜索（如pass@k和best@k）上匹配或超越最强的标量RL基线，且差距随搜索预算增加而扩大。对于进化搜索，VPO模型能解决GRPO模型根本无法解决的问题。

Conclusion: 随着推理时搜索变得越来越标准化，优化多样性可能需要成为默认的后训练目标。

Abstract: Language models must now generalize out of the box to novel environments and work inside inference-scaling search procedures, such as AlphaEvolve, that select rollouts with a variety of task-specific reward functions. Unfortunately, the standard paradigm of LLM post-training optimizes a pre-specified scalar reward, often leading current LLMs to produce low-entropy response distributions and thus to struggle at displaying the diversity that inference-time search will require. We propose Vector Policy Optimization (VPO), an RL algorithm that explicitly trains policies to anticipate diverse downstream reward functions and to produce diverse solutions. VPO exploits that rewards are often vector-valued in practice, like per-test-case correctness in code generation or, say, multiple different user personas or reward models. VPO is essentially a drop-in replacement for the GRPO advantage estimator, but it trains the LLM to output a set of solutions where individual solutions specialize to different trade-offs in the vector reward space. Across four tasks, VPO matches or beats the strongest scalar RL baselines on test-time search (e.g. pass@k and best@k), with the gap widening as the search budget grows. For evolutionary search, VPO models unlock problems that GRPO models cannot solve at all. As test-time search becomes more standardized, optimizing for diversity may need to become the default post-training objective.

</details>


### [166] [Decomposing Ensemble Spread in Lorenz '96 With Learned Stochastic Parameterizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22242)
*Birgit Kühbacher,Daan Crommelin,Niki Kilbertus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 基于Lorenz 1996系统研究了集合预报中不确定性的来源和交互机制，发现时间持续性随机参数化是改善散度-误差一致性的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天气预报和气候预测存在固有的不确定性，但现有集合预报方法往往欠离散化，散度相对于预报误差增长过慢。

Method: 采用双尺度Lorenz 1996系统作为受控测试平台，设计系统性方法分离内禀变异性、初值扰动和随机模型不确定性，比较多种集合配置和参数化策略（包括确定性、自回归及新型贝叶斯和基于流的方法）。

Result: 集合扰动不会增加系统的长期方差，而是调节轨迹去相关的速度；具有时间持续结构的随机参数化能增强早期散度增长并改善散度-误差一致性。

Conclusion: This study clarifies how different sources of uncertainty interact in chaotic systems and provides guidance for the design and evaluation of stochastic parameterizations in weather and climate models.

Abstract: Weather and climate forecasts are inherently uncertain due to chaotic dynamics, imperfect initial conditions, and incomplete representation of the underlying physical processes. Operational ensemble forecasts aim to represent these uncertainties through forecast spread, yet many approaches yield underdispersive estimates, with spread that grows too slowly relative to forecast error. Using the two-scale Lorenz 1996 system as a widely used, controlled testbed, we design a systematic approach to disentangle intrinsic variability, initial-condition perturbations, and stochastic model uncertainty. We compare multiple ensemble configurations and parameterization strategies, including existing deterministic and autoregressive as well as novel Bayesian and flow-based approaches. Our results show that ensemble perturbations do not increase the system's long-term variance; rather, they regulate how rapidly trajectories decorrelate and explore the invariant measure. Stochastic parameterizations, particularly those with temporally persistent structure, enhance early spread growth and improve spread-error consistency. Overall, we bring clarity to how different sources of uncertainty interact in a chaotic system and provide guidance for the design and evaluation of stochastic parameterizations in weather and climate models.

</details>


### [167] [Explainable AI for Data-Driven Design of High-Dimensional Predictive Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22243)
*Junyu Yan,Damian Machlanski,Kurt Butler,Panagiotis Dimitrakopoulos,Ewen M Harrison,Bruce Guthrie,Sotirios A Tsaftaris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究开发了"探索性AI推荐器"框架，利用可解释AI将复杂数据模式转化为特征排除、非线性项和交互建议，以改进透明预测模型的性能。在大规模患者跌倒预测数据中，该方法使C-index提升至0.815，并在多数据集上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 健康数据预测建模面临挑战：当特征数量庞大时，手动设计最优方案困难；复杂机器学习模型虽性能高，但"黑箱"特性限制了临床信任度和可解释性，无法满足决策透明性要求。

Method: 开发了一个"探索性AI推荐器"框架，使用灵活的AI建模捕捉复杂数据模式，并通过可解释AI技术将模式转化为三类建议：特征排除、非线性项和特征交互。通过将建议纳入Cox比例风险(CPH)模型进行评估。

Result: 在245,614例患者数据中，推荐排除23个特征、2个特征的非线性项、221个特征交互。C-index从0.805提升至0.815，校正性能也改善（截距：-0.006→0.003；斜率：1.063→0.950）。所有建议均有文献支持，并在两个额外公开数据集上验证有效。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一个结合可解释AI与数据驱动设计的方法，能够在保持模型透明性的前提下有效提升预测性能。C-index提升1个百分点，校正性能也得到改善，且所有建议均有文献支持。该方法在多个数据集上展现了广泛适用性，为高维透明预测模型的开发提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Predictive modelling is important for health data analysis and data-driven clinical decision-making. However, predictive studies are challenging to design optimally by hand when tens or even hundreds of features require selection, transformation, or interaction modelling. While complex machine learning models offer high performance, their "black-box" nature limits the clinical trust, transparency, and interpretability required for decision-making. We developed and evaluated an Exploratory AI Recommender that provides data-driven recommendations to improve predictive performance of existing interpretable statistical models. The developed framework uses flexible AI modelling to capture complex data patterns and explainable AI techniques to translate the patterns into three recommendation types: feature exclusion, non-linear terms, and feature interactions. We evaluated the framework by comparing predictive performance of a baseline (i.e., no interactions or non-linear terms) Cox Proportional Hazards (CPH) model against an augmented CPH incorporating recommendations suggested by our method. The primary analysis predicts the time to the first occurrence of a fall or related injury in 245,614 patients. Our method recommended excluding 23 features, including non-linear terms for two features, and including 221 suggested feature interactions. The C-index improved from 0.805 (95% CI 0.798-0.812) to 0.815 (95% CI 0.809-0.822), and so did calibration (intercept: -0.006 to 0.003; slope: 1.063 to 0.950). All recommendations were supported by existing literature. The method also proved effective on two additional public datasets, demonstrating wider applicability. The proposed Exploratory AI Recommender demonstrates the potential of explainable AI and data-driven study design to improve the process of developing, and the performance of high-dimensional transparent predictive models.

</details>


### [168] [What are the Right Symmetries for Formal Theorem Proving?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22257)
*Krzysztof Olejniczak,Radoslav Dimitrov,Xingyue Huang,Bernardo Cuenca Grau,Jinwoo Kim,İsmail İlkan Ceylan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM定理证明器对问题表示变化的敏感性，发现现有SOTA模型不满足证明等变性证明对称性和成功不变性。提出测试时聚合等价重写的方法，理论上可恢复成功不变性，实证上可提高鲁棒性和性能。强调对称性是LLM定理证明中缺失的关键归纳偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-based formal theorem provers are highly sensitive to superficial variations in problem representation: semantically equivalent statements exhibit drastically different proof success rates, revealing a failure to respect structural symmetries inherent in formal mathematics. This raises the central question of what the right symmetries for formal theorem proving are.

Method: Introduced rewriting categories, a category-theoretic framework capturing compositional (generally non-invertible) transformations induced by proof tactics. Used this to formalize two symmetry notions: (1) proof equivariance governing how proof distributions transform under rewrites, and (2) success invariance requiring equivalent statements to be solved with equal probability.

Result: State-based next-tactic provers naturally satisfy proof equivariance by operating on proof states. In contrast, state-of-the-art LLM-based provers satisfy neither proof equivariance nor success invariance, exhibiting large performance variation across equivalent formulations.

Conclusion: Test-time aggregation methods recover success invariance in the sampling limit and improve robustness/performance under fixed inference budgets. Symmetry is identified as a key missing inductive bias in LLM-based theorem proving, with test-time computation suggested as a practical route to approximate it.

Abstract: Formal theorem provers based on large language models (LLMs) are highly sensitive to superficial variations in problem representation: semantically equivalent statements can exhibit drastically different proof success rates, revealing a failure to respect structural symmetries inherent in formal mathematics. This raises a central question: what are the right symmetries for formal theorem proving? We introduce rewriting categories, a category-theoretic framework capturing the compositional, generally non-invertible transformations induced by proof tactics, and use it to formalize two symmetry notions: proof equivariance, governing how proof distributions transform under rewrites, and success invariance (i.e., invariance of success probability), requiring equivalent statements to be solved with the same probability. We observe that state-based next-tactic provers naturally satisfy proof equivariance by operating on proof states. In contrast, state-of-the-art LLM-based provers satisfy neither property, exhibiting large performance variation across equivalent formulations. To mitigate this, we propose test-time methods that aggregate over equivalent rewritings of the input, showing theoretically that they recover success invariance in the sampling limit, and empirically, that they improve robustness and performance under fixed inference budgets. Our results highlight symmetry as a key missing inductive bias in LLM-based theorem proving and suggest test-time computation as a practical route to approximate it.

</details>


### [169] [An Evidence Hierarchy for Bayesian Object Classification via OSINT-Aided Heterogeneous Sensor Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22259)
*Jan Nausner,Michael Hubner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种上下文感知与领域知识增强的异构传感器融合框架，用于CBRNE威胁检测与分类。该框架通过建立证据层次结构（直接/指示性/上下文信息）、集成OSINT环境上下文信息、以及贝叶斯先验优化，有效克服了单传感器检测局限性和高杂波环境挑战。在仿真评估中，该方法达到95%的分类准确率，并展现出对杂波和先验失配的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构传感器融合在CBRNE威胁检测、定位和分类中至关重要，但面临以下挑战：单个传感器检测能力有限且可靠性各异，仅能提供间接威胁指示；高杂波率对融合系统构成严峻挑战；高质量标注数据集的稀缺制约了智能传感器中基于学习的检测与分类模型的性能提升。

Method: 提出了一种上下文感知且领域知识增强的融合方法，包含三个核心组件：1）构建证据层次结构，支持直接信息、指示性信息和上下文信息的建模；2）通过收集、处理和利用开源情报（OSINT）输入将环境上下文信息引入融合过程；3）利用证据层次结构的所有层级，设计贝叶斯威胁类型分类机制，采用领域知识驱动的先验分布。

Result: 仿真场景评估结果表明，所提融合方法在杂波干扰和先验失配场景下表现出较强的鲁棒性，总体分类准确率最高可达95%。

Conclusion: 所提出的上下文感知与领域知识增强的异构传感器融合方法，通过证据层次化建模、OSINT上下文信息集成及贝叶斯先验优化，有效解决了CBRNE威胁检测中的传感器局限性、杂波干扰和先验失配问题。该方法在模拟场景中展现出较高的分类准确率（最高达95%），为智能传感器领域的威胁检测与分类提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Heterogeneous sensor fusion is vital for detecting, localizing, and classifying CBRNE threats. However, individual sensors are often only capable of detecting a subset of relevant threats with varying reliability or can even provide only indirect threat indications, making threat classification challenging. Furthermore, high clutter rates on the sensor side present a great challenge for fusion systems. Additionally, the limited availability of high quality datasets hinders the advancement of learning-based detection and classification models in smart sensors. To mitigate these sensor related shortcomings, a context-aware and domain knowledge-enhanced fusion process is proposed. First, a novel evidence hierarchy is established that enables modeling of direct, indicative, and contextual information. Second, contextual information about the environment is introduced into the fusion process, by collecting, processing, and exploiting OSINT inputs. Third, all levels of the evidence hierarchy are used to craft a Bayesian threat type classification mechanism with domain knowledge-informed priors. The proposed methodology is evaluated in simulated scenarios, and the results demonstrate the benefit of the proposed fusion approach in terms of robustness to clutter and prior mismatch, with an overall classification accuracy of up to 95%.

</details>


### [170] [Tailoring Teaching to Aptitude: Direction-Adaptive Self-Distillation for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22263)
*Hongbin Zhang,Chaozheng Wang,Kehai Chen,Youcheng Pan,Yang Xiang,Jinpeng Wang,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DASD通过引入基于token不确定性水平的自适应方向监督机制，解决了OPSD在复杂推理任务中抑制预测不确定性的问题，在数学推理基准上取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD(策略自蒸馏)通过抑制预测不确定性来降低复杂推理能力。问题根源在于对不同不确定性水平的token应用统一方向的教师监督：高熵时服从教师会抑制探索，低熵时偏离教师会降低步骤准确性。

Method: 提出了方向自适应自蒸馏(DASD)，将特权自蒸馏从统一的教师模仿重新框架为基于熵路由的方向监督：针对高熵token远离特权教师以保持探索性，针对低熵token拉向教师以稳定执行。

Result: 在六个数学推理基准上，DASD实现了最佳的宏 Avg@16，超越了强RLVR和自蒸馏基线。Pass@k、推理健康度和泛化分析表明这些平均收益来自于保持探索性同时不牺牲步骤级执行。

Conclusion: DASD preserves exploration without sacrificing step-level execution, providing a principled solution to the uncertainty suppression problem in OPSD for complex reasoning tasks.

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) is an emerging LLM post-training paradigm in which the model serves as its own teacher: conditioned on privileged information such as a reference trace or hint, the same policy provides dense token-level supervision on its own rollouts. However, recent studies show that OPSD degrades complex reasoning by suppressing predictive uncertainty, which supports exploration and hypothesis revision. Our token-level analysis shows that this failure arises from applying a uniform direction of teacher supervision across tokens with different uncertainty levels: conformity to the privileged self-teacher suppresses exploration at high entropy, while deviation from the teacher degrades step accuracy at low entropy. Accordingly, we propose \textbf{Direction-Adaptive Self-Distillation} (\textbf{DASD}), which reframes privileged self-distillation from uniform teacher imitation into entropy-routed directional supervision: high-entropy tokens are pushed away from the privileged teacher to preserve exploration, while low-entropy tokens are pulled toward the teacher to stabilize step-level execution. Across six mathematical reasoning benchmarks, DASD achieves the best macro Avg@16 over strong RLVR and self-distillation baselines. Pass@$k$, reasoning-health, and generalization analyses show that these average gains come from preserving exploration without sacrificing step-level execution.

</details>


### [171] [Detecting Atypical Clients in Federated Learning via Representation-Level Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22266)
*Cristian Pérez-Corral,Jose I. Mestre,Alberto Fernández-Hernández,Manuel F. Dolz,Enrique S. Quitana-Ortí*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对联邦学习中客户端数据异构和异常更新问题，提出一种基于激活空间划分的功能偏离度量，通过共享探测集评估客户端对模型输入空间处理方式的改变，实现对异常客户端的有效识别，为风险感知聚合提供理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中数据异构性导致更新不稳定和全局性能下降，且客户端更新可能因异常输入而偏离预期，影响聚合可靠性。

Method: 通过共享探测集评估每个客户端本地训练如何改变激活诱导的输入空间划分，生成置换不变且可解释的度量。

Result: 该信号可有效识别诱导非典型功能变化的客户端，区分稳定但异构的客户端与显著偏离全局状态的客户端。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于激活划分的功能偏离度量可有效识别异常客户端，为风险感知聚合策略提供了轻量级、可解释的工具。

Abstract: Federated learning enables collaborative training across distributed clients with heterogeneous data, but such heterogeneity often leads to unstable updates and degraded global performance. Moreover, in practical deployments, client updates may deviate from the expected behavior not only due to benign not i.i.d. distributions, but also due to distributional shifts or anomalous inputs, raising concerns about the reliability of the aggregation process. In this work, we propose a lightweight geometric signal to quantify the functional deviation of a client with respect to the global model. Instead of comparing model parameters or gradients, our approach measures how the local training of each client alters the activation-induced partition of the input space, evaluated on a shared probe set. This yields a permutation-invariant, interpretable metric of client--global divergence that captures differences in how data is processed by the model. We show that this signal effectively identifies clients that induce atypical functional changes, distinguishing stable yet heterogeneous clients from those whose updates significantly diverge from the global regime. As a result, the proposed metric provides a simple tool for monitoring client behavior and enabling risk-aware aggregation strategies in federated learning systems.

</details>


### [172] [Adaptive Measurement Allocation for Learning Kernelized SVMs Under Noisy Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22275)
*Artur Miroszewski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对量子机器学习中从噪声伯努利观测学习核化SVM的问题，提出了一种自适应测量分配策略。该方法结合几何敏感性与主动集不稳定性两个指标，将测量资源集中于核矩阵中最具决策关键性的区域。理论分析揭示了自适应分配的收益取决于核重要性结构的异质性，实验验证了在支持向量恢复和决策精度上的显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统核方法假设对Gram矩阵的精确、无噪声访问，但在量子机器学习等新兴场景中，每个核元素必须从噪声观测中推断，且精度取决于有限的测量预算如何分配。现有方法主要依赖均匀分配策略，忽略了核化分类器对Gram矩阵元素依赖的高度非均匀性。

Method: 提出一种结合几何敏感性与主动集不稳定性的自适应测量分配策略。几何敏感性量化单个核矩阵元素扰动对分类器间隔的影响，主动集不稳定性量化测量噪声导致的支持向量成员离散变化的概率。理论分析建立了自适应分配的优势与核重要性结构异质性之间的联系。

Result: 自适应分配显著改善了在固定测量预算下的支持向量恢复、间隔估计和决策函数精度；双重系数稳定性准则实现早停，仅使用部分测量成本即可达到近优性能；量子核实验验证了与核浓度等现象一致的机制依赖性行为。

Conclusion: 理论分析表明，自适应分配策略的优势由核重要性结构的异质性决定，在不同机制下自适应或均匀策略各有优劣；实验验证了该方法在量子核场景下的有效性，并揭示了核浓度等现象导致的机制依赖性行为。

Abstract: Kernel methods are typically formulated under the assumption of exact, noise-free access to the Gram matrix. However, in emerging settings such as quantum machine learning, each kernel entry must be inferred from noisy observations, and its accuracy depends on how a limited measurement budget is allocated. Despite this, existing approaches overwhelmingly rely on uniform allocation, which equalizes estimator variance but ignores the highly non-uniform dependence of kernelized classifiers on the Gram matrix. In this work, we introduce an adaptive measurement-allocation strategy for learning kernelized Support Vector Machines (SVMs) from noisy Bernoulli observations. Our approach combines two complementary principles: (i) geometric sensitivity, capturing how perturbations of individual kernel entries affect the classifier margin, and (ii) active-set instability, quantifying the probability of discrete changes in support-vector membership induced by measurement noise. These signals define a task-aware allocation scheme that concentrates measurements on the most decision-critical regions of the kernel matrix. We provide a theoretical analysis showing that the benefit of adaptive allocation is governed by the heterogeneity of the induced kernel importance structure, leading to distinct regimes in which adaptive or uniform strategies are preferable. Empirical evaluations on synthetic datasets demonstrate that adaptive allocation significantly improves support-vector recovery, margin estimation, and decision-function accuracy under fixed measurement budgets. A dual-coefficient stability criterion further enables early stopping, achieving near-optimal performance while using only a fraction of the measurement cost. Additional experiments on quantum kernels derived from real-world data reveal a regime-dependent behavior aligned with known phenomena such as kernel concentration. Together...

</details>


### [173] [EmoTrack: Robust Depression Tracking from Counseling Transcripts across Session Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22286)
*Zhaomin Wu,Jiayi Li,Bingsheng He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究针对心理咨询场景中的抑郁严重度预测问题，提出 LongCounsel 多会话数据集与 EmoTrack 预测框架。核心创新在于结合 LLM 临床信号提取与语义嵌入表示，并通过症状特异性预测器和跨会话记忆机制，实现单会话和多会话场景下的鲁棒 PHQ-8 预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抑郁预测方法存在两难：微调方法泛化性差，提示方法缺乏纵向上下文支持，难以在单会话与多会话场景下实现鲁棒的 PHQ-8 预测。

Method: EmoTrack 结合 LLM 提取的临床信号与冻结的轮级语义嵌入，在转录表示上训练症状特异性预测器；当存在历史会话时，通过紧凑的跨会话记忆模块融合先前的会话信息，从而兼顾临床诊断精度与纵向追踪能力。

Result: 在 LongCounsel 和 DAIC-WOZ 数据集上，单会话基准测试 MAE 相对降低 13.5%，多会话场景下与最强纵向基线持平。

Conclusion: EmoTrack 通过将 LLM 提取的临床信号与冻结的语义嵌入结合，并使用症状特异性预测器，在单会话场景下相较最强基线实现 13.5% 的 MAE 相对降低，在多会话场景下也与最强纵向基线保持竞争力，证明了所提框架的有效性。

Abstract: Text-based counseling is an important interface for AI mental-health support, where transcripts may be used to monitor depression severity and flag sessions requiring timely human review. However, robust PHQ-8 prediction across session regimes remains challenging: fine-tuning-based methods can exploit richer supervision but may generalize poorly under data scarcity, while prompt-based LLM methods are data-efficient but usually treat each transcript holistically and provide limited support for longitudinal context. We study robust depression tracking from counseling transcripts across single-session and multi-session regimes. We introduce LongCounsel, a multi-session counseling dataset with session-level PHQ-8 supervision for evaluating repeated-session tracking under partial symptom disclosure and cross-session continuity. We further propose EmoTrack, a PHQ-8 prediction framework that combines LLM-extracted clinical signals with frozen turn-level semantic embeddings and trains symptom-specific predictors over the resulting transcript representation. When prior sessions are available, EmoTrack can further incorporate them through compact cross-session memory. Experiments on LongCounsel and DAIC-WOZ show that EmoTrack achieves a clear gain on the real single-session benchmark, including a 13.5% relative MAE reduction over the strongest DAIC-WOZ baseline, and remains competitive with the strongest longitudinal baseline on LongCounsel.

</details>


### [174] [Long-term Fairness with Selective Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22291)
*Giovani Valdrighi,Isabel Valera,Marcos Medeiros Raimundo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究选择性标签场景下的长期公平性问题，提出利用标签预测模型置信度修正可观测公平性的理论框架，并基于此设计强化学习算法实现长期公平决策，在半合成环境中验证了该方法可达到接近 oracle 的公平性和性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard long-term fairness approaches assume fully observed labels, but critical applications like hiring and lending exhibit selective labels—labels only revealed for positively decided instances (e.g., loan repayment only observed when loan is granted). This creates a fundamental challenge: fairness measures computed from observable data may not reflect true underlying fairness.

Method: 1) Analytical analysis proving naive solutions fail in selective labels setting; 2) Novel decomposition framework separating fairness measure into observed fairness and predictor-induced bias; 3) Derivation of sufficient conditions for true fairness using predictor confidence; 4) RL algorithm design leveraging the theoretical framework for long-term fair decision-making.

Result: 1) Analytical proof that naive fairness solutions are insufficient under selective labels; 2) Theoretical framework enabling true fairness estimation from observable data using predictor confidence as a correction mechanism; 3) Empirical validation showing the RL algorithm achieves performance comparable to oracle agents with full label access.

Conclusion: The proposed RL algorithm achieves comparable fairness and performance to an oracle agent with access to true labels, validated in semisynthetic environments. The framework enables practitioners to achieve true fairness by appropriately setting confidence thresholds for the label predictor.

Abstract: Long-term fairness algorithms aim to satisfy fairness beyond static and short-term notions by accounting for the dynamics between decision-making policies and population behavior. Most previous approaches evaluate performance and fairness measures from observable features and a label, which is assumed to be fully observed. However, in scenarios such as hiring or lending, the labels (e.g., ability to repay the loan) are selective labels as they are only revealed based on positive decisions (e.g., when a loan is granted). In this paper, we study long-term fairness in the selective labels setting and analytically show that naive solutions do not guarantee fairness. To address this gap, we then introduce a novel framework that leverages both the observed data and a label predictor model to estimate the true fairness measure value by decomposing it into the observed fairness and bias from label predictions. This allows us to derive sufficient conditions to satisfy true fairness from observable quantities by using the confidence in the predictor model. Finally, we rely on our theoretical results to propose a novel reinforcement learning algorithm for effective long-term fair decision-making with selective labels. In semisynthetic environments, the proposed algorithm reached comparable fairness and performance to an agent with oracle access to the true labels.

</details>


### [175] [One LR Doesn't Fit All: Heavy-Tail Guided Layerwise Learning Rates for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22297)
*Di He,Songjun Tu,Keyu Wang,Lu Yin,Shiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLR introduces an adaptive layerwise learning rate scheme for Transformers based on heavy-tailedness quantification. By assigning larger learning rates to layers with weaker heavy-tailedness, LLR promotes balanced training, achieving 1.5x speedup and accuracy improvements from 47.09% to 49.02% across diverse architectures and scales, with minimal tuning overhead.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The prevailing practice of applying a uniform learning rate across all layers overlooks the structural heterogeneity of Transformers, potentially limiting their effectiveness as backbones for LLMs.

Method: Layerwise Learning Rate (LLR) assigns distinct learning rates to individual Transformer layers based on Heavy-Tailed Self-Regularization (HT-SR) theory. The method analyzes the empirical spectral density (ESD) of weight correlation matrices to quantify heavy-tailedness—layers with weaker heavy-tailedness receive larger learning rates to accelerate training, while those with stronger heavy-tailedness receive smaller learning rates.

Result: Experiments across architectures (LLaMA to GPT-nano), optimizers (AdamW and Muon), and scales (60M-1B) demonstrate up to 1.5x training speedup and improved generalization (average zero-shot accuracy improved from 47.09% to 49.02%).

Conclusion: LLR achieves balanced training across layers, enabling faster convergence and improved generalization. It demonstrates strong transferability of optimal LR settings from uniform baselines, offering a practical and low-overhead approach to optimizing Transformer training.

Abstract: Learning rate configuration is a fundamental aspect of modern deep learning. The prevailing practice of applying a uniform learning rate across all layers overlooks the structural heterogeneity of Transformers, potentially limiting their effectiveness as the backbone of Large Language Models (LLMs). In this paper, we introduce Layerwise Learning Rate (LLR), an adaptive scheme that assigns distinct learning rates to individual Transformer layers. Our method is grounded in Heavy-Tailed Self-Regularization (HT-SR) theory, which characterizes the empirical spectral density (ESD) of weight correlation matrices to quantify heavy-tailedness. Layers with weaker heavy-tailedness are assigned larger learning rates to accelerate their training, while layers with stronger heavy-tailedness receive smaller learning rates. By tailoring learning rates in this manner, LLR promotes balanced training across layers, leading to faster convergence and improved generalization. Extensive experiments across architectures (from LLaMA to GPT-nano), optimizers (AdamW and Muon), and parameter scales (60M-1B) demonstrate that LLR achieves up to 1.5x training speedup and outperforms baselines, notably raising average zero-shot accuracy from 47.09% to 49.02%. A key advantage of LLR is its low tuning overhead: it transfers nearly optimal LR settings directly from the uniform baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/hed-ucas/Layer-wise-Learning-Rate.

</details>


### [176] [Chebyshev Policies and the Mountain Car Problem: Reinforcement Learning for Low-Dimensional Control Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22305)
*Stefan Huber,Hannes Unger,Georg Schäfer,Jakob Rehrl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: The authors analytically solved the 36-year-old Mountain Car problem and discovered that despite simple optimal control, modern RL agents perform poorly. They introduced Chebyshev policies—a lightweight, universal policy class from first principles that reduces regret by 4.18x with 277x fewer parameters than neural networks, offering a practical alternative for low-dimensional control tasks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Despite 36 years as an RL benchmark, the Mountain Car problem lacked an analytical solution. The authors discovered that optimal control is surprisingly simple, yet modern RL agents exhibit a large performance gap to optimality. This motivated the development of a principled, lightweight policy class.

Method: The authors analytically solved the Mountain Car problem to derive an optimal control solution. Based on this analysis, they introduced Chebyshev policies—a universal (dense) class of RL policies derived from first principles. These policies can be trained as drop-in replacements for neural networks.

Result: Chebyshev policies reduced regret by 4.18x compared to neural nets while using 277x fewer parameters. Consistent improvements were observed across multiple RL tasks including real-world nonlinear motion control, with PPO, ARS, and REINFORCE algorithms.

Conclusion: Chebyshev policies represent a compelling, lightweight alternative (or addition) to neural networks for low-dimensional control tasks, offering improved sample efficiency, explainability, and real-time capability.

Abstract: We analytically solve the Mountain Car problem, a canonical benchmark in RL, and derive an optimal control solution, closing a gap after 36 years. This enables us to reveal two surprising insights: The optimal control is quite simple, yet modern RL agents display a large gap to optimality. Motivated by the analysis of the optimal control, we introduce Chebyshev policies as a universal (i.e. dense) class of RL policies from first principles. They can be trained as drop-in replacements of neural nets, reducing the regret by a factor of 4.18, while requiring 277 times fewer parameters, fostering sample efficiency, explainability and realtime capability. Chebyshev policies are evaluated on further RL tasks, including a real-world nonlinear motion control testbed. They consistently improve performance over neural nets with PPO, ARS and REINFORCE. Our results demonstrate how Chebyshev policies offer a compelling and lightweight alternative or addition to neural nets for low-dimensional control tasks.

</details>


### [177] [Learning Causal Orderings for In-Context Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22335)
*Sascha Xu,Sarah Mameche,Jilles Vreeken*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TabOrder模型，将因果发现与表格预测统一于同一框架，通过学习因果拓扑序约束注意力机制，仅使用因果上先于目标的特征进行预测，在无监督学习最优变量序的同时提升模型在分布偏移和干预下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据的上下文学习主要依赖相关性结构，在分布偏移或干预情况下不可靠；现有因果发现方法侧重于结构可识别性，与预测架构脱节，无法利用因果结构提升预测模型的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出TabOrder模型，采用因果序约束的注意力机制，仅基于目标变量在因果序中位于其前的特征进行预测；通过无监督似然目标函数学习最优变量序；在标准函数模型类下提供理论依据，并分析样本缺失对因果方向识别的交互影响。

Result: 实验验证TabOrder能恢复准确的变量序，同时完成预测和填补任务，并对真实生物数据在干预下提供可解释性洞察。

Conclusion: TabOrder通过将因果结构发现与预测架构集成，在分布偏移和干预情况下比标准相关学习方法更鲁棒，同时可处理缺失值填补和生物数据干预分析等任务。

Abstract: In-context learning for tabular data sets strong predictive standards in observational settings; it however primarily relies on correlational structure, which becomes unreliable under distribution shift or intervention. While established methods to discover causal structure exist, they are often focused on structure identifiability and decoupled from the predictive architectures that could benefit from them.
  To bridge these perspectives, we study how to simultaneously infer and enforce causal structure in the form of topological variable orderings into tabular prediction. Unlike standard architectures, our model TabOrder uses causal order-constrained attention, basing predictions only on features that precede a target under a learned causal order. Similar to causal discovery methods, TabOrder learns the optimal variable ordering in an unsupervised manner through a likelihood-based objective. We justify this choice under standard functional model classes and also study how sample missingness, a common challenge in tabular data, interacts with causal direction identification. Empirically, we confirm that TabOrder recovers accurate variable orderings while addressing prediction and imputation tasks, as well as gives insight into real-world biological data under intervention.

</details>


### [178] [Physics-Informed Generative Solver: Bridging Data-Driven Priors and Conservation Laws for Stable Spatiotemporal Field Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22338)
*Ziyuan Zhu,Keyu Hu,Zhifei Chen,Yuhao Shi,Ming Bao,Jing Zhao,Gang Wang,Haitan Xu,Jiadong Li,Qijun Zhao,Xiaodong Li,Minghui Lu,Yanfeng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出物理信息生成求解器，通过将稳定先验学习与推理时物理约束分离，实现从稀疏传感器重建连续物理场，声学和气象实验验证了方法的有效性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的生成模型在从稀疏测量重建连续物理场时可能产生违反物理守恒定律的状态，这是核心难题

Method: 两个核心技术：1) Martingale-Regularized Score Matching：利用Score Fokker-Planck约束正则化分数预训练，获得动态稳定先验；2) Physics-Informed Implicit Score Sampling：通过物理残差梯度引导去噪轨迹，将样本投影到可行流形上，无需再训练

Result: 声学领域：从稀疏传感器协同生成压力场和粒子速度场，实现抑制空间混叠的稠密虚拟阵列；气象领域：成功泛化至极端稀疏条件下的ERA5真实气象场

Conclusion: 建立了将生成式AI与第一性原理科学相结合的高维反问题求解范式，具有严格性和可推广性

Abstract: Reconstructing continuous physical fields from sparse measurements is a central inverse problem, but data-driven generative models can produce states that violate governing dynamics. We introduce a physics-informed generative solver that separates stable prior learning from inference-time enforcement of conservation laws. Martingale-Regularized Score Matching regularizes score pretraining with a Score Fokker-Planck constraint, yielding a dynamically stable prior. Physics-Informed Implicit Score Sampling then guides denoising trajectories by gradients of physical residuals, projecting samples toward admissible manifolds without retraining. In acoustics, the method co-generates pressure and particle velocity from sparse sensors, enabling dense virtual arrays that suppress spatial aliasing. The same framework generalizes to real-world ERA5 meteorological fields under extreme sparsity. Together, this work establishes a rigorous and generalizable paradigm for solving high-dimensional inverse problems, bridging the gap between generative artificial intelligence and first-principles science.

</details>


### [179] [From Snapshots to Trajectories: Learning Single-Cell Gene Expression Dynamics via Conditional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22340)
*Siyu Pu,Qingqing Long,Xiaohan Huang,Haotian Chen,Jiajia Wang,Meng Xiao,Xiao Luo,Hengshu Zhu,Yuanchun Zhou,Xuezhi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: scFM addresses trajectory inference in sparse time-series scRNA-seq data by combining entropically regularized optimal transport couplings with bidirectional flow matching and regularization techniques. This framework successfully handles temporal interpolation and extrapolation while mitigating error compounding in long-horizon predictions.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Time-resolved scRNA-seq data is collected at discrete, sparsely distributed time points as unpaired snapshots, creating temporal gaps that challenge trajectory inference. Existing methods face two key issues: ambiguous local transitions leading to unstable supervision, and error compounding in long-horizon prediction due to repeated integration.

Method: Single-cell Flow Matching (scFM): A latent generative framework using coupling-conditioned flow matching with three key components: (1) Entropically regularized OT couplings between adjacent snapshots for soft flow-matching targets; (2) Bidirectional velocity fields with consistency constraints to refine couplings under sparse supervision; (3) Distribution-level alignment and latent dynamic regularization to anchor long rollouts.

Result: scFM consistently improves distributional prediction for both temporal interpolation and extrapolation. It yields more accurate trajectory reconstruction and temporally coherent visualizations, indicating faithful recovery of underlying temporal gene expression dynamics even when intermediate time points are absent.

Conclusion: scFM provides a robust framework for trajectory inference in scRNA-seq data, enabling accurate reconstruction of temporal gene expression dynamics even with sparse time points. The combination of OT-based coupling, bidirectional velocity fields, and regularization techniques effectively addresses both interpolation and extrapolation challenges.

Abstract: Single-cell RNA sequencing (scRNA-seq) provides high-dimensional profiles of cellular states, enabling data-driven modeling of cellular dynamics over time. In practice, time-resolved scRNA-seq is collected at only a few discrete time points as unpaired snapshot populations, leaving substantial temporal gaps. This motivates trajectory inference at unmeasured time points. Existing methods mainly follow two directions, optimal-transport (OT) alignment provides distribution-level matching between observed snapshots, while continuous-time generative models support forecasting via learned dynamics. However, two challenges remain: (i) unpaired snapshots render local transitions between adjacent time points ambiguous, leading to unstable supervision; and (ii) long-horizon prediction relies on repeated integration, where small modeling errors compound and cause distribution drift. To address these challenges, we propose single-cell Flow Matching (scFM), a latent generative framework based on coupling-conditioned flow matching. First, we compute entropically regularized OT couplings between adjacent snapshots and use them to construct soft, weighted flow-matching targets for learning time-dependent velocity fields. Second, we learn bidirectional velocity fields and leverage their consistency to refine couplings and improve temporal coherence under sparse supervision. Third, we introduce distribution-level alignment and latent dynamic regularization to anchor long rollouts and mitigate drift. Experiments on real-world time-series scRNA-seq datasets show that scFM consistently improves distributional prediction performance for both temporal interpolation and extrapolation. Moreover, scFM yields more accurate trajectory reconstruction and temporally coherent visualizations where intermediate time points are absent, indicating a more faithful recovery of underlying temporal gene expression dynamics.

</details>


### [180] [ASAP: Attention Sink Anchored Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22372)
*Jaehyuk Lee,Hanyoung Kim,Yanggee Kim,Donghun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ASAP是一种无需训练的ViT token剪枝框架，利用惰性随机游走模型将注意力sink建模为概率质量的dominant累积器，通过径向扩散聚类和转移权重池化实现单次快照式压缩，有效解决了高分辨率下自注意力二次复杂度带来的计算瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有token减少方法依赖局部指标（如单层注意力分数），易受注意力sink现象影响，导致无信息量的token被保留，而显著的前景物体反而被丢弃，引发计算效率与精度之间的矛盾。

Method: 将ViT信息流建模为惰性随机游走，通过累积转移矩阵计算到注意力sink的扩散距离；采用径向扩散聚类（Radial Diffusion Clustering）对token进行分区；通过转移权重池化（Transition Weight Pooling）压缩背景冗余；整个过程为单次快照式（single-shot）剪枝，无需额外训练。

Result: ASAP在图像、视频和视觉-语言任务中均优于现有最先进方法，实现了高达48%的吞吐量提升，同时保持甚至超越基线精度。

Conclusion: ASAP在保持甚至超越基线精度的同时，吞吐量提升高达48%，验证了该方法在图像、视频和视觉-语言任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) face severe computational bottlenecks due to the quadratic complexity of self-attention at high resolutions. Existing token reduction methods rely on local metrics - such as single-layer attention scores - that are inherently vulnerable to the attention sink phenomenon, where uninformative tokens are paradoxically preserved over salient foreground objects. We propose ASAP (Attention Sink Anchored Pruning), a training-free framework that recasts this sink as a feature. Modeling ViT information flow as a Lazy Random Walk, ASAP identifies the sink as a dominant accumulator of probability mass. By computing the diffusion distance to the sink within the cumulative transition matrix, ASAP partitions tokens via Radial Diffusion Clustering and compresses background redundancy through Transition Weight Pooling in a single shot. Extensive experiments across image, video, and vision-language tasks demonstrate ASAP outperforms state-of-the-art methods, accelerating throughput by up to 48% while maintaining - or even exceeding - baseline accuracy.

</details>


### [181] [Target-Aligned Bellman Backup for Cross-domain Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22376)
*Wei Liu,Ting Long*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: The paper identifies a critical flaw in existing CDRL approaches: relying on transition-level similarity for data selection ignores long-term return consistency. The proposed TABB method addresses this by evaluating source-domain data based on its contribution to accurate Bellman target estimation in the target domain, rather than superficial similarity metrics.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing CDRL methods assess data transferability solely based on transition-level similarity, which fails to capture long-term consequences. Similar transitions may yield vastly different outcomes in the target domain, potentially misleading policy learning and degrading performance.

Method: TABB measures the contribution of source-domain transitions to accurate Bellman target estimation in the target domain, selectively leveraging data based on this alignment rather than superficial transition similarity.

Result: TABB achieves strong and consistent performance across diverse cross-domain offline RL scenarios with highly limited target-domain data, demonstrating effective data selection.

Conclusion: TABB consistently outperforms baseline methods across various cross-domain offline RL settings, particularly when target-domain data is extremely limited.

Abstract: Cross-domain offline reinforcement learning (CDRL) aims to improve policy learning in a target domain by leveraging data collected from a source domain. Existing works typically assess the transferability of source-domain data by measuring its similarity to target-domain transitions, and implicitly perform transition-level selection. Transitions that are considered similar are assigned higher weights or rewards, while dissimilar ones are down-weighted. However, transition-level similarity does not necessarily imply consistency in long-term returns. Even visually or dynamically similar transitions may lead to significantly different outcomes in the target domain, which can mislead policy learning and degrade performance. To address this issue, we revisit the fundamental objective of policy learning. Since policy optimization ultimately relies on Bellman targets to evaluate the quality of decisions, we propose to assess the transferability of source-domain transitions based on their alignment with target-domain Bellman targets, rather than superficial transition similarity. Based on this insight, we propose a method termed Target-Aligned Bellman Backup (TABB), which selectively leverages source-domain data by measuring their contribution to accurate Bellman target estimation in the target domain. We evaluate TABB across a broad range of cross-domain offline RL settings with highly limited target-domain data. Experimental results show that TABB consistently achieves strong performance.

</details>


### [182] [Towards Explainability of SLMs by investigating Token Level Activation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22377)
*Sayantani Ghosh,Rajashik Datta,Amit Kumar Das,Amlan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Transformer-based language models such as BERT having 110M+ parameters have revolutionized natural language understanding, yet their internal mechanisms remain largely opaque to researchers and practitioners. Traditional attention-based interpretability methods often emphasize structurally important but semantically weak tokens such as punctuation marks rather than meaningful semantic relationships. This work introduces a lightweight and model-agnostic framework for quantifying token-level representational importance using hidden-state activation strengths at Layer 8 of BERT. The proposed Activation Flow Network (AFN) framework computes Token Activation Strength using the L2 norm of Layer-8 hidden representations, enabling direct ranking of semantically salient tokens. The study further introduces a threshold-based activation bucket formulation that partitions tokens into HIGH-activation and LOW-activation groups using an empirical upper-quartile activation boundary. Experimental observations demonstrate that semantically meaningful content words consistently occupy the HIGH-activation bucket and dominate representational activation shifts, while structurally supportive tokens contribute comparatively less. The results suggest that Layer 8 acts as a critical semantic consolidation zone balancing structural and semantic information processing. By revealing how activation magnitudes concentrate around semantically informative tokens, this work provides an interpretable and computationally efficient alternative to attentioncentric analysis, contributing toward transforming BERT from a "black box" into a more transparent "glass box" model for natural language understanding.

</details>


### [183] [Hybrid Kolmogorov-Arnold Network and XGBoost Framework for Week-Ahead Price Forecasting in Australia's National Electricity Market](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22387)
*Houxuan Zhou,Sriram Prasad,Chenghao Huang,Jiajie Feng,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对高可再生能源渗透的澳大利亚电力市场，提出KAN+XGBoost混合框架进行周度电价预测，融合全局非线性建模与局部稳健性，在真实数据上验证了混合策略的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确电价预测对市场参与者的运营规划和风险管理至关重要，但面临强波动性、非线性动态和频繁极端价格飙升等挑战。澳大利亚NEM市场因高可再生能源渗透率进一步增加了不确定性。

Method: 提出了一种混合KAN+XGBoost框架，将Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks（KAN）的全局非线性表示能力与XGBoost的局部稳健性相结合，以同时捕捉长期依赖关系和短期价格波动。采用扩展窗口评估策略在澳大利亚国家电力市场（NEM）真实数据上进行周度预测实验。

Result: 所提模型在MAE指标上相比XGBoost降低约12%，相比朴素基准降低超过50%，优于SARIMAX、LSTM、 standalone KAN等基准方法。

Conclusion: 混合学习策略（KAN+XGBoost）为高度动态的电力市场中的电价预测提供了有效且稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate electricity price forecasting (EPF) is essential for market participants to support operational planning and risk management, yet remains challenging due to strong volatility, nonlinear dynamics, and frequent extreme price spikes. These challenges are particularly pronounced in the Australian National Electricity Market (NEM), where high renewable penetration further increases uncertainty. This paper investigates week-ahead electricity price forecasting and proposes a hybrid KAN+XGBoost framework that integrates Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KAN) with tree-based learning. The proposed approach combines the global nonlinear representation capability of KAN with the local robustness of XGBoost to capture both long-term dependencies and short-term price fluctuations. Experiments are conducted on real-world NEM data using an expanding window evaluation strategy. The results demonstrate that the proposed model outperforms benchmark methods, including SARIMAX, Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM), standalone KAN, and XGBoost, reducing MAE by approximately 12% compared to XGBoost and by over 50% compared to a naive baseline. The results suggest that hybrid learning strategies provide an effective and robust solution for electricity price forecasting in highly dynamic electricity markets.

</details>


### [184] [Cross-Species RSA Reveals Conserved Early Visual Alignment but Divergent Higher-Area Rankings Across Human fMRI and Macaque Electrophysiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22401)
*Nils Leutenegger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This study tests whether five neural network learning rules generalize across species by comparing them to macaque electrophysiology data. Results show that early visual alignment is robust across species, with STDP and predictive coding performing best, but higher-area (IT) alignment is primarily limited by model capacity and training data rather than the learning rule itself.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Test whether the relationship between learning rules and brain alignment generalizes across species by extending findings from human fMRI to macaque electrophysiology.

Method: Representational Similarity Analysis (RSA) comparing five learning rules (backpropagation, feedback alignment, predictive coding, STDP, random weights) against macaque electrophysiology data using identical model weights from prior human study.

Result: All models show higher alignment with macaque early visual cortex (V1/V2) than human fMRI; STDP and PC produce highest V1/V2 alignment; learning rule rankings show no detectable correlation across species at IT; pretrained ResNet-50 substantially outperforms custom CNNs at IT.

Conclusion: Early visual alignment is robust across species, while higher-area alignment is modulated by model capacity and stimulus domain.

Abstract: Does the relationship between learning rules and brain alignment generalize across species? We extend our prior finding that untrained CNNs match backpropagation at human V1 by testing the same five learning rules against macaque electrophysiology. The rules are backpropagation (BP), feedback alignment (FA), predictive coding (PC), spike-timing-dependent plasticity (STDP), and an untrained random-weights baseline. The macaque data come from two datasets: MajajHong2015 (V4/IT, 3,200 stimulus presentations, 88/168 neurons) and FreemanZiemba2013 (V1/V2, 135 stimuli, 102/103 neurons). Using RSA with identical model weights from our human study, we find: (1) all models achieve higher alignment with macaque early visual cortex (rho = 0.15-0.30 at V1/V2) than with human fMRI (rho = 0.01-0.08), consistent with the higher signal-to-noise ratio of electrophysiology; (2) STDP and PC produce the highest macaque V1/V2 alignment (rho ~ 0.30 and 0.28), consistent with their leading position among trained rules in human V1; (3) at IT, learning rule rankings show no detectable correlation across species (Kendall's tau = 0.00, p = 1.00), though this null result is expected given that n = 5 provides power only at tau = +/-1.0, and is further confounded by stimulus set differences; (4) a pretrained ResNet-50 (ImageNet) achieves rho = 0.25 at macaque IT, substantially above all custom CNN conditions (rho = 0.07-0.14), suggesting IT alignment is limited by model capacity and training data rather than by the learning rule. Noise ceilings, multi-seed variability (5 seeds), and a stimulus-control analysis are reported. These results demonstrate that early visual alignment is robust across species, while higher-area alignment is modulated by model capacity and stimulus domain.

</details>


### [185] [AMUSE: Anytime Muon with Stable Gradient Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22432)
*Jueun Kim,Baekrok Shin,Jihun Yun,Beomhan Baek,Minhak Song,Chulhee Yun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Muon优化器的底层机制，通过"河流-山谷"损失景观框架揭示其加速bulk进展但放大dominant方向振荡的双重效应，并提出AMUSE算法，通过时变插值系数动态融合Muon快速进展与Schedule-Free稳定化效果，实现无需学习率计划的随时训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习依赖AdamW和学习率计划，但两者都受到挑战：Schedule-Free通过迭代平均去除显式计划，Muon通过正交化动量改善更新几何。尽管Muon表现优异，其底层机制仍未被完全理解。

Method: 研究通过"河流-山谷"损失景观框架分析Muon，发现其正交化加速bulk子空间进展但放大了dominant方向噪声导致振荡。在此基础上提出AMUSE，集成Muon的快速bulk进展与Schedule-Free平均化的稳定效果，采用时变插值系数在快速Muon序列和稳定平均序列之间动态过渡。

Result: AMUSE在视觉任务和大语言模型预训练中持续改善了性能-迭代帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: AMUSE实现了无需学习率计划的随时训练能力，在视觉任务和大语言模型预训练中均改善了性能-迭代帕累托前沿，超越(Schedule-Free) AdamW和Muon。

Abstract: Modern deep learning commonly relies on AdamW with prescribed learning rate schedules, but recent works challenge both components: Schedule-Free optimization removes explicit schedules via iterate averaging, and Muon improves the update geometry by orthogonalizing momentum for matrix parameters. Despite Muon's strong empirical performance, its underlying mechanism remains partially understood. We study Muon through the river-valley loss landscape, where useful training progress occurs along a flat, low-curvature bulk subspace (the river), while high-curvature dominant directions form steep valley walls that induce oscillations. We empirically show that while Muon's orthogonalization accelerates river progress by increasing the bulk component, it also amplifies dominant-direction noise, causing oscillatory trajectories. Building on this, we propose Anytime MUon with Stable gradient Evaluation (AMUSE), which integrates Muon's rapid bulk progress with the stabilizing effect of Schedule-Free averaging. AMUSE uses a time-varying interpolation coefficient that initially evaluates gradients near the fast Muon sequence for rapid adaptation, then gradually shifts toward the stable averaged sequence to suppress valley-wall oscillations. As a result, AMUSE requires no learning rate schedules and supports anytime training. Across vision tasks and large language model pretraining, AMUSE consistently improves the performance-iteration Pareto frontier over (Schedule-Free) AdamW and Muon.

</details>


### [186] [Lost in Tokenization: Fundamental Trade-offs in Graph Tokenization for Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22471)
*Maya Bechler-Speicher,Gilad Yehudai,Gil Harari,Clayton Sanford,Amir Globerson,Joan Bruna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper proves that the choice of graph tokenization is a fundamental determinant of transformer expressivity for graph learning. Different tokenizations (spectral, random-walk, adjacency) require different depth regimes to perform the same computations. The work establishes lower bounds and impossibility results, showing that unfavorable tokenizations can preclude efficient recovery of suitable structural representations. Experiments confirm that different tasks favor different structural views, and combining complementary tokenizations benefits overall performance.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Graph transformers require a graph-to-token mapping (tokenization), but the fundamental impact of this design choice on transformer expressivity has not been systematically studied.

Method: Theoretical analysis (proof-based) of three graph tokenization families (spectral, random-walk, adjacency) via depth regime comparisons; supplemented with controlled experiments on synthetic and real-world tasks.

Result: Different tokenizations induce distinct depth regimes for equivalent computations; random-walk tokenization is inherently lossy; spectral tokenization is ill-conditioned for local tasks; limited-depth transformers cannot convert between tokenization families.

Conclusion: Tokenization choice fundamentally impacts transformer performance; complementary tokenizations enable transformers to leverage distinct structural signals, suggesting hybrid approaches are most effective.

Abstract: Transformers have become a central architecture for graph learning, but their application to graphs requires first choosing a tokenization: a graph-to-token map that determines which structural information is exposed at the input. In this work, we show that this choice is a fundamental component of transformer expressivity. We examine three tokenizations that serve as building blocks for many existing graph tokenizations: spectral, random-walk, and adjacency tokenizations. We prove that different tokenizations induce distinct depth regimes: the same graph computation may be realizable by a shallow transformer under one tokenization, while requiring substantially larger depth under another. For example, we prove that random-walk tokenization is lossy for any walk length, making it impossible in general to recover the graph from it, and that while spectral tokenization is lossless, it is ill-conditioned for local tasks. We further show that although both random-walk and spectral tokenizations are derived from adjacency information, it is impossible for a limited-depth transformer to convert between tokenization families in general. In particular, we establish lower bounds and impossibility results showing that unfavorable tokenizations may preclude the efficient recovery of more suitable structural representations. Finally, we complement our theory with controlled experiments on synthetic and real-world tasks, validating the predicted separations and showing that different tasks favor different structural views, and combining complementary tokenizations allows the transformer to leverage distinct signals from each representation.

</details>


### [187] [Understanding Multimodal Failure in Action-Chunking Behavioral Cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22493)
*Lorenzo Mazza,Massimiliano Datres,Ariel Rodriguez,Sebastian Bodenstedt,Gitta Kutyniok,Stefanie Speidel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper analyzes why different multimodal parameterizations fail differently in behavioral cloning for action-chunking policies. Latent-variable policies suffer from regularization trade-offs (mode information vs sampling reliability), while action-space generative policies are fundamentally limited by transport smoothness. Both findings are validated experimentally.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: When a state admits multiple valid actions (multimodal demonstrations), standard behavioral cloning fails because it cannot capture the multi-valued mapping. Understanding why different multimodal policies fail is critical for designing robust imitation learning systems.

Method: Theoretical analysis comparing latent-variable policies and action-space generative policies under the action-chunking setting, validated on synthetic multimodal tasks and robotic simulation benchmarks.

Result: For latent-variable policies: posterior-prior regularization improves deployment sampling but can excessively remove action-conditioned mode information; prior coverage is crucial. For action-space generative policies: multimodality is bounded by the Lipschitz constant of base-to-action transport—a smooth transport cannot assign probability to many well-separated modes; covering modes requires sharp transitions or off-support bridge regions.

Conclusion: Different multimodal parameterizations have distinct failure modes that require tailored solutions; latent-variable policies need balanced regularization while action-space generative policies require careful base-to-action transport design.

Abstract: Behavioral cloning becomes difficult when the same observation admits several valid actions. We study this problem for action-chunking policies and show that different multimodal parameterizations fail in different ways. For latent-variable policies, posterior-prior regularization makes deployment-time sampling more reliable, but excessive regularization removes the action-conditioned information needed to distinguish demonstrated modes. Reducing this regularization can preserve mode information, but then success depends on whether the prior covers the relevant latent regions.
