For action-space generative policies, multimodality is constrained by the smoothness of the base-to-action transport: a map with small Lipschitz constant cannot assign substantial probability to many well-separated modes. Covering many modes therefore requires either sharp transitions in base space or off-support bridge regions in action space. Experiments on synthetic multimodal tasks and robotic simulation benchmarks support these mechanisms.

</details>


### [188] [The Neural Compiler: Program-to-Network Translation for Hybrid Scientific Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22498)
*Lucas Sheneman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Neural Compiler将符号程序编译为精确可微分的PyTorch模块，以极少量参数（1-4个）精确恢复物理常数，显著优于大规模PINN模型，且具有零误差组合特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学机器学习通常需要将已知物理规律与从未知参数或数据学习的修正项相结合。现有方法存在局限：忽略已知结构、将已知结构编码为软惩罚，或需要为每个方程手写PyTorch代码。

Method: 提出"The Neural Compiler"系统，将一阶Scheme类表达式语言的程序翻译为冻结的、可微分的PyTorch模块。这些模块在浮点精度上与源程序匹配，并通过autograd提供梯度。在混合模型中，编译模块精确编码已知物理规律，而学习组件对未知部分建模。

Result: 在六个实验领域评估了该编译器（费曼物理方程、Lotka-Volterra动力学、阻尼摆、一维热方程、三维向量力学及组合泛化）。编译模块在数值上与手写PyTorch实现匹配，编译无精度损失。仅用1-4个可训练参数，编译模型将物理常数恢复误差控制在1%以下，而具有8500+参数的标准PINN基线误差高达7-93%。编译模块组合误差为零，而神经近似在深层组合链中会累积较大误差。

Conclusion: 该编译器的核心价值在于系统性可组合性，而非超越手工编码的准确性。它能从符号规范自动生成正确、可微分的模块，支持51种原始操作（包括向量和矩阵代数），为大型语言模型将科学描述转换为可执行模块提供了自然接口。

Abstract: Scientific machine learning often requires combining known physics with unknown parameters or correction terms learned from data. Existing approaches either ignore known structure, encode it as a soft penalty, or require hand-written PyTorch code for each equation. We present The Neural Compiler, a system that translates programs written in a first-order Scheme-like expression language into frozen, differentiable PyTorch modules. These modules match the source program to floating-point precision and provide gradients through autograd. In hybrid models, the compiled module encodes known physics exactly while learned components model the unknown remainder. We evaluate the compiler across six experiment domains: Feynman physics equations, Lotka-Volterra dynamics, a damped pendulum, a one-dimensional heat equation, three-dimensional vector mechanics, and compositional generalization. Compiled modules match hand-coded PyTorch implementations numerically for single equations, showing no accuracy loss from compilation. With only 1 to 4 trainable parameters, compiled models recover physical constants to less than 1 percent error in most cases, while standard PINN baselines with more than 8500 parameters show 7 to 93 percent error. Compiled modules also compose with zero error, while neural approximations can accumulate large errors in deep composition chains. The main value of the compiler is not improved accuracy over hand-coded equations, but systematic composability: it generates correct, differentiable modules from symbolic specifications without rewriting each equation by hand. The system supports 51 primitive operations, including vector and matrix algebra, enabling PDE discretizations and hybrid scientific models. This string-in, module-out interface also provides a natural target for large language models that translate scientific descriptions into executable differentiable modules.

</details>


### [189] [The Signal in the Noise: OOD Detection Through Goodness-of-Fit Testing in Factorised Latent Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22496)
*Philipp Bomatter,Jack Geary,Henry Gouk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Deep generative models offer a natural foundation for out-of-distribution (OOD) detection, yet prior work has shown that their assigned likelihoods are notoriously unreliable indicators for in- vs out-of-distribution data. In this paper, we address this problem by leveraging the diffeomorphic and mass-preserving properties of continuous normalising flows. Our analysis shows that OOD samples are mapped to noise samples that are highly atypical under the noise prior in ways not captured by the likelihood. Based on this observation, we propose a new method -- Signal in the Noise (SITN) -- for OOD detection on the single-sample level. SITN requires no access to OOD data, incurs minimal computational overhead, and provides strict control of false positive rates. Comprehensive evaluations through standard benchmarks and synthetic perturbations highlight the method's effectiveness and the absence of the complexity bias inherent to likelihood-based methods.

</details>


### [190] [Stabilising Explainability Fragility in Cybersecurity AI: The Impact and Mitigation of Multicollinearity in Public Benchmark Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22529)
*Ioannis J. Vourganas,Anna Lito Michala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了入侵检测系统中AI可解释性工具（SHAP/LIME）的一个关键漏洞——特征多重共线性导致的归因方差膨胀问题。通过形式化定理证明、UNSW-NB15数据集验证，提出Explanability Fragility Score指标及两种缓解方法（CAA-Filtering和SHARP正则化），为安全关键场景下XAI的可信度和可重复性提供了重要理论支撑和实践指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: A critical yet unexplored vulnerability in AI explainability (XAI) for intrusion detection systems (IDS): correlated features (multicollinearity) significantly impact explanation robustness, rendering explanations and feature importances non-identifiable.

Method: 1) Formal theorem proving multicollinearity inflates attribution variance; 2) Comprehensive experiments on UNSW-NB15 dataset evaluating 4 model families (linear, tree-based, kernel, neural) across full and pruned feature sets (VIF and correlation thresholding); 3) Novel Explanability Fragility Score metric; 4) Two mitigation methods: CAA-Filtering (post-hoc grouping attributions) and SHARP (training-time regularization penalizing attribution instability); 5) Kendall's τ for quantifying instability across bootstrapped explanations.

Result: Multicollinearity inflates attribution variance, and explanations are non-identifiable under correlated features. Both CAA-Filtering and SHARP effectively mitigate this instability with controllable and monotonic improvement. Stable predictive performance is achievable.

Conclusion: This work has direct implications for the trustworthiness and reproducibility of XAI in security-critical contexts, and motivates incorporating multicollinearity mitigations into the IDS pipelines, providing a set of guidelines for practitioners.

Abstract: This paper investigates a unexplored yet impactful vulnerability in AI explainability used in intrusion detection (IDS): multicollinearity-induced instability. Despite extensive reliance on post-hoc explainability tools such as SHAP or LIME, the impact of correlated features on explanation robustness is not evaluated. We introduce a formal theorem stating that multicollinearity inflates attribution variance. This demonstrates that explanations and feature importances are non-identifiable under multicollinearity. A suite of comprehensive experiments validates the theorem on a representative benchmark dataset, UNSW-NB15. Four widely used families of models are evaluated, including linear, tree-based, kernel, and neural, across full and pruned feature sets based on VIF and correlation thresholding. We propose the novel metric of Explanability Fragility Score and two novel methods to mitigate it with variable integration complexity. CAA-Filtering focuses on stabilising explanations by grouping attributions of trained models. SHARP is a novel training-time regularisation framework that penalises attribution instability, enabling controllable and monotonic improvement of explainability stability. The findings support stable predictive performance, using Kendall's τ to quantify instability across bootstrapped explanations. This work has direct implications for the trustworthiness and reproducibility of XAI in security-critical contexts, and motivates incorporating multicollinearity mitigations into the IDS pipelines, providing a set of guidelines for practitioners.

</details>


### [191] [Generative Modeling by Value-Driven Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22507)
*Pablo Moreno-Muñoz,Adrian Müller,Gergely Neu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于离散时间随机控制 formulations 的生成模型新框架，通过线性规划和对偶理论导出价值驱动传输(VDT)策略，具有直线传输路径、快速模拟、支持多种增强功能的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型方法（如基于流、扩散或薛定谔桥的方法）在传输路径模拟效率和鲁棒性方面存在不足。

Method: 将问题构建为线性规划，利用其对偶变量对应最优价值函数的最优控制策略，开发了高效的无模拟原对偶算法来计算近似最优价值函数和价值驱动传输(VDT)策略。

Result: VDT策略具有直线传输路径、可快速鲁棒模拟的特点，同时支持条件生成、无分类器引导、非配对数据到数据翻译等增强功能，性能表现优异。

Conclusion: VDT方法具有直线传输路径、可快速鲁棒模拟的优势，并可与扩散和基于流的模型的所有增强功能（条件生成、无分类器引导、非配对数据到数据翻译）相媲美，展现出良好的可扩展性潜力。

Abstract: We propose a new framework for generative modeling based on a discrete-time stochastic control formulation of measure transport. Adapting classic results from control theory, we formulate our problem as a linear program whose dual variables correspond to the \emph{optimal value function} of the control problem, which directly encodes the optimal control policy. Exploiting this LP formulation, we develop an efficient simulation-free primal-dual algorithm for computing approximately optimal value functions and the associated \emph{value-driven transport} (VDT) policies which approximate the true optimal policy. We show that well-trained VDT policies enjoy numerous favorable properties in comparison with other state-of-the-art methods based on flows, diffusions, or Schrödinger bridges: they lead to straight transport paths which can be simulated quickly and robustly, and can be enhanced in all the same ways as diffusion and flow-based models (e.g., conditional generation, classifier-free guidance, unpaired data-to-data translation are all easy to incorporate). We evaluate our methodology in a range of experiments, with results that indicate strong performance and good potential for scalability.

</details>


### [192] [MoSA: Motion-constrained Stress Adaptation for Mitigating Real-to-Sim Gap in Continuum Dynamics via Learning Residual Anisotropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22597)
*Jiaxu Wang,Junhao He,Jingkai Sun,Yi Gu,Yunyang Mo,Jiahang Cao,Qiang Zhang,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MoSA is a motion-constrained stress adaptation framework that bridges the real-to-sim gap by combining isotropic physics priors with learned residual stress operators, validated on robot manipulation for sim-to-real transfer.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Real-world objects exhibit mild anisotropy and heterogeneity, but existing physics simulators assume homogeneous and isotropic materials. After calibrating the near-isotropic backbone, these residual effects become the key bottleneck for further closing the real-to-sim gap. Pure neural network approaches discard strong physical priors, leading to poor data efficiency and overfitting.

Method: MoSA uses an isotropic model as physics prior and learns residual stress operators to capture mild anisotropy and heterogeneity. It progressively adapts stresses via microplane-constrained redistribution in a physics-informed cascaded network, with motion constraints supervising temporal and spatial derivatives of the deformation field.

Result: The learned dynamics achieves superior accuracy, generalization, and robustness compared to baselines, while learning physically meaningful residual anisotropy patterns.

Conclusion: Better real-to-sim dynamics modeling through MoSA translates into more reliable sim-to-real transfer in robot manipulation tasks, demonstrating practical applicability of the proposed approach.

Abstract: Learning real-world dynamics from visual observations is crucial for various domains. A common strategy is to calibrate simulators by estimating physical parameters, yet accuracy is ultimately bounded by the underlying physical models, which often assume materials are homogeneous and isotropic. Even if reasonable, real-world objects typically exhibit mild anisotropy and heterogeneity. After the near-isotropic backbone is well calibrated, these residual effects become the key bottleneck for further closing the real-to-sim gap. Although neural networks can fit dynamics end-to-end, such black-box modeling discards strong physical priors, leading to poor data efficiency and overfitting. Therefore, we propose MoSA, a motion-constrained stress adaptation framework that targets these residual effects to further improve real-to-sim dynamics learning. MoSA uses an isotropic model as a physics prior and learns residual stress operators to capture mild anisotropy and heterogeneity. It progressively adapts stresses via microplane-constrained redistribution in a physics-informed cascaded network. We further impose motion constraints by supervising temporal and spatial derivatives of the deformation field. Experimentally, our learned dynamics achieves superior accuracy, generalization, and robustness, while learning physically meaningful residual anisotropy. Finally, we validate MoSA in a robot manipulation setting, showing that better real-to-sim dynamics modeling translates into more reliable sim-to-real transfer. Project Page is available at https://mercerai.github.io/MoSA/.

</details>


### [193] [Disentanglement Beyond Generative Models with Riemannian ICA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22531)
*Edmond Cunningham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Riemannian ICA (RICA) bridges theory and practice of disentanglement by replacing global generative assumptions with local geometric structure based on Riemannian geometry. The novel disentanglement tensor captures pointwise disentanglement via Hessian and Ricci curvature. RICA demonstrates robust source recovery across manifolds independent of coordinate choice.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: A significant gap exists between theoretical foundations of disentanglement (ICA frameworks) and modern representation learning practice. Existing generative frameworks have strong assumptions limiting applicability to pretrained encoders that learn disentangled features without explicit generative models.

Method: Riemannian ICA replaces ICA's global generative model with local geometric structure using Riemannian geometry. The key contribution is the disentanglement tensor, which encodes pointwise disentanglement and depends on the Hessian of data log-likelihood and Ricci curvature induced by the model. Factors of variation are understood through radial curves mapping to axis-aligned lines in latent space.

Result: In controlled source recovery experiments with known ground-truth sources, RICA successfully recovers sources across multiple manifolds, while traditional ICA baseline success depends on coordinate representation of observations.

Conclusion: RICA provides a theoretical basis for studying local disentanglement without assuming a global generative model, applicable across various manifolds with coordinate-independent recovery.

Abstract: There is a gap between the theoretical foundations of disentanglement and the practice of modern representation learning. Existing theoretical frameworks, particularly Independent Component Analysis (ICA) and its nonlinear variants, assume a generative model with statistically independent latent variables underlying the data so that disentanglement amounts to identifying the latents that could have generated the data. This generative framework is interpretable and theoretically justified, but its strong assumptions make it difficult to apply to modern representation learning. Modern pretrained encoders often learn features that exhibit disentangled properties without making generative assumptions, yet there is no general theory for interpreting these features as independent factors of variation. We take a step toward such a theory by introducing Riemannian ICA (RICA), which replaces ICA's global generative model with local geometric structure. RICA is founded on the observation that in ICA, the factors of variation underlying a data point can be understood through radial curves emanating from the point that map to axis-aligned lines in the latent space. We formalize this perspective using Riemannian geometry and introduce our theory in a way that is consistent with the existing generative approach. Our main contribution is the disentanglement tensor, which encodes a second-order notion of disentanglement that we call pointwise disentanglement. This tensor depends on the Hessian of the data log likelihood as well as the Ricci curvature induced by the model. In a controlled source recovery setting with known ground-truth sources, RICA recovers sources across several manifolds, while the success of ICA baselines depends on the coordinates used to represent the observations. Our work provides a theoretical basis for studying local disentanglement without assuming a global generative model.

</details>


### [194] [Relational Linear Properties in Language Models: An Empirical Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22532)
*Giovanni Valer,Luigi Gresele,Marco Bronzini,Emanuele Marconato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper introduces a computationally efficient KL-divergence-based probing method to test relational linearity in language models. Validation across four datasets reveals that such linearity varies by model, exhibits predictable layer-wise patterns, and is sensitive to paraphrasing variations.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Linear properties are ubiquitous in language model representations, but experimentally testing these properties remains challenging. Specifically, the hypothesis of relational linearity—that an object's unembedding can be predicted from its subject's embedding via a linear map—needed a rigorous experimental validation method.

Method: The authors propose a KL-divergence-based probing method to test relational linearity formulated by Marconato et al. (2025). This approach is computationally more efficient than prior methods, circumventing the crude Jacobian approximations used in earlier work.

Result: Experiments across four datasets demonstrate that relational linearity varies across models, shows layer-wise patterns aligning with known linguistic information distribution in model representations, and is differentially impacted by paraphrasing variations.

Conclusion: The findings reveal that relational linearity: (1) varies across different language models, (2) exhibits layer-wise patterns consistent with prior linguistic information localization research, and (3) is differentially affected by paraphrasing variations of relational queries.

Abstract: Linear properties are ubiquitous in the representations of language models; however, testing them experimentally remains a challenging task. This work focuses on relational linearity: the hypothesis that, for a fixed relation (e.g., "plays"), the unembedding of an object (e.g., "trumpet") can be predicted from the embedding of its subject (e.g.,"Miles Davis") by a linear map. We present an experimental method to test the formulation of relational linearity by Marconato et al. (2025). Specifically, we introduce a probing method, based on Kullback-Leibler divergence, to evaluate this property and examine its variation across layers and paraphrased relational queries. It is also more efficient than previous work; for example, it avoids the crude Jacobian approximations used in Linear Relational Embeddings by Hernandez et al. (2024). Our findings across four datasets show that relational linearity varies across models, exhibits layer-wise patterns consistent with prior observations about linguistic information in model representations, and is differently affected by changes in how the relation is phrased.

</details>


### [195] [F-TIS: Harnessing Diverse Models in Collaborative GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22537)
*Nikolay Blagoev,Oğuzhan Ersoy,Wendelin Boehmer,Lydia Yiyu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对去中心化系统中异构模型协作训练的问题，提出了F-TIS框架，通过过滤截断重要性采样使off-policy样本可用于训练，在保持通信效率的同时实现了与on-policy训练相当的收敛性，并可能在泛化能力上取得优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式推理方案假设训练中模型主要是同质的，以保持样本接近on-policy。然而在去中心化系统中，不同计算资源和偏好的参与方希望协作训练，这需要处理异构模型，导致高度off-policy的样本，而prior work已表明off-policy样本会损害GRPO收敛。

Method: 提出Filtered Truncated Importance Sampling (F-TIS)，一种GRPO风格的训练范式，通过过滤截断重要性采样来处理off-policy样本，实现异构模型协作，同时保持通信效率。

Result: 在各种异构设置下验证，F-TIS展现出与纯on-policy训练相同的最终模型收敛性；在某些设置下，对分布外任务展现出更好的泛化能力，性能提升可达12%。

Conclusion: F-TIS在异构环境中展现了与纯on-policy训练相当的最终收敛性，并在某些设置下展现出更好的泛化能力，性能提升高达12%。该框架为去中心化协作学习提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning methods such as GRPO have seen great popularity in LLM post-training. In GRPO, models produce completions to a set of prompts, which are rewarded, and the policy is updated towards the relatively high reward completions. Due to the auto-regressive nature of models, the generation phase of such style of training can be extremely time consuming. As a solution, prior work has sought to distribute the inference step across many nodes, working parallel. These works assume primarily homogeneous models in the training in order to keep samples as close to on-policy as possible. This assumption may be impractical in decentralized systems, where parties with various computes and preferences may wish to collaborate on the same task. Thus, decentralized training requires an approach that can handle heterogeneous models - different models collaborating on the same tasks. However, this leads to highly off-policy samples presented during training, which prior work has identified that off-policy samples can hurt GRPO convergence. To enable heterogeneity, we propose Filtered Truncated Importance Sampling (F-TIS) - a GRPO-style training paradigm that can use off-policy samples to improve local model's learning. Our framework allows various models to collaborate in the same RL training run while being communication efficient. We extensively evaluate F-TIS in various heterogeneous setups and we show that it exhibits identical final model convergence to purely on-sample training. Furthermore, we observe in some setups better generalization on out-of-distribution tasks than on-policy training, increasing model's performance by up to 12\%.

</details>


### [196] [ImplicitTerrainV2: Wavelet-Guided Spatially Adaptive Neural Terrain Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22556)
*Haoan Feng,Xin Xu,Leila De Floriani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ImplicitTerrainV2 proposes a compact neural terrain representation combining wavelet-based spatial adaptivity, derivative-aware training, and model compression to achieve high accuracy (66.25 dB PSNR) with 3.2x parameter reduction, supporting continuous off-grid queries and derivative evaluation for GIS applications.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current DEM raster formats have limitations: rely on interpolation for off-grid sampling, finite-difference operators for derivatives; existing INRs lack frequency control, neglect gradient structure, and are too large/costly for practical deployment.

Method: Wavelet complexity field (WCF) for spatial-adaptive frequency masks, complexity-aware adaptive sampling, gradient matching for derivative supervision, post-training quantization and entropy coding (1.23 bpp)

Result: On 50 Swiss terrain tiles: 66.25 dB PSNR (5.70 dB improvement over prior work), 3.2x fewer parameters, 55s training per tile on single GPU; compressed to 1.23 bpp with 0.28 dB PSNR drop; competitive rate-distortion with traditional DEM codecs.

Conclusion: ImplicitTerrainV2 is a compact, efficient neural terrain data format competitive with traditional DEM codecs while offering continuous querying, derivative evaluation, and resolution-independent reconstruction for GIS applications.

Abstract: Digital elevation models (DEMs) underpin terrain analysis in Geographic Information Systems (GIS), but in their common raster form, they rely on interpolation for off-grid sampling and finite-difference operators for derivative-based analysis. Implicit neural representations (INRs) offer a continuous alternative, but prior terrain INRs lack explicit frequency control, neglect the gradient structure of terrain, and remain too large and costly to train for practical deployment. We present ImplicitTerrainV2, which advances terrain INRs toward a compact, efficient neural terrain data format by combining a spectral control mechanism with wavelet-guided spatial adaptivity, derivative-aware supervision, and post-training model compression. At its core, a wavelet complexity field (WCF) derives spatially-adaptive frequency masks from analytically computed wavelet coefficients, localizing high-frequency capacity to complex terrain regions. The same field guides complexity-aware adaptive sampling that concentrates training in high-complexity regions, while gradient matching applies extra supervision to enforce the smooth manifold structure of terrain DEMs for improved derivative fidelity. Post-training mixed-precision quantization and entropy coding reduce storage to 1.23 bpp with a 0.28 dB PSNR drop. On 50 Swiss terrain tiles, ImplicitTerrainV2 reaches 66.25 dB end-to-end PSNR, improving over the prior work by 5.70 dB while using 3.2x fewer parameters and training in 55 s per tile on a single GPU. Our compressed neural format is competitive with several established DEM codecs in rate-distortion performance, while additionally supporting off-grid point queries, closed-form derivative evaluation, and resolution-independent reconstruction, which may benefit many downstream GIS applications.

</details>


### [197] [Regret-Based $(ε,δ)$-optimal Stopping Criteria for Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22561)
*Haowei Wang,Jingyi Wang,Qiyu Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为GP-UCB提供了更紧的瞬时遗憾界，基于此提出了具有理论保证的停止准则，确保贝叶斯优化以高概率获得ε最优解并避免不必要的评估开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯优化在实际应用中通常采用固定评估预算终止，导致不必要的计算成本且无法保证解的质量最优性。现有停止准则缺乏理论支撑。

Method: 本文提出了针对GP-UCB在任意迭代时刻的理论上界更紧的瞬时遗憾界，并基于该紧界设计停止准则，确保终止时以高概率1-δ获得ε最优解。

Result: 所提停止准则能够以高概率1-δ保证获得ε最优解。

Conclusion: 数值实验验证了所提停止准则的有效性和效率。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization (BO) is a widely used iterative black-box optimization method that utilizes Gaussian process (GP) surrogate models. In practice, BO is typically terminated after a fixed evaluation budget is exhausted, which can incur unnecessary cost and provides no optimality guarantee on solution quality. Recent research in developing a practical stopping criterion has made empirical progress, yet a theoretically sound stopping criterion remains a work in progress. In this work, we present provably tighter instantaneous regret bounds for GP upper confidence bound (GP-UCB) at any given iteration. Then, we propose stopping criteria for GP-UCB based on this tighter bound that ensures an $ε$-optimal solution with high probability $1-δ$ upon termination. Numerical experiments are performed to validate and demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of our stopping criteria.

</details>


### [198] [Abstraction for Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22711)
*Clarisse Wibault,Alexander Goldie,Antonio Villares,Maike Osborne,Jakob Foerster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出相对化选项框架，通过层次化表示实现状态空间中的经验复用，解决离线目标条件强化学习中MDP结构冗余问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界GCRL中，MDP存在由对称性和状态-目标对共享结构导致的显著冗余；现有层次策略仅被用于通过时间抽象缩短规划 horizon，未能充分挖掘层次结构的抽象潜力。

Method: 引入相对化选项（relativised options）和层次化表示框架，使智能体能在状态空间的相似上下文中复用经验。

Result: 实验证明，所提归纳偏置显著提升了离线GCRL性能。

Conclusion: 两个简单算法有效证明了相对化选项和层次表示的可行性，为离线GCRL提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) often exhibit significant redundancy due to symmetries and shared structure across state-goal pairs in real-world Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning (GCRL). While hierarchical policies have been motivated for horizon reduction via temporal abstraction in offline GCRL, we demonstrate that hierarchy also enables absolute abstraction. By introducing relativised options as well as distinct representations for different levels of the hierarchy, we demonstrate how an agent can reuse experience across similar contexts of the state-space. Based on this framework, we introduce two simple algorithms for learning relativised options and abstracting from the absolute frame of reference. Our experiments show that such inductive biases significantly improve performance in offline GCRL.

</details>


### [199] [The Value of Covariance Matching in Gaussian DDPMs and the Lanczos Sampler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22723)
*Md Sahil Akhtar,Aymane El Gadarri,Vivek F. Farias,Adam D. Jozefiak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明匹配完整后验协方差可突破Gaussian DDPM中路径-KL误差的Ω(1/T)下界，将其降至O(1/T²)；并提出实用化方案LGS采样器，以指数级效率逼近最优逆向协方差，仅需少量Jacobian-向量积即可显著提升采样质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准各向同性逆向协方差存在不可避免的Ω(1/T)路径-KL误差，当扩散步数T增大时误差无法降低。该误差对分类器引导等扰动整个逆向轨迹的过程影响尤为严重

Method: 提出Lanczos Gaussian采样器(LGS)，一种无需训练、无需存储稠密协方差矩阵的方法，利用Lanczos算法从最优逆向协方差中采样，仅需协方差-向量积（通过后验均值Jacobian-向量积获得）。LGS的核心是Lanczos步骤，每步仅需一次Jacobian-向量积

Result: 理论与实验双重验证：(1) 理论上证明全后验协方差匹配将路径-KL误差从O(1/T)降低至O(1/T²)，实现量级改进；(2) LGS的近似误差随Lanczos步数指数衰减；(3) 仅用3步Lanczos迭代即可在标准图像基准上超越OCM-DDPM等强对角协方差基线方法

Conclusion: LGS方法在仅使用三个Lanczos步骤的情况下即可在标准图像基准上提升样本质量，验证了全协方差匹配的实用价值

Abstract: A central error measure in Gaussian DDPMs is the path-space KL divergence between the exact reverse chain and the learned Gaussian reverse process. This quantity is especially relevant for procedures such as classifier guidance, which perturb the entire reverse trajectory rather than only the terminal sample. Prior analyses show that standard isotropic reverse covariances suffer an unavoidable $Ω(1/T)$ path-KL error as the number of denoising steps $T$ grows. We show that matching the full posterior covariance breaks this barrier, yielding an order-wise improvement that reduces the path KL to $O(1/T^2)$. To make full covariance matching practical, we introduce the Lanczos Gaussian sampler (LGS), a training-free, matrix-free method for sampling from the optimal reverse covariance using only covariance-vector products, which are available through Jacobian-vector products of the posterior mean. LGS avoids dense covariance storage and auxiliary covariance models. We prove that LGS approximation error decays exponentially in the number of Lanczos steps, where each Lanczos step requires a single Jacobian-vector product. Empirically, using only just three such steps improves sample quality over strong diagonal-covariance baselines, including OCM-DDPM, across standard image benchmarks. This identifies full covariance matching as both theoretically valuable and practically accessible for fast DDPM sampling.

</details>


### [200] [Proxy-Based Approximation of Shapley and Banzhaf Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22738)
*Santo M. A. R. Thies,Hubert Baniecki,R. Teal Witter,Eyke Hüllermeier,Maximilian Muschalik,Fabian Fumagalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProxySHAP introduces a novel framework for estimating Shapley and Banzhaf interactions that achieves exact computation for tree ensembles in polynomial time (via interventional TreeSHAP generalization) while maintaining high sample efficiency through residual correction. It establishes new state-of-the-art accuracy across all computational budgets, outperforming ProxySPEX and KernelSHAP-IQ.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current estimators for higher-order Shapley and Banzhaf interactions face a trade-off between speed and accuracy, limiting their practical applicability in complex machine learning systems.

Method: ProxySHAP combines high sample efficiency of tree-based proxy models with a residual correction strategy. The paper derives a polynomial-time generalization of interventional TreeSHAP for exact interaction indices on tree ensembles, bypassing exponential tree-depth dependencies. The residual adjustment strategy is formally analyzed to characterize when Maximum Sample Reuse (MSR) corrects proxy bias without exponential variance scaling.

Result: ProxySHAP significantly outperforms prior best estimators (ProxySPEX and KernelSHAP-IQ) in both small- and large-budget regimes, achieving the lowest approximation error. Superior performance is also demonstrated on downstream explainability tasks.

Conclusion: ProxySHAP sets a new state-of-the-art for interaction estimation, achieving the lowest error across all budget regimes while maintaining practical applicability to large-scale problems.

Abstract: Shapley and Banzhaf interactions capture the complex dynamics inherent in modern machine learning applications. However, current estimators for these higher-order interactions trade off between speed and accuracy. To overcome this limitation, we introduce ProxySHAP. ProxySHAP reconciles the high sample efficiency of tree-based proxy models with a principled path to consistency via residual correction. On a theoretical level, we derive a polynomial-time generalization of interventional TreeSHAP to compute exact interaction indices for tree ensembles, successfully bypassing exponential tree-depth dependencies in prior methods. Furthermore, we formally analyze the residual adjustment strategy, characterizing the specific conditions under which Maximum Sample Reuse (MSR) corrects proxy bias without its variance scaling exponentially with interaction size. Extensive benchmarking demonstrates that ProxySHAP sets a new state-of-the-art standard for approximation quality, including in large-scale applications with thousands of features. By achieving the lowest error in both small- and large-budget regimes, ProxySHAP significantly outperforms the prior best estimators ProxySPEX and KernelSHAP-IQ, while also delivering superior performance on downstream explainability tasks.

</details>


### [201] [Evolutionary Multi-Task Optimization for LLM-Guided Program Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22613)
*Halil Alperen Gozeten,Xuechen Zhang,Emrullah Ildiz,Ege Onur Taga,Tara Javidi,Samet Oymak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EMO-STA框架，通过两阶段（共享进化+任务适应）将多任务优化引入LLM引导的程序发现，在8个任务家族上验证了其在计算效率和泛化能力上的优势，特别是在低证据场景下可有效缓解过拟合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM引导的进化搜索方法虽能发现强算法，但通常独立优化每个任务，未能利用相关任务间的可复用结构，导致计算效率低下且泛化能力不足。

Method: 提出EMO-STA框架，包含两个阶段：(1)Shared阶段：在任务家族层面共享进化程序存档；(2)Adaptation阶段：探索多种适应策略——Warm-Start、STA Best-Average、STA Best-Local、STA Best-Shared。在8个任务家族上验证，覆盖连续优化、几何构造、建模和算法优化领域。

Result: 在大多数设置中EMO-STA优于匹配计算的单任务进化；STA Best-Local提供最强的分布内适应性能，STA Best-Shared对未见任务具有稳健迁移能力；共享进化在低证据场景（如ARC任务、时间序列特征工程）中可缓解过拟合。

Conclusion: EMO-STA通过两阶段框架在多任务场景下实现高效的程序进化，在8个任务家族上优于单任务进化，且共享进化有助于缓解低证据场景下的过拟合问题。

Abstract: Recent LLM-guided evolutionary search methods have shown that iterative program mutation can discover strong algorithms, but they typically optimize each task independently, even when related tasks share reusable structure. We introduce Evolutionary Multi-Task Optimization (EMO) for LLM-guided program discovery, and propose EMO-STA (Shared-Then-Adapt), a two-stage framework that first evolves a shared archive of executable programs across a task family and then adapts selected shared candidates to each target task. Within EMO-STA, we explore multiple adaptation strategies, including warm-starting from the shared archive, adapting the best average shared program, and adapting the shared program that performs best on each target task. Across eight task families spanning continuous optimization, geometric construction, modeling, and algorithmic optimization, EMO-STA improves over matched-compute single-task evolution in most settings, with STA Best-Local providing the strongest in-distribution adaptation and STA Best-Shared yielding robust transfer to unseen tasks. Compute-allocation experiments show that allocating a substantial fraction of the family-level budget to shared evolution is consistently beneficial, with roughly balanced shared and adaptation budgets often being optimal. Beyond compute efficiency, we show that shared evolution can mitigate overfitting in low-evidence settings (e.g. few training data), including ARC tasks and time-series feature engineering, by favoring programs that generalize across all tasks rather than exploiting task-specific brittle artifacts.

</details>


### [202] [CogAdapt: Transferring Clinical ECG Foundation Models to Wearable Cognitive Load Assessment via Lead Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22774)
*Amir Mousavi,Mohammad Sadegh Sirjani,Erfan Nourbakhsh,Mimi Xie,Rocky Slavin,Leslie Neely,John Davis,John Quarles*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CogAdapt框架，通过LeadBridge适配器和ProFine渐进式微调策略，将临床ECG基础模型成功迁移至可穿戴设备认知负荷评估任务，解决了传感器配置不匹配和跨被试泛化的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时认知负荷评估对于自适应人机交互至关重要，但面临标注数据有限和跨被试泛化能力差等挑战。临床ECG基础模型虽在大规模临床数据上预训练表现良好，但由于传感器配置不匹配和任务差异，无法直接应用于可穿戴设备。

Method: 该方法包含两个核心组件：1) LeadBridge，一个可学习的适配器，将3导联可穿戴信号转换为解剖学上一致的12导联表示；2) ProFine，一种渐进式微调策略，逐步解冻编码器层同时防止灾难性遗忘。在CLARE和CL-Drive两个公共数据集上采用留一被试交叉验证进行评估。

Result: CogAdapt在两个数据集上均显著超越从头训练的基线模型，分别达到0.626和0.768的宏F1分数。

Conclusion: CogAdapt通过将临床ECG基础模型适配到可穿戴设备，实现了对认知负荷的跨被试评估，在CLARE和CL-Drive数据集上分别取得了0.626和0.768的宏F1分数，显著优于从头训练的基线模型，证明了基础模型适配在可穿戴传感器认知负荷评估中的潜力。

Abstract: Real-time cognitive load assessment is essential for adaptive human-computer interaction but remains challenging due to limited labeled data and poor cross-subject generalization. Recent ECG foundation models pre-trained on millions of clinical recordings offer rich representations, but cannot be directly applied to wearable devices due to sensor configuration mismatch and task differences. In this paper, we propose CogAdapt, a framework that adapts clinical ECG foundation models to wearable cognitive load assessment. CogAdapt introduces LeadBridge, a learnable adapter that transforms 3-lead wearable signals into anatomically consistent 12-lead representations, and ProFine, a progressive fine-tuning strategy that gradually unfreezes encoder layers while preventing catastrophic forgetting. Evaluations on two public datasets (CLARE and CL-Drive) under leave-one-subject-out cross-validation show that CogAdapt substantially outperforms baselines trained from scratch, achieving macro-F1 scores of 0.626 and 0.768. These results demonstrate the promise of foundation model adaptation for subject-independent cognitive load assessment from wearable sensors.

</details>


### [203] [A note on convergence of Wasserstein policy optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22622)
*David Šiška,Yufei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper provides theoretical convergence guarantees for Wasserstein Policy Optimization (WPO) in entropy-regularized Markov Decision Processes, demonstrating that WPO achieves linear convergence to the global optimum. The analysis relies on mean-field techniques and log-Sobolev inequalities, establishing monotonic energy dissipation and a local log-Sobolev inequality along the gradient flow.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The theoretical convergence properties of Wasserstein Policy Optimization in continuous action spaces had not been fully established, despite its empirical success

Method: Uses log-Sobolev inequalities and mean-field analysis for gradient flow convergence; establishes monotonic energy dissipation along the flow and local log-Sobolev inequality under the assumption of sufficiently regular solution to the gradient flow equation

Result: Linear convergence of WPO to the global optimum in entropy-regularized MDPs

Conclusion: WPO converges linearly to the global optimum of the value function

Abstract: Wasserstein Policy Optimization (WPO) is a recently proposed reinforcement learning algorithm that leverages Wasserstein gradient flows to optimize stochastic policies in continuous action spaces. Despite its empirical success, the theoretical convergence properties of WPO in environments with continuous state and action spaces have yet to be fully established. In this note, we argue that WPO within the framework of entropy-regularised Markov Decision Processes converges linearly. This is done by leveraging recent advances in mean-field analysis for convergence of gradient flows using log-Sobole inequalities. Assuming existence of sufficiently regular solution to the gradient flow equation we demonstrate monotonic energy dissipation along the flow and establish a local log-Sobolev inequality. Ultimately, these properties allow us to argue that the value function should converge linearly to the global optimum.

</details>


### [204] [MambaGaze: Bidirectional Mamba with Explicit Missing Data Modeling for Cognitive Load Assessment from Eye-Gaze Tracking Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22775)
*Amir Mousavi,Mohammad Sadegh Sirjani,Erfan Nourbakhsh,Mimi Xie,Rocky Slavin,Leslie Neely,John Davis,John Quarles*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MambaGaze是一个用于眼动信号实时认知负荷评估的深度学习框架，通过XMD编码处理数据缺失问题，结合双向Mamba-2架构高效建模时间依赖，在两个数据集上显著优于CNN、Transformer等基线方法，并具备边缘设备实时部署能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时认知负荷评估在安全关键应用中具有重要价值，但面临两个核心挑战：1) 眨眼和追踪失败导致的数据频繁缺失；2) 长程时间依赖的高效建模。

Method: MambaGaze采用双模块设计：1) XMD编码，通过观察掩码和时间差分增强原始特征以显式建模数据不确定性；2) 双向Mamba-2架构，以线性计算复杂度捕获长程时间依赖关系。

Result: 在CLARE和CL-Drive数据集的留一被试评估中，MambaGaze分别达到76.8%和73.1%的准确率，较CNN、Transformer、ResNet和VGG基线模型提升4-12个百分点。NVIDIA Jetson平台边缘部署测试实现43-68 FPS推理速度，功耗低于7.5W。

Conclusion: MambaGaze框架通过XMD编码和双向Mamba-2架构，在认知负荷评估任务上实现了最优性能，同时满足边缘部署的实时性要求，为可穿戴认知负荷监测应用提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: Real-time cognitive load assessment from eye-tracking signals could potentially enable adaptive human-centered-AI such as safety-critical applications such as driver vigilance monitoring or automated flight deck assistance, yet two challenges persist: handling frequent data missingness from blinks and tracking failures, and efficiently modeling long-range temporal dependencies. We propose MambaGaze, a framework that addresses these challenges through 1) XMD encoding, which augments raw features with observation masks and time-deltas to explicitly model data uncertainty, and 2) bidirectional Mamba-2, which captures temporal dependencies with linear computational complexity. Experiments on CLARE and CL-Drive datasets under leave-one-subject-out evaluation show that MambaGaze achieves 76.8% and 73.1% accuracy, respectively, outperforming CNN, Transformer, ResNet, and VGG baselines by 4-12 percentage points. Edge deployment benchmarks on NVIDIA Jetson platforms demonstrate real-time inference at 43-68 FPS with power consumption below 7.5W, confirming feasibility for wearable cognitive load monitoring.

</details>


### [205] [SDPM: Survival Diffusion Probabilistic Model for Continuous-Time Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22776)
*Stanislav R. Kirpichenko,Andrei V. Konstantinov,Lev V. Utkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper proposes SDPM, a diffusion probabilistic model for continuous-time survival analysis that avoids parametric assumptions and time discretization. It achieves competitive performance on multiple benchmarks and accurately recovers underlying survival distributions.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional survival analysis methods either impose structural assumptions on the hazard function or discretize the time axis, which limits flexibility and introduces approximation errors. There is a need for methods that avoid parametric assumptions and don't require time discretization.

Method: SDPM employs a denoising diffusion model to learn the conditional distribution P(T,δ|x) for survival outcomes. The model operates in a transformed target space using standardized log-times and continuous Gaussian-mixture representation of censoring indicators. Generated samples are converted to survival function estimates via Kaplan-Meier estimator under conditionally independent censoring assumption.

Result: SDPM achieves competitive predictive performance across C-index, integrated time-dependent AUC, and integrated Brier score on 10 real survival datasets compared to 5 strong baselines. On synthetic Cox-Weibull data, it recovers continuous survival distributions more accurately than nonparametric baselines. Ablation studies confirm that the proposed target-space transformations improve event-rate calibration, reduce invalid generated times, and provide consistent gains in predictive discrimination.

Conclusion: SDPM provides a flexible generative approach for continuous-time survival analysis, with target-space transformations being crucial for good performance. The method offers a promising alternative to traditional and neural survival models, particularly when accurate recovery of continuous survival distributions is needed.

Abstract: Survival analysis aims to estimate a time-to-event distribution from data with censored observations. Many existing methods either impose structural assumptions on the hazard function or discretize the time axis, which may limit flexibility and introduce approximation errors. We propose the Survival Diffusion Probabilistic Model (SDPM), a generative approach to continuous-time survival analysis. SDPM models the conditional distribution of the survival outcome, represented by the pair of observed time and censoring indicator, $\mathbb{P}(T,δ\mid \mathbf{x})$, using a denoising diffusion model. Under the assumption of conditionally independent censoring, conditional samples generated by the model can be transformed into survival function estimates using the Kaplan-Meier estimator. This formulation avoids parametric assumptions on the event-time distribution and does not require a discretization of the output time space. The model operates in a transformed target space, using standardized log-times and a continuous Gaussian-mixture representation of the censoring indicator. We evaluate SDPM on ten real survival datasets and compare it with five strong baselines, including tree-based, boosting-based, and neural survival models. Results show that SDPM achieves competitive predictive performance across C-index, integrated time-dependent AUC, and integrated Brier score. A study on synthetic Cox-Weibull data demonstrates that SDPM can recover the shape of an underlying continuous survival distribution more accurately than a strong nonparametric baseline when sufficiently many samples are generated. An ablation study confirms the importance of the proposed target-space transformations, which improve event-rate calibration, reduce invalid generated times, and provide consistent gains in predictive discrimination. Codes implementing the proposed model are publicly available.

</details>


### [206] [The Matching Principle: A Geometric Theory of Loss Functions for Nuisance-Robust Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22800)
*Vishal Rajput*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper unifies six robustness problems (domain adaptation, invariance, alignment safety, etc.) under one statistical framework by identifying "deployment nuisance covariance" estimation as the core task, then proves the "matching principle" is optimal in linear-Gaussian settings, and validates the theory at 7B scale with 12/13 pre-registered experiments passing.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Six robustness problems (domain adaptation, photometric/occlusion invariance, compositional generalization, temporal robustness, alignment safety, anisotropic regularization) are traditionally treated separately. The paper identifies their shared structure as one statistical problem to achieve unified understanding.

Method: Unified framework: estimate covariance of label-preserving deployment nuisance, then regularize encoder Jacobian along matched covariance (matching principle). Applied to linear-Gaussian model with cube-root water-filling, TDI metric, and style-PMH at 7B scale (Qwen2.5-7B).

Result: Theorem A proves closed-form optimality with cube-root water-filling; Theorem G shows necessity of range coverage for quadratic Jacobian penalties; same range dichotomy at deep global minima. Two falsification controls (Lemma C; Corollaries E) with seven conditional consistency lemmas (D1-D7). 12 out of 13 pre-registered experiments pass.

Conclusion: Authors introduce TDI as a label-free probe and validate the matched-isotropic-wrong ordering hypothesis through 13 pre-registered blocks, with 12 passing. At 7B scale, matched style-PMH improves selective honesty while preserving Style TDI. The paper's contribution is framing deployment nuisance covariance estimation as a unified problem and providing a closed-form falsifiable theory.

Abstract: Robustness, domain adaptation, photometric and occlusion invariance, compositional generalisation, temporal robustness, alignment safety, and classical anisotropic regularisation are usually treated as separate problems with separate method families. This paper argues that much of their shared structure is one statistical problem: estimate the covariance of label-preserving deployment nuisance, then regularise the encoder Jacobian along a matrix whose range covers that covariance (the matching principle). CORAL, adversarial training, IRM, augmentation, metric learning, Jacobian penalties, and alignment-style constraints are different estimators of that object, not independent robustness tricks.
  In the linear-Gaussian model we prove closed-form optimality (Theorem A), including cube-root water-filling within the matched range; necessity of range coverage for quadratic Jacobian penalties (Theorem G); the same range dichotomy at deep global minima; and two falsification controls (Lemma C; Corollaries E), with seven conditional consistency lemmas (D1-D7) for estimation under standard identifiability assumptions.
  We introduce the Trajectory Deviation Index (TDI), a label-free probe of embedding sensitivity when task accuracy or Jacobian Frobenius norm is insufficient.
  Thirteen pre-registered blocks from classical ML through Qwen2.5-7B test the predicted matched, then isotropic, then wrong-W ordering on geometry and deployment drift; twelve pass, and the sole exception (Office-31) is an eigengap failure named before the run. At 7B scale, matched style-PMH improves selective honesty and preserves Style TDI where standard DPO degrades it.
  The contribution is naming the deployment nuisance covariance, stating what the regulariser must do, and supplying a closed-form falsifiable theory once that object is identified, not universality on every leaderboard.

</details>


### [207] [Posterior Collapse as Automatic Spectral Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22691)
*Johannes Hirn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper shows that posterior collapse in β-VAEs is equivalent to automatic spectral pruning, providing a theoretical framework through Landau stability analysis that connects collapse dynamics, utility ranking, and PCA, with experimental validation on climate data.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The paper addresses the phenomenon of posterior collapse in β-VAEs, where latent modes become inactive, and seeks to understand its mechanism and implications for variable selection.

Method: The authors derive this connection through a Landau stability analysis of the β-VAE loss function, defining a latent-rescaling-invariant order parameter that quantifies the utility of each latent mode and establishes collapse thresholds. They validate the theory analytically in the linear Gaussian case, demonstrating that the collapse spectrum, utility spectrum, and normalized PCA spectrum coincide, with each collapse following a mean-field law.

Result: The analysis reveals that posterior collapse in β-VAEs naturally implements spectral pruning, where latent modes collapse from least to most useful as β increases, providing a principled ranking of active latent modes and identifying which effective variables to inspect first. The theoretical predictions are confirmed through experiments on the WorldClim dataset.

Conclusion: This work provides a theoretical foundation for understanding posterior collapse in β-VAEs as a form of automatic spectral pruning, with practical implications for variable selection in high-dimensional data.

Abstract: We show that posterior collapse in $β$-VAEs implements automatic spectral pruning. A latent mode collapses if its contribution to reconstruction is below the cutoff set by $β$. Equilibrium solutions with different $β$ thus reveal a cascade of collapses as latent modes decouple from least to most useful.
  We derive this as a consequence of the loss via a Landau stability analysis. We define a latent-rescaling-invariant order parameter that ranks active latent modes and whose collapse thresholds identify which effective variables to inspect first.
  In the linear Gaussian case, the collapse spectrum, utility spectrum, and normalized PCA spectrum coincide, and each collapse follows a mean-field law. We test these predictions on the WorldClim dataset.

</details>


### [208] [Clipping Bottleneck: Stabilizing RLVR via Stochastic Recovery of Near-Boundary Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22703)
*Shuo Yang,Jinda Lu,Chiyu Ma,Kexin Huang,Haoming Meng,Qihui Zhang,Yuyang Liu,Bolin Ding,Guoyin Wang,Li Yuan,Jingren Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对RLVR中硬裁剪导致的训练信号丢失问题，提出NSR随机保留近边界token，在多种模型架构上验证有效，超越DAPO/GSPO基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于GRPO的RLVR方法中，硬裁剪规则过于刚性，将近边界区域（阈值边缘）的有价值信号丢弃，导致优化信息损失。

Method: 提出Near-boundary Stochastic Rescue (NSR)方法：对边界附近超出裁剪阈值的token进行随机保留，通过随机采样隐式引入梯度衰减，同时保持边界局部的随机救援机制。

Result: NSR在7B到30B不同规模及Dense/MoE架构上均实现稳定训练和性能提升。

Conclusion: NSR作为一个即插即用的方案，在7B至30B模型规模上验证有效，提升了训练稳定性并超越DAPO和GSPO基线。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a central paradigm for scaling LLM reasoning, yet its optimization often suffers from training instability and suboptimal convergence. Through a systematic dissection of clipping-based GRPO-style objectives, we identify the rigid clipping decision induced by hard clipping as a key practical bottleneck in the studied RLVR setups. Specifically, our analysis suggests that informative signals can lie in the near-boundary region just beyond the clipping threshold, and are therefore discarded by the standard hard-clipping rule. Notably, once this bottleneck is precisely identified, even simple stochastic perturbations at the boundary can recover meaningful performance gains. Building on this finding, we propose Near-boundary Stochastic Rescue (NSR), a minimal, plug-and-play modification that stochastically retains these slightly out-of-bound tokens to recover lost signals. While NSR, via stochastic sampling, can be interpreted as inducing an implicit gradient decay in expectation, our ablations reveal that its stochastic, boundary-local rescue mechanism is consistently more effective than deterministic gradient decay. Validated by extensive experiments across model sizes from 7B to 30B and both dense and MoE architectures, as a plug-and-play solution, NSR substantially improves training stability and delivers consistent gains over strong baselines such as DAPO and GSPO.

</details>


### [209] [Reading Task Failure Off the Activations: A Sparse-Feature Audit of GPT-2 Small on Indirect Object Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22719)
*Mahdi Nasermoghadasi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对GPT-2 small的SAE特征进行任务失败关联审计，发现"加密密钥"特征17,491与IOI任务失败高度相关，但三重控制实验表明该特征仅是相关而非因果，且SAE基底并未增加预测能力——核心贡献是可复现的廉价审计pipeline本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解大语言模型内部表征与行为失败之间的对应关系，识别哪些SAE特征可作为任务失败的指标，为可解释性研究提供可复现的方法论框架。

Method: 三阶段审计pipeline：(1) 显著性筛选——300个提示下对24,576个SAE特征进行Holm校正显著性检验和Cohen's d效应量分析；(2) 三重控制实验——因果消融（置零特征）、表征基线（逻辑回归对比）、种子鲁棒性检验（5个随机种子）；(3) 关键案例解剖——Fisher精确检验特征17,491与特定对象的关联。

Result: 146个特征通过Holm校正显著性阈值，105个达到大效应量(|d|>0.8)。最强相关特征17,491（标记为"加密密钥"）在"the keys"转移对象上GPT-2 small失败率93.3%对其他对象7.5%（Fisher精确p=8.79×10⁻³³）。但因果消融未能恢复准确率，逻辑回归在原始768维残差空间达到0.929 ROC AUC（匹配top-100 SAE特征），特征17,491仅在1/5随机种子中保持top-|d|排名。

Conclusion: SAE特征提供了可解释性而非额外预测能力；单一特征不足以解释失败，审计pipeline（低成本、模型无关）是主要贡献。全部资源（代码、语料库、激活矩阵、脚本）已开源，可在笔记本电脑运行。

Abstract: We report a small, reproducible audit of which sparse-autoencoder (SAE) features of GPT-2 small fire differently on failed versus successful trials of the Indirect Object Identification (IOI) task. On 300 prompts, GPT-2 small reaches 79.7% accuracy; 146 of the 24,576 features in the layer-8 residual-stream SAE release of Bloom (2024) clear a Holm-corrected significance threshold and 105 reach a large effect size (|Cohen's d| > 0.8). The strongest single correlate of failure -- feature 17,491, d=+2.93, Neuronpedia label 'cryptographic keys' -- is essentially silent except when the prompt's transferred object is 'the keys,' on which GPT-2 small fails 93.3% of the time vs. 7.5% on the other seven objects (Fisher exact p = 8.79 x 10^-33). We put this correlate through three controls that a mechanistic claim should pass. (i) A causal ablation: zeroing feature 17,491 in the residual stream across all token positions of the 45 keys prompts does not restore accuracy (6.7% -> 4.4%); the feature is a correlate, not a sufficient cause at this layer. (ii) A representation baseline: a logistic regression on the raw 768-dimensional residual stream reaches 5-fold ROC AUC = 0.929, matching the top-100 SAE features (0.927); the SAE basis adds interpretability, not predictive power. (iii) A seed-robustness check: across five random seeds the keys-subset failure rate stays in 75.0--93.3% (the behavioural effect is real), but feature 17,491 is the top-|d| feature in only 1 of 5 runs. The methodological contribution is therefore the audit pipeline (cheap, model-agnostic, surfaces named correlates) rather than any single feature found through it. We release the code, the 300-prompt corpus, the 300x24,576 activation matrix, the ablation and baseline scripts, and the figures. The full pipeline runs on a laptop (Apple M3 Max, no discrete GPU).

</details>


### [210] [Multiple Neural Operators Achieve Near-Optimal Rates for Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22724)
*Adrien Weihs,Hayden Schaeffer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多任务场景下学习算子集合的近似和统计复杂度，以MNO架构为核心，推导了Lipschitz多算子映射的近优上界，并建立了参数复杂度的维度诅咒和极小极大速率。研究表明，跨任务共享表征不会增加总体成本，多任务算子学习与单任务学习遵循相同缩放定律；MNO与拼接式DeepONet扩展在渐近速率上基本等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多任务场景下算子学习（尤其是MNO架构）的近似能力和统计泛化能力，建立多任务算子学习的理论基础

Method: 对广泛类的Lipschitz多算子映射推导近优上界；建立参数复杂度的维度诅咒并证明相应的极小极大速率；与基于任务输入拼接的多任务DeepONet扩展进行比较分析

Result: 多任务算子学习遵循与单任务算子学习相同的缩放定律；两种架构（MNO与多任务DeepONet）满足基本相同的渐近速率

Conclusion: 共享表征策略不会增加多任务算子学习的总体复杂度成本；MNO与扩展版DeepONet从最坏情况近似复杂度角度遵循相同的渐近率

Abstract: We study the approximation and statistical complexity of learning collections of operators in a shared multi-task setting, with a focus on the Multiple Neural Operators (MNO) architecture. For broad classes of Lipschitz multiple operator maps, we derive near-optimal upper bounds for approximation and statistical generalization. On the lower-bound side, we establish a curse of parametric complexity and prove corresponding minimax rates. Together, these results show that shared representations across tasks do not increase the overall cost: multi-task operator learning follows the same scaling laws as single operator learning. We also compare MNO with a multi-task extension of DeepONet based on concatenated task inputs and show that, from a worst-case approximation-complexity perspective, both architectures satisfy essentially the same asymptotic rates.

</details>


### [211] [Ternary Decision Trees with Locally-Adaptive Uncertainty Zones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22740)
*William Smits*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出三叉决策树，通过在学习过程中自动估计的局部不确定性区域δ，将标准二分类决策扩展为"确定拒绝-融合预测-确定接受"的三路分支，在保持预测性能的同时为下游应用提供可解释的不确定性信号。Margin方法以其零超参数特性和最高效率成为推荐首选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统决策树使用硬二值阈值划分特征空间，导致远离决策边界的样本与紧贴边界的样本获得相同的置信度预测，无法有效表达预测不确定性。

Method: 提出三叉决策树框架，在每个分裂节点引入宽度为δ的不确定性区域。落入该区域的样本通过加权融合两个子树的预测结果进行预测，并被标记为"边界不确定"。δ的计算基于CART分裂过程中已获取的统计量，无需外部噪声参数。五种δ估计方法：(1) quality-plateau基于分裂准则曲线的平台宽度；(2) class-overlap基于经验类分布重叠；(3) gain-ratio基于分裂质量与分裂熵的比值；(4) node-bootstrap基于节点级重采样的阈值方差；(5) margin基于SVM风格的最近异类样本距离。

Result: 在72个OpenML-CC18数据集的5折交叉验证中，所有五种方法在"已决策准确率"指标上均显著优于标准CART（Wilcoxon符号秩检验p < 0.001）。margin方法效率最高（0.104）、获胜率最高（42/72）、且无超参数。在Breiman合成数据上验证了margin的自校准特性，以及node-bootstrap和quality-plateau对理论不可约误差的追踪能力。

Conclusion: 该方法在统计和实际应用中均验证有效：margin方法凭借零超参数和最高效率（0.104）成为最佳选择；node-bootstrap和quality-plateau在追踪理论不可约误差方面表现最优；在医学影像筛查任务中，node-bootstrap仅标记10.8%的边界不确定样本即可获得+0.71%的准确率提升。

Abstract: Decision trees partition the feature space using hard binary thresholds, assigning identical confidence to instances far from a decision boundary and to those directly on it. We introduce ternary decision trees, which augment each split node with an uncertainty zone of half-width delta centered on the optimal threshold. Instances in this zone receive predictions formed by weighted blending of both child subtrees and are flagged as boundary-uncertain, signaling that downstream applications may treat these predictions differently. Crucially, delta is computed locally at each node from statistics already available during standard CART split finding, requiring no external noise specification. We propose and evaluate five delta-estimation methods: quality-plateau (plateau width of the split criterion curve), class-overlap (empirical class-distribution overlap), gain-ratio (split quality relative to split entropy), node-bootstrap (threshold variance under node-level resampling), and margin (SVM-inspired distance to the nearest cross-class training example). Evaluated across 72 OpenML-CC18 datasets with 5-fold cross-validation, all five methods with probabilistic routing significantly outperform standard CART on decided accuracy (Wilcoxon signed-rank, p < 0.001). The margin method achieves the best efficiency (0.104 accuracy gain per unit of boundary-uncertain flagging rate), wins on 42 of 72 datasets, and requires zero additional hyperparameters. Analysis on three Breiman synthetic benchmarks reveals that margin is self-calibrating on clean data while node-bootstrap and quality-plateau best track theoretical irreducible error. Experiments on four medical and financial datasets demonstrate practical value: on mammography, node-bootstrap achieves +0.71% decided accuracy by flagging 10.8% of screening cases as boundary-uncertain.

</details>


### [212] [SeqLoRA: Bilevel Orthogonal Adaptation for Continual Multi-Concept Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22743)
*Javad Parsa,Enis Simsar,Amir Joudaki,Thomas Hofmann,André M. H. Teixeira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SeqLoRA是一种新的约束持续学习框架，通过双层优化联合训练LoRA因子，用于解决文本到图像扩散模型中多概念组合的表征干扰问题。理论证明了收敛性和灾难性遗忘界限，实验验证了在101个概念规模下的身份保持和属性干扰减少效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效微调方法虽能快速个性化文本到图像扩散模型，但在组合多个自定义概念时存在表征干扰问题。现有模块化方法依赖昂贵的后处理融合或冻结适应子空间，限制了表达力和概念保真度。

Method: SeqLoRA采用约束性持续学习框架，通过双层优化联合优化LoRA的两个因子；同时建立层激活的矩阵次高斯过程模型来推导灾难性遗忘的高概率界，理论证明从数据学习LoRA基比冻结基方法更有效降低残余干扰能量。

Result: 在多概念图像生成任务中，SeqLoRA在101个概念规模下提升了身份保持能力和可扩展性，同时避免了昂贵的融合过程并减少了组合生成中的属性干扰。

Conclusion: SeqLoRA通过联合优化LoRA因子，有效解决了多概念组合中的表征干扰问题，在保持表达力的同时避免昂贵的后处理融合。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning enables fast personalization of text-to-image diffusion models, but composing multiple custom concepts remains challenging due to representation interference. Existing modular methods either rely on expensive post-hoc fusion or freeze adaptation subspaces, which limit expressiveness and concept fidelity. To address this trade-off, we propose Sequential regularized LoRA (SeqLoRA), a constrained continual learning framework that jointly optimizes both LoRA factors via bilevel optimization. Theoretically, we establish strong convergence guarantees for our algorithm and model the residual layer activations as a matrix sub-Gaussian process to derive high-probability bounds on catastrophic forgetting. We further prove that learning the LoRA basis from data minimizes residual interference energy more effectively than frozen-basis methods. Experiments on multi-concept image generation demonstrate that SeqLoRA improves identity preservation and scalability across up to 101 concepts, while avoiding costly fusion and reducing attribute interference in composed generations.

</details>


### [213] [Plug-in Losses for Evidential Deep Learning: A Simplified Framework for Uncertainty Estimation that Includes the Softmax Classifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22746)
*Berk Hayta,Hannah Laus,Simon Mittermaier,Felix Krahmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了对Evidential Deep Learning (EDL)目标的简化近似方法，通过Dirichlet均值处的plug-in损失替代复杂的目标函数，证明了近似误差随证据增加而衰减，并验证了该方法在语音识别任务中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实传感器学习系统需要可靠且计算高效的的不确定性估计方法。EDL通过Dirichlet分布建模类别概率实现单次传递的不确定性估计，但其目标函数比标准监督学习损失更复杂，增加了分析和实现难度。

Method: 用Dirichlet均值处的plug-in损失近似原始EDL目标；针对MSE和交叉熵等损失函数，证明了在温和条件下近似误差随证据增长而衰减；通过softmax映射将框架与标准分类器统一。

Result: 在Google Speech Commands数据集上，简化后的目标函数在预测准确性和选择性预测性能上与经典EDL相当，同时更易于使用标准深度学习损失和训练流程实现。

Conclusion: 该工作为EDL提供了理论分析和简化实现方案，证明了在证据增长时近似误差会衰减，并首次将EDL应用于语音识别任务获得覆盖-准确性权衡曲线。

Abstract: Real-world sensor-based learning systems require uncertainty estimation that is both reliable and computationally efficient. Evidential Deep Learning (EDL) provides single-pass uncertainty estimation by modeling the class probabilities via Dirichlet distributions, where the Dirichlet parameters are predicted by a learned neural network mapping. However, this approach can lead to computational challenges, as Dirichlet expected objectives are more complex than standard supervised learning losses, complicating their analysis and implementation. We address this issue by approximating the objective of the first-order empirical risk minimization problem induced by EDL with a plug-in loss evaluated at the Dirichlet mean and show that, under mild assumptions, the approximation error decays with growing evidence for a broad class of loss functions, including mean-squared error and cross-entropy loss. As a special case, our analysis provides justification for the use of softmax in the context of uncertainty estimation, since under a particular evidence-to-Dirichlet mapping, our framework includes the standard softmax classifier. We validate the proposed simplified objectives on the Google Speech Commands dataset and show that they achieve predictive accuracy and selective prediction performance comparable to classical EDL, while being simpler to implement using standard deep learning losses and training pipelines. To the best of our knowledge, this empirical analysis is the first to obtain coverage-accuracy trade-offs for speech recognition tasks through EDL.

</details>


### [214] [Uniform Diffusion Models Revisited: Leave-One-Out Denoiser and Absorbing State Reformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22765)
*Samson Gourevitch,Yazid Janati,Dario Shariatian,Umut Simsekli,Eric Moulines,Eric P. Xing,Alain Durmus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了Uniform Diffusion Models (UDM)中标准插件参数化与去噪目标之间的理论不匹配，提出用leave-one-out后验替代标准桥参数化。通过精确转换推导、改进采样策略和吸收态重构，统一了掩码与均匀扩散框架，实验证明两类扩散模型的性能差距主要源于参数化和采样设计而非边缘分布选择本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在离散扩散模型中，干净数据预测可以以不同方式用于定义反向动力学。Masked Diffusion Models (MDM)中这些选择基本一致，但Uniform Diffusion Models (UDM)中并不一致。标准插件桥参数化存在理论不匹配：优化目标并非去噪后验，而应是一种leave-one-out后验，导致插件ELBO与常规交叉熵去噪目标之间存在偏差。

Method: 1. 理论分析：识别标准插件桥参数化与去噪后验之间的不匹配，定义leave-one-out后验来预测每个干净token而不使用其自身的噪声观测。2. 精确转换：推导去噪器、leave-one-out后验和分数函数之间的精确转换关系，实现参数化与训练目标的解耦。3. 推理改进：设计informed predictor-corrector采样器和基于leave-one-out预测器的改进温度采样。4. 吸收态重构：将均匀扩散重新表述为包含掩码式采样操作的形式，保留UDM联合分布，同时简化去噪后验、引入unmasking和自然重掩码机制。

Result: 1. 识别并解决了UDM中参数化与训练目标的理论不匹配问题。2. 推导出去噪器、leave-one-out后验与分数函数之间的精确转换。3. 提出无需额外训练的informed predictor-corrector采样器和改进温度采样方法。4. 提出吸收态重构，保持UDM联合分布同时简化采样操作。5. 语言建模实验表明leave-one-out参数化一致提升UDM生成质量，吸收态构造达到或超越掩码扩散模型。

Conclusion: 该工作通过引入leave-one-out后验解决了UDM中参数化与训练目标不匹配的问题，并提出了改进的采样策略和吸收态重构，为统一理解掩码扩散与均匀扩散提供了理论框架。实验表明，掩码与均匀扩散之间的性能差距主要源于参数化和采样设计，而非边缘分布本身的选择。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models are often trained through clean-data prediction, but the prediction can be used in different ways to define the reverse dynamics. In Masked Diffusion Models (MDM) these choices largely coincide, whereas in Uniform Diffusion Models (UDM) they do not. We show that the standard plug-in bridge parameterization for UDM is not optimized by the denoising posterior, but by a leave-one-out posterior that predicts each clean token without using its own noisy observation. This identifies a mismatch between the plug-in ELBO and the usual cross-entropy denoising objective. We characterize the leave-one-out target and derive exact conversions between the denoiser, the leave-one-out posterior, and the score. These conversions allow us to disentangle parameterization and training objective. Our results also lead to inference improvements without any additional training through an informed predictor-corrector sampler and improved temperature sampling based on the leave-one-out predictor.
  We further introduce an absorbing-state reformulation of uniform diffusion that preserves the UDM joint law while decomposing it into masked-diffusion-like sampling operations, with simpler denoising posteriors, carry-over unmasking, and a natural remasking mechanism. On language modeling, leave-one-out parameterizations consistently improve UDM generation, while the absorbing construction matches or surpasses masked diffusion. These results suggest that the empirical gap between masked and uniform diffusion is driven less by the choice of marginals themselves than by parameterization and sampling design. The code and models can be found at https://github.com/samsongourevitch/rev_udm.

</details>


### [215] [Remember to be Curious: Episodic Context and Persistent Worlds for 3D Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22814)
*Lily Goli,Justin Kerr,Daniele Reda,Alec Jacobson,Andrea Tagliasacchi,Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This work reveals that effective curiosity in photorealistic 3D environments requires a persistent world model (online 3D reconstruction) combined with episodic trajectory context (sequence model over RGB), enabling effective exploration purely via intrinsic rewards and successful zero-shot generalization to new environments.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Curiosity-driven RL in complex photorealistic 3D environments suffers from local loops and redundant rewards for revisiting forgotten states due to lack of spatial persistence and episodic context in traditional approaches.

Method: The method uses two key components: (1) An online 3D reconstruction as a persistent world model that is continuously updated, and (2) An agent policy parameterized as a sequence model over RGB observations to maintain episodic trajectory history. At deployment, the agent navigates using solely RGB frames without the reconstruction module.

Result: On HM3D, the proposed agent outperforms RL-based active mapping baselines. It generalizes zero-shot to Gibson and AI-generated worlds. End-to-end policy enables efficient adaptation to downstream tasks (apple picking and image-goal navigation), outperforming from-scratch baselines.

Conclusion: By combining online 3D reconstruction with sequence-based policy modeling, the method achieves effective exploration purely via curiosity signals. The approach demonstrates strong zero-shot generalization across different 3D environment datasets and successful adaptation to downstream tasks like apple picking and image-goal navigation.

Abstract: Exploration is a prerequisite for learning useful behaviors in sparse-reward, long-horizon tasks, particularly within 3D environments. Curiosity-driven reinforcement learning addresses this via intrinsic rewards derived from the mismatch between the agent's predictive model of the world and reality. However, translating this intrinsic motivation to complex, photorealistic environments remains difficult, as agents can become trapped in local loops and receive fresh rewards for revisiting forgotten states. In this work, we demonstrate that this failure stems from a lack of spatial persistence and episodic context. We show that effective curiosity requires a model of the world that is persistent and continuously updated, paired with an agent that maintains an episodic trajectory history to navigate toward novel regions. We achieve this using an online 3D reconstruction as a persistent model of the world, while the agent policy is parameterized as a sequence model over RGB observations to maintain episodic context. This design enables effective exploration during training while allowing the agent to navigate using solely RGB frames at deployment. Trained purely via curiosity on HM3D, our agent outperforms RL-based active mapping baselines and generalizes zero-shot to Gibson and AI-generated worlds. Our end-to-end policy enables efficient adaptation to downstream tasks, such as apple picking and image-goal navigation, outperforming from-scratch baselines. Please see video results at https://recuriosity.github.io/.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [216] [Near-Optimal Generalized Private Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21601)
*Anamay Chaturvedi,Monika Henzinger,Jalaj Upadhyay*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出广义阈值机制(GTM)解决差分隐私下的广义私有测试问题，证明了其在准确性和样本复杂度上的近优性，并给出从持续观察(CO)设置到批量设置的首个黑盒约简，为差分隐私优化领域提供新工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展标准私有测试问题(Sparse Vector Technique)的应用范围，解决先前提出的自适应选择接受阈值的挑战，特别是在超参数优化场景中(如ICLR 2022 Papernot和Steinke的工作)，并为差分隐私优化提供从持续观察(CO)设置到批量设置的理论基础。

Method: 提出广义阈值机制(GTM)，通过精心设计的阈值函数p̄_t = max(p*/γΛ_t, 1-γΛ_t(1-p*)) - δ_t/ε_t和样本复杂度界限O((ln(t/β)/(γ-1)²)·max(Λ_t/p*, (1-p*)⁻¹))实现广义私有测试。

Result: GTM对任意(ε_t, δ_t)-DP机制序列保持纯ε-DP；在概率1-β下，所有t≥1的评估次数受限于O((ln(t/β)/(γ-1)²)·max(Λ_t/p*, (1-p*)⁻¹))；理论下限证明了该方法在准确性和样本复杂度上的近优性。

Conclusion: GTM是广义私有测试问题的最优解决方案，为差分隐私优化提供了从持续观察设置到批量设置的黑盒约简，首次实现了许多最大化问题的DP-CO算法。

Abstract: In differential privacy (DP), the generalized private testing problem was introduced by Liu and Talwar (STOC 2019). Given a dataset $X \in \mathcal{X}$ and a sequence of black-box $\varepsilon_t$-DP mechanisms $M_t:\mathcal{X}\to\{+1,-1\}$, the analyst must accept the first mechanism whose success probability $p_t=\Pr[M_t(X)=+1]$ exceeds a given threshold $p^*\in(0,1)$, while achieving DP. Accuracy is measured by the gap between $p^*$ and a rejection threshold $\bar{p}$, such that with probability $1-β$ for all $t\geq1$, if $p_t\leq\bar{p}$, then $M_t$ is rejected, and if $p_t\geq p^*$, then it is accepted. This generalizes the standard private testing problem, whose solution, the Sparse Vector Technique, is ubiquitous in DP.
  We introduce the Generalized Thresholding Mechanism (GTM) for generalized private testing. For $\varepsilon>0$ and any sequence of $(\varepsilon_t,δ_t)$-DP mechanisms $M_t$, the GTM is pure $\varepsilon$-DP. For $θ>0$, $γ\in(1,2]$, and $β\in(0,1)$, $\bar{p}_t=\max(p^*/γΛ_t, 1 - γΛ_t(1-p^*))-δ_t/\varepsilon_t$ for $Λ_t=(5t\ln^3(t+2))^{(2+θ)\varepsilon_t/\varepsilon}(4/β)^{(3+θ+2/θ)\varepsilon_t/\varepsilon}$. With probability $1-β$, the number of evaluations of $M_t$ is at most $O((\ln(t/β)/(γ-1)^2)\max(Λ_t/p^*,(1-p^*)^{-1}))$ for all $t\geq 1$. Our lower bounds prove near-optimality of our accuracy and sample complexity guarantees.
  Via the GTM, we give a black-box reduction for DP optimization from the continual observation (CO) setting to the batch setting. This gives us the first DP-CO algorithms for many maximization problems. Further, the GTM permits an adaptive choice of acceptance thresholds $(p^*_t)_{t\geq1}$, addressing a challenge mentioned in prior work on using generalized private testing for hyperparameter optimization (Papernot and Steinke (ICLR 2022)).

</details>


### [217] [Finding a Solution to the Erdős-Ginzburg-Ziv Theorem in Linear Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21753)
*Sunghyeon Jo*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文给出Erdős-Ginzburg-Ziv定理中找长度为n的和可被n整除的子序列问题的首个确定性线性时间算法(O(n))，改进了之前Leung的O(n log log log n)算法。核心贡献是素数目标子集和问题的线性时间算法，通过维护等差数列表示并利用Frobenius区间合并策略实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进Erdős-Ginzburg-Ziv定理子序列查找算法的时间复杂度，从O(n log log log n)进一步降低到线性时间，以更高效地找到长度为n的和可被n整除的子序列

Method: 通过维护可达和的紧致等差数列表示来解决素数目标子集和问题。当两个等差数列相交时，使用有界Frobenius区间将它们合并为一个更长的等差数列。利用乘法归约将素数算法扩展到任意模数

Result: 给出了第一个确定性线性时间算法用于Erdős-Ginzburg-Ziv问题，算法核心是在素数域上维护等差数列可达和表示，当表示包含完整等差数列或覆盖所有非零剩余类时，可构造性地恢复目标剩余

Conclusion: 提出了第一个确定性线性时间算法，将之前最优的 O(n log log log n) 复杂度进一步降低到 O(n)，其中核心是关于素数目标子集和问题的线性时间算法，通过维护可达和的紧致等差数列表示实现

Abstract: The Erdős-Ginzburg-Ziv theorem states that every sequence of 2n - 1 integers contains a subsequence of length n whose sum is divisible by n. Choi, Kang, and Lim gave a simple deterministic O(n log n) algorithm for finding such a subsequence, and Leung recently improved this to O(n log log log n). We give a deterministic linear-time algorithm. The core is a linear-time algorithm for the following prime target subset-sum problem: given p - 1 nonzero residues in Z_p and a target residue, find a subset with the prescribed sum. Our algorithm maintains a compact arithmetic-progression representation of reachable sums. When two progressions intersect, a bounded Frobenius interval in their sum allows them to be merged into one longer progression, with enough growth to pay for the update. When the representation either contains a full progression or covers all nonzero residues, the target residue is recovered constructively. The standard multiplicative reduction then extends the prime algorithm to arbitrary moduli.

</details>


### [218] [An $Ω(n \log n)$ Randomized Lower Bound for Cutting a Cake into Proportionally Fair Pieces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21829)
*Stephen Arndt,Kirk Pruhs,Trung Tran*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在Robertson-Webb模型中，任何实现比例公平的随机化蛋糕切割算法都需要Ω(n log n)次查询，为该问题建立了查询复杂度的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究蛋糕切割问题的计算复杂度极限，确定随机化算法在该问题上的理论效率边界。

Method: 通过复杂性理论分析，证明随机化算法在Robertson-Webb模型下存在查询复杂度的下界。

Result: 证明任何随机化算法在Robertson-Webb模型中实现比例公平分配都需要至少Ω(n log n)次查询。

Conclusion: 随机化算法在蛋糕切割问题的查询复杂度下界为Ω(n log n)，这意味着任何随机化算法在最坏情况下都需要至少这个数量的查询才能达到比例公平。

Abstract: We consider the classic cake cutting problem in the Robertson-Webb model, with the objective of proportional fairness. We show that any randomized algorithm must use $Ω(n \log n)$ queries.

</details>


### [219] [Optimal $e^{(γ+o(1))n}$-Approximation of the Permanent of Positive Semidefinite Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21946)
*Nima Anari,Farzam Ebrahimnejad*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文确定了确定性多项式时间算法下正半定矩阵行列式最优近似比为e^γ≈1.78（每变量），完整解决了这一开放问题。证明使用Wick公式的熵方法，揭示了近似损失恰好源于Exp(1)分布的-γ熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定厄米正半定矩阵的行列式（permanent）在密码学、组合优化和量子信息等领域具有重要应用。已有工作证明了e^(γ-ε)n的不可近似性下界，但确定性多项式时间算法的上界（即近似比）一直未能匹配，存在最优比率的悬而未决问题。

Method: 作者采用熵方法（entropy argument），将Wick积分公式应用于per(A)的计算。通过定义Φ(V)——一个关于正定矩阵X的最大化凹函数——构造估计量P̂(A)=e^Φ(V)。核心洞察在于：对于T~Exp(1)的指数分布，E[log T] = -γ恰好损失了欧拉-马歇罗尼常数，这解释了近似比中e^γ因子的来源。

Result: 论文证明了精确的"夹心"不等式：e^(-γn)·P̂(A) ≤ per(A) ≤ P̂(A)。其中P̂(A)可通过解凹最大化问题计算得多项式时间。这给出了确定性多项式时间下的e^((γ+ε)n)近似算法。结合已有硬度结果，该工作完整刻画了近似比的最优值：e^(γ+o(1))n。

Conclusion: 研究解决了确定性多项式时间算法下正定厄米矩阵行列式（permanent）最优指数近似比的悬而未决问题。证明了下界e^(γn)和上界完全匹配（其中γ为欧拉-马歇罗尼常数≈1.78），从而确定最优近似比为e^(γ+o(1))n，假设P≠NP。此外，该工作还展示了GPT等AI工具在数学证明研究中的实际应用价值。

Abstract: We determine, up to lower-order terms in the exponent, the best possible deterministic polynomial-time approximation ratio for the permanent of a Hermitian positive semidefinite matrix. If $A\succeq 0$ has no zero diagonal entry, $d=\operatorname{rank}(A)$, $A=VV^\dagger$ with $V\in\mathbb{C}^{n\times d}$ full column rank, and $v_1,\ldots,v_n$ are the rows of $V$, define \[
  Φ(V)=\max_{X\succ 0}
