  \left\{\sum_{i=1}^n \log(v_i^\dagger Xv_i)+\log\det X-\operatorname{tr} X+d\right\},
  \qquad
  \widehat P(A)=e^{Φ(V)}. \] We prove the exact sandwich \[
  e^{-γn}\widehat P(A)\le \operatorname{per}(A)\le \widehat P(A). \] Here $γ$ is the Euler--Mascheroni constant. Since the maximization is concave, this gives a deterministic polynomial-time $e^{(γ+\varepsilon)n}$-approximation for every $\varepsilon>0$. Combined with the previous $e^{(γ-\varepsilon)n}$-hardness of approximation for positive semidefinite permanents, this resolves the optimal exponential approximation ratio for deterministic polynomial-time algorithms as $e^{(γ+o(1))n}$, assuming $\mathrm{P}\ne\mathrm{NP}$. The proof is an entropy argument applied to the standard Wick integral formula for $\operatorname{per}(A)$; the loss is exactly $γ$ per factor because $\mathbb{E}[\log T]=-γ$ for $T\sim\operatorname{Exp}(1)$.
  The result was obtained through interactions with GPT 5.5 Pro Extended: the first author's interaction was one-shot, while the second author's was a separate multi-turn interaction with high-level guidance. Both authors verified the theorem and proof. Codex was used to assemble and typeset the manuscript.

</details>


### [220] [A Coalgebraic Dijkstra Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22149)
*Takahiro Sanada,Yoàv Montacute,Kittiphon Phalakarn,Ichiro Hasuo*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文引入coalgebraic shortest path problem（CSPP）作为状态转移系统上一大类优化问题的统一框架，涵盖经典最短路径、最大宽度路径、二人博弈等问题以及新的最短二叉树问题等。提出coalgebraic Dijkstra算法，并给出算法能返回正确解的充分必要条件，同时证明该算法具有与经典Dijkstra算法相当的渐近复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dijkstra算法虽然经典，但不同变体（如最大宽度路径问题、二人博弈问题）缺乏统一的抽象框架。该研究旨在用一个统一的co-代数框架整合这些优化问题。

Method: 提出coalgebraic shortest path problem（CSPP）统一框架，并开发coalgebraic Dijkstra算法求解该问题。

Result: 证明了提出的条件是充分必要的（确保算法返回正确解），且算法渐近复杂度与经典Dijkstra算法相当。

Conclusion: 提出了一个充分必要条件来判断Dijkstra风格的加速是否可行，并证明了算法具有与经典Dijkstra算法相当的渐近复杂度，为一大类状态转移系统上的优化问题提供了统一的算法框架。

Abstract: The Dijkstra algorithm is a classical method for solving the shortest path problem on weighted graphs. There are several variations of the Dijkstra algorithm, including algorithms for the widest path problem and for two-player games. In this paper, we introduce the coalgebraic shortest path problem (CSPP), a unifying framework for a broad class of optimization problems on state-transition systems. This framework encompasses not only the aforementioned problems but also new ones such as the shortest binary tree problem. We further present a coalgebraic Dijkstra algorithm for solving the CSPP efficiently under a suitable condition. Our condition is necessary and sufficient for the algorithm to return correct solutions, thereby providing a precise criterion for when Dijkstra-style acceleration is possible. We also show that the proposed algorithm achieves asymptotic complexity comparable to that of the classical Dijkstra algorithm.

</details>


### [221] [The Secretary Problem with a Stochastic Precursor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22653)
*Franziska Eberle,Alexander Lindermayr*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了经典秘书问题结合随机前驱信号的新范式，发现信号的到达时间本身具有独立价值。随机顺序模型中，单一均匀时序前驱即可将成功率从1/e提升至至少1/2；对抗顺序模型中，集中时序的信号可恢复常数保证，证明异步时序信息是一种有效的新型建议形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统预测研究关注预测内容的价值（如数值估计、解决方案），而本文提出预测的到达时间本身也具有独立价值，聚焦于一种新型的异步时序信息。

Method: 在随机顺序模型中使用动态规划与阈值策略分析最优策略的结构；对抗顺序模型中分析信号时间分布集中度对成功率的影响。

Result: 1) 随机顺序模型下，单个均匀时序的前驱信号即可将成功率从经典1/e提升至至少1/2，且随信号越晚接近，成功率趋近于1。2) 对抗顺序模型下，充分集中的信号可恢复常数级成功保证。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了异步时序信息是一种新颖且强大的在线决策辅助形式，可推广至其他问题。

Abstract: In learning-augmented online algorithms, predictions are usually valued for what they say: a value estimate, a solution, or an algorithmic recommendation. This paper shows that predictions can also be valuable solely due to their arrival time. We study the fundamental secretary problem augmented with a stochastic precursor: a content-free signal that is guaranteed to arrive no later than the best item, but is otherwise stochastically timed. The signal does not carry any additional information; nevertheless, its timing alone changes the structure of optimal stopping. We characterize optimal policies in the random-order and adversarial-order models. In random order, a single uniformly timed precursor already gives success probability at least $\frac12$, improving on the classic $\frac1e$ benchmark. With increasingly late precursors, the success probability approaches $1$. In adversarial order, for which traditional models do not admit strong guarantees, sufficiently concentrated precursors recover constant success guarantees. Our results show that such novel forms of asynchronous temporal information are a distinct and powerful form of advice in online decision making and may also be effective for other problems.

</details>


### [222] [Optimal Testing of Reed-Muller Codes with an Online Adversary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22813)
*Esty Kelman,Uri Meir,Kai Zhe Zheng*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究在线擦除模型下的 Reed-Muller 码测试问题，提出半采样测试器框架，通过先选子集再随机采样的方式抵抗在线擦除攻击，实现了该模型下的最优查询复杂度，并扩展至提升的仿射不变码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线擦除模型（Kalemaj, Raskhodnikova, Varma）中，对手可在每次查询后擦除多达 t 个输入点，这使得依赖可预测查询模式的传统测试器失效。为在此 adversarial 环境下进行 Reed-Muller 码的属性测试，需要设计具有随机化查询模式的测试器。

Method: 定义了半采样测试器这一混合测试框架（介于纯采样测试器与标准测试器之间），其核心操作模式为：首先选择域的一个子集 S，随后在 S 内均匀随机进行查询。这一设计使得查询模式具有随机性，从而能够抵抗对手在每次查询后进行的在线擦除攻击。

Result: 给出了 Reed-Muller 码的半采样测试器及其最优的 soundness 分析；同时将框架应用于提升的仿射不变码（Guo, Kopparty, Sudan），首次实现在线擦除模型下的测试。

Conclusion: 提出了半采样测试器框架，在在线擦除模型中实现了 Reed-Muller 码测试的最优查询复杂度，改进了 Minzer 和 Zheng (SODA 2024) 的结果，并将该框架扩展到提升的仿射不变码，首次为这类码提供了在线擦除模型下的测试器。

Abstract: Motivated by applications to property testing in the online-erasure model of Kalemaj, Raskhodnikova, and Varma (ITCS 2022 and Theory of Computing 2023), we define and analyze {\em semi-sample-based testers} for Reed-Muller codes. The task in Reed-Muller testing is to determine whether an input function $f: \F^n \to \F$ belongs to the Reed-Muller code or is far from it, using as few point queries to $f$ as possible. Reed-Muller testing is a well-studied task with its roots in both the Property Testing and Probabilistically Checkable Proofs literature. The online-erasure model introduces a twist: after each query made, an adversary may erase up to $t$ points of the input function, potentially thwarting any test in which the queries follow a predictable pattern.
  Semi-sample-based testers are a hybrid between sample-based testers -- which can only make uniformly random queries to the input function -- and standard testers, which can choose their queries freely. They are designed with the online-erasure model in mind and operate by first choosing some subset $S$ of the domain and then making their queries uniformly at random inside of $S$. We describe semi-sample-based testers for the Reed-Muller code and give an optimal analysis of their soundness.
  Consequently, we show that semi-sample-based testers are indeed effective in the presence of online erasures, and thereby achieve optimal query complexity for testing the Reed-Muller code in the online-erasure model. This result improves upon prior work of Minzer and Zheng (SODA 2024). As an added bonus, we show that semi-sample-based testers also exist for the lifted affine-invariant codes of Guo, Kopparty, and Sudan (ITCS 2013), thereby providing the first known testers for these codes in the online-erasure model.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [223] [Benchmarking and Improving Monitors for Out-Of-Distribution Alignment Failure in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21602)
*Dylan Feng,Pragya Srivastava,Cassidy Laidlaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MOOD基准用于评估LLM监控pipeline对分布外对齐失败的检测能力。研究发现单独使用guard模型泛化性能有限，但结合Mahalanobis距离和困惑度OOD检测器可将召回率从39%提升至45%。实验表明OOD检测的加入在性能收益上优于单纯增大模型规模，为LLM监控系统的关键组件选择提供了重要依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的安全和对齐失败往往发生在分布外场景，而现有监控pipeline难以有效检测这类OOH对齐失败；同时由于模型在海量安全数据上训练，真正OOH的失败样本难以获取。

Method: 构建MOOD基准，包含受限训练集和七个多样化的对齐失败测试集；测试guard模型与四类OOD检测器的组合性能。

Result: Guard模型在分布外泛化能力不足；结合guard模型与OOD检测器可将召回率从39%提升至45%；模型规模增大时监控性能呈正向Scaling趋势；加入OOD检测的性能提升超过将guard模型参数扩大20倍的效果。

Conclusion: 结合guard模型与基于Mahalanobis距离和困惑度的OOD检测器可显著提升召回率，且OOD检测的加入带来的性能提升超过将guard模型参数扩大20倍的效果。MOOD基准为该领域后续研究提供了重要基础。

Abstract: Many safety and alignment failures of large language models (LLMs) occur due to out-of-distribution (OOD) situations: unusual prompt or response patterns that are unforeseen by model developers. We systematically study whether LLM monitoring pipelines can detect these OOD alignment failures by introducing a benchmark called Misalignment Out Of Distribution (MOOD). It is difficult to find failures that are truly OOD for off-the-shelf models trained on vast safety datasets. We sidestep this by including a restricted training set in MOOD that we use to train our own monitors, as well as seven test sets with diverse alignment failures that are outside the training distribution. Using MOOD, we find that guard models (safety classifiers) often fail to generalize OOD. To fix this, we propose combining guard models with OOD detectors. We test four types of OOD detectors and find that a combination of a guard model with Mahalanobis distance and perplexity-based OOD detectors can improve recall from 39% to 45%. We also establish positive scaling trends across model scales for monitors that combine a guard model and OOD detector; we find that incorporating OOD detection into monitoring achieves a higher recall gain than using a guard model with 20 times more parameters. Our work suggests that OOD detection should be a crucial component of LLM monitoring and provides a foundation for further work on this important problem.

</details>


### [224] [TO-Agents: A Multi-Agent AI Pipeline for Preference-Guided Topology Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21622)
*Isabella A. Stewart,Hongrui Chen,Faez Ahmed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TO-Agents是一个多智能体AI框架，通过自然语言接口将设计意图连接到迭代拓扑优化中。框架包含求解器转换、优化执行、可视化渲染和多智能体评判反馈机制。实验表明该方法相比基线消融管道可获得6倍更高的设计成功率，并支持端到端从设计意图到增材制造原型的工作流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑优化可生成高效结构，但设计师常需将定性意图（如视觉风格、产品体验、可制造性）手动转换为求解器设置，这些设置与设计偏好的关联不直观，导致设计迭代效率低下。

Method: 提出TO-Agents多智能体AI框架：1）将自然语言问题描述转换为经验证的求解器输入；2）运行拓扑优化求解器并渲染3D拓扑；3）使用多视角视觉-语言推理与独立评判智能体评估结果并修订求解器参数；4）制造智能体对顶级设计进行后处理以适配增材制造。

Result: 在悬臂梁基准测试和手机支架产品设计两个长时域任务中，TO-Agents在4轮修订周期、10次独立重复实验中，60%的试验至少产生一个偏好对齐设计；相比无视觉或历史反馈的消融管道，成功率提升高达6倍。评判分数和人工评估表明该框架能识别有效参数杠杆、从差修订中恢复、扩展设计探索。

Conclusion: TO-Agents通过将设计师从低层次参数调整转向高层次形式与功能规范，展示了自主工程设计的潜力，但同时也识别出需要安全措施的关键失效模式（超调、选择性记忆、工具误用、参数推理错误），以确保可靠的自主设计流程。

Abstract: Topology optimization can generate efficient structures, but designers often must manually translate qualitative intent, such as desired visual style, product experience, or manufacturability into solver settings that are not directly tied to those preferences. We present TO-Agents, a multi-agent AI framework that connects natural-language design intent with iterative topology optimization. The framework converts a human-provided problem description into validated solver inputs, runs a topology optimization solver, renders the resulting 3D topology, and uses multi-view vision-language reasoning with an independent judge agent to critique each result and revise solver parameters. We evaluate the framework on two long-horizon design tasks: a cantilever beam benchmark and a phone-stand product design. In both tasks, the designer specifies an aesthetic preference for hierarchically branched structures inspired by natural tree morphologies, and the system performs four revision cycles across ten independent replicates. TO-Agents produces at least one preference-aligned design in 60% of trials for each case study, corresponding to up to 6x more successful trials than an ablated pipeline without visual or historical feedback. Judge scores and human evaluations show that the pipeline can identify effective parameter levers, recover from poor revisions, and expand design exploration. A manufacturing agent further post-processes top-ranked designs for additive manufacturing, enabling end-to-end intent-to-prototype design. We also identify failure modes, including overshooting, selective memory, misplaced tools, and incorrect parameter reasoning. These results suggest that agentic topology optimization can shift designers from low-level parameter tuning toward higher-level specification of form and function, while highlighting safeguards needed for reliable autonomous engineering design.

</details>


### [225] [The Shape of Testimony: A Scalable Framework for Oral History Archive Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21623)
*Itamar Trainin,Renana Keydar,Amit Pinchevski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过计算分析方法（话语分割、主题建模、LLM）对1600余份大屠杀幸存者证词进行比较分析，验证了USC Shoah Foundation与Yale Fortunoff两个档案在结构化程度上的差异假设，但同时揭示了二者之间存在的重要重叠，复杂化了该领域长期以来的"结构化 vs.自由式"二元划分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation:  Holocaust研究领域长期存在一个基础性假设：USC Shoah Foundation的证词访谈采用结构化、访谈者引导的形式，而Yale Fortunoff Video Archive则偏好更自由式、开放性的风格。这一区分影响了后续的学术研究和档案建设，但该假设本身从未经过大规模实证检验。

Method: 研究采用了多种计算方法对1600余份证词进行大规模分析：(1)话语分割（discourse segmentation）；(2)主题建模（topic modeling）；(3)大型语言模型（LLM）分析。通过话题一致性、访谈者-幸存者互动动态和问题类型分布三个维度来量化证词的"结构化程度"。

Result: 研究结果总体上证实了先前研究识别的结构性差异，但同时揭示了两个档案之间存在显著重叠——既体现在单个访谈内部，也体现在共同的叙事模式中。这一发现使简单化的"结构化 vs.自由式"二元框架变得复杂化。

Conclusion: 研究提供了一个可扩展、可复现的语料库比较分析框架，证明了对两个档案之间差异的量化验证，并揭示了简单的"结构化 vs.自由式"二元划分是不够准确的。研究还提出了该框架在数字口述历史、叙事分析和公民科学标注平台设计中的更广泛应用前景。

Abstract: Researchers in Holocaust studies have often distinguished between two styles of oral survivor testimony: the USC Shoah Foundation's interviews tend to follow a structured, interviewer-guided format, whereas the Yale Fortunoff Video Archive generally favors a more free-form, open-ended style. This distinction has influenced both scholarly research and the development of later archives. In this study, we critically examine that claim by conducting a large-scale computational analysis of more than 1,600 testimonies from both collections. Leveraging discourse segmentation, topic modeling, and large language model (LLM) based analysis, we quantify the "structuredness" level of testimonies through topic coherence, interviewer-survivor dynamics, and the distribution of question types. Our results generally corroborate the structural differences identified in earlier research, while also revealing significant overlaps between the collections, both within individual interviews and across common narrative patterns. This complicates the simple "structured vs. free-form" dichotomy often applied to these oral histories. Beyond revisiting a foundational claim in Holocaust studies, our work provides a scalable, replicable framework for comparative corpus analysis. As a proof of concept, it suggests broader applications for digital oral history, narrative analysis, and the design of citizen-science annotation platforms.

</details>


### [226] [Evaluation of Pipelines for Data Integration into Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22304)
*Marvin Hofer,Erhard Rahm*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出KGI-Bench基准测试，用于评估知识图谱集成管道的质量。通过覆盖度、正确性和一致性三个指标，对12种不同管道进行了对比分析，为知识图谱数据集成提供了系统性评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将新数据集成到知识图谱(KG)中通常涉及不同工作流或管道中的任务；针对特定的集成问题存在多种可能的管道，但目前尚无通用方法来评估这些管道的整体质量和性能。

Method: 提出新基准测试KGI-Bench，用于评估将不同类型输入数据摄取到现有KG中的集成管道；通过三个互补的质量指标（覆盖度、正确性、一致性）评估管道；提供电影领域的基准数据集。

Result: 比较评估了12种管道；分析了不同输入数据格式和设计选择下的管道行为。

Conclusion: KGI-Bench能够有效评估知识图谱集成管道的质量和性能，为选择最佳集成方案提供了系统性基准。

Abstract: Integrating new data into knowledge graphs (KG) typically involves different tasks that are executed within workflows or pipelines There are many possible pipelines for a specific integration problem but there is not yet a general approach to evaluate the overall quality and performance of such pipelines to be able to determine the best choices. We therefore propose a new benchmark KGI-Bench to evaluate integration pipelines that ingest different kinds of input data into an existing KG. We evaluate pipelines by analyzing their output, i.e., the updated KG, with the three complementary quality metrics coverage, correctness and consistency. We also provide benchmark datasets (seed KG, overlapping input data of three formats, reference KG as a ground truth) for the movie domain. To demonstrate the applicability and usefulness of the proposed benchmark, we comparatively evaluate 12 pipelines and analyze their behavior across different input data formats and design choices.

</details>


### [227] [MindLoom: Composing Thought Modes for Frontier-Level Reasoning Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21630)
*Haiyang Shen,Taian Guo,Xuanzhong Chen,Mugeng Liu,Weichen Bi,Wenchun Jing,Sixiong Xie,Zhuofan Shi,Yudong Han,Chongyang Pan,Siqi Zhong,Jinsheng Huang,Ming Zhang,Yun Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MindLoom是一个基于组合思维模式工程的前沿级推理数据合成框架，通过将难题解决方案分解为思维模式链来理解问题构建逻辑，训练检索模型匹配问题状态与思维模式，从而合成具有可控难度和多样推理覆盖的新问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理数据合成方法对控制问题难度的结构因素缺乏深入理解，导致生成数据的推理多样性有限且难度控制不稳定，难以系统化地生成前沿级推理数据。

Method: MindLoom通过组合思维模式工程合成推理数据：首先将难题的解决方案分解为揭示构建逻辑的思维模式链，然后训练检索模型将问题状态与兼容的思维模式匹配，接着通过迭代应用检索到的思维模式到种子问题来合成新问题，采用分布对齐采样确保推理覆盖多样性，最后通过rollout评判对生成的问题进行难度标注并提供用于监督微调的正确响应。

Result: 在5个STEM学科和4个数学推理任务的9个基准测试上，MindLoom生成数据微调的模型在性能上优于基座模型、知识蒸馏和外部数据基线。消融实验验证了各组件的贡献。

Conclusion: MindLoom covers a broad range of reasoning patterns while maintaining useful difficulty control, and the implementation has been open-sourced.

Abstract: Although LLMs have made substantial progress in reasoning, systematically producing frontier-level reasoning data remains difficult. Existing synthesis methods often have limited visibility into the structural factors that govern problem difficulty, which can result in narrow diversity and unstable difficulty control. In this work, we view the difficulty of a reasoning problem as arising from the accumulation of atomic knowledge-reasoning transformations, which we term thought modes. Building on this perspective, we propose MindLoom, a framework for synthesizing frontier-level reasoning data through compositional thought mode engineering. Given a collection of hard problems with verified solutions, MindLoom first decomposes those solutions into thought mode chains that reveal each problem's construction logic. It then trains a retrieval model that matches problem states to compatible thought modes, providing guidance on which reasoning challenges to introduce during synthesis. New problems are composed by iteratively applying retrieved thought modes to seed questions, with distribution-aligned sampling to encourage diverse reasoning coverage. Finally, a rollout-based judging stage labels generated questions by difficulty and supplies judged-correct responses for supervised fine-tuning. We evaluate MindLoom on nine benchmarks covering five STEM disciplines and four mathematical reasoning tasks across multiple model families and sizes. Models fine-tuned on MindLoom-generated data achieves favorable performances over base models, distillation, and external-data baselines across the reported benchmarks. Ablation studies indicate the contribution of each component, and further analysis suggests that MindLoom covers a broad range of reasoning patterns while maintaining useful difficulty control. We have open-sourced our implementation at https://github.com/EachSheep/MindLoom.

</details>


### [228] [Investigating Concept Alignment Using Implausible Category Members](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21683)
*Sunayana Rane,Brenden M. Lake,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过询问不合理的类别成员问题探测AI系统的概念理解能力，发现AI在多个基本概念上的类别边界与人类存在显著差异，如词汇分类、水果蔬菜区分、武器概念等，这些概念不对齐会影响AI系统的安全性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法通过询问合理的类别成员（如"车是交通工具吗？"）容易召回训练数据中的模式，无法真正探测概念层面的知识。该研究采用询问不合理的类别成员（如"橄榄是交通工具吗？"）来探测概念边界。

Method: 使用Rosch和Mervis经典心理学研究中的物体集合，要求AI系统和人类参与者对物体进行超ordinate类别分配（包括匹配和不匹配的类别），比较两者在类别内和跨类别分配任务上的表现差异。

Result: 发现多类概念不对齐：1)将"words"归类为"vehicles"和"clothing"；2)将多个"vegetable"成员误判为"fruit"；3)将非武器类物品归入"weapons"类别。这些不对齐导致下游行为出现问题，影响AI安全性。

Conclusion: AI模型在概念理解上与人类存在系统性偏差，这些偏差会导致下游安全问题。需要开发更精确的方法来评估AI的概念边界，并在关键应用中验证概念对齐。

Abstract: Developing AI systems with a human-like understanding of everyday concepts is a key step towards developing safe, reliable systems whose behavior makes sense to humans. When probing concept understanding, asking questions about plausible category members (e.g., "Is a car a vehicle?") is likely to recall patterns in the model's vast training data. We pursue an alternative strategy, characterizing the boundaries of conceptual categories by asking about implausible category members (e.g., "Is an olive a vehicle?") to probe the kind of concept-level knowledge we take for granted in fellow humans. We characterize concept boundaries for a set of fundamental concepts by studying AI systems' assignments of objects to superordinate categories from a classic psychological study by Rosch and Mervis, as well as their assignments of the same objects to mismatched superordinate categories. We compare these assignments to those made by human participants on the full range of within-category and cross-category assignment tasks. Our results reveal a range of concepts for which which models differ in meaningful and surprising ways from humans, including treating "words" as belonging to categories like "vehicles" and "clothing," identifying several "vegetable" category members as "fruit," and assigning exemplars from non-weapon categories to the "weapons" category. We also demonstrate how these instances of concept misalignment translate into problematic downstream behavior with implications for AI safety.

</details>


### [229] [The Impact of AI Usage and Informativeness on Skill Development in Logical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21695)
*Shang Wu,Hongyu Yao,Catarina Belem,Shuyuan Fu,Mark Steyvers,Padhraic Smyth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了AI辅助对人类逻辑推理技能发展的影响，发现重度使用AI会削弱技能发展，而轻度使用则无显著负面影响。AI的信息量是关键调节因素——低信息量AI无益于学习和即时表现，高信息量AI可提升短期表现但效果因人而异。研究强调需要合理监管AI使用以促进技能发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能正越来越多地被整合到人类问题解决过程中，但其对个体技能发展的影响尚不明确。

Method: 在受控的逻辑推理任务中设置按需AI辅助访问，考察AI使用程度和AI信息量两个维度对学习效果的影响。

Result: 重度AI用户表现弱于对照组，轻度AI用户表现与未使用AI的匹配用户相当。低信息量AI既不能改善即时表现，也不能在移除AI后保持表现，且与整体较弱的学习效果相关。高信息量AI可提升短期表现但不平均降低移除AI后的表现，但存在异质性效应。

Conclusion: AI对人类技能发展的影响取决于具体情境：可能通过放大独立推理来互补促进技能发展，也可能作为替代品削弱推理能力。研究结果表明，在AI辅助环境下，监管AI访问和使用对促进技能发展至关重要。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is being increasingly integrated into human problem-solving, yet its effects on individual skill development remain unclear. We examine how both AI usage and informativeness can shape learning in the context of a controlled logical reasoning task with on-demand access to AI assistance. We find that greater AI usage is associated with weaker skill development: heavy AI users underperform relative to comparable peers, whereas light AI users perform similarly to matched users who do not use AI. We also find in our study that these patterns are mediated by AI informativeness. Low-information AI neither improves immediate performance nor preserves performance after AI assistance is removed, and is linked to weaker learning overall. On the other hand, high-information AI was found to improve short-run performance without reducing post-AI outcomes on average in our experiments, but with heterogeneous effects. Our findings in general suggest that AI can, depending on context, either complement human skill development by amplifying independent reasoning or can act as a substitute that undermines such reasoning, with the implication that regulating AI access and usage will be important for promoting skill development in the presence of AI assistance.

</details>


### [230] [AI-Enabled Serious Games: Integrating Intelligence and Adaptivity in Training Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21962)
*Priyamvada Tripathi,Bill Kapralos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了AI技术在严肃游戏中实现实时教学适配的潜力与挑战。核心贡献在于区分"教学智能"与"适应性"概念，回顾自适应学习系统从CAI到AI赋能架构的演进，探讨LLM、强化学习和智能体架构的应用前景，并指出可解释性验证和长期学习效果实证研究等开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 严肃游戏面临静态场景设计、创作瓶颈、学习者建模不足及实时教学适配困难等持久挑战，AI技术的进展可能提供新的解决路径。

Method: 历史综述法，追溯从早期CAI到ITS、DDA、创作平台、学习分析，再到近期AI赋能架构的演进脉络；区分了"教学智能"（推断学习者知识并推理教学响应）与"适应性"（交互中修改教学行为）的概念。

Result: 明确了AI赋能严肃游戏的理论基础与技术路径，同时识别出可解释性、验证、计算成本及长期学习效果实证不足等关键挑战。

Conclusion: AI技术（LLM、强化学习、基于智能体的架构）有望实现更深度整合的智能与适应性，但需解决可解释性、验证、计算成本及长期学习效果的实证研究不足等挑战。

Abstract: Serious games are widely used for learning and training across domains such as healthcare, defense, and education. Persistent challenges remain, however, including static scenario design, authoring bottlenecks, limited learner modeling, and difficulty implementing meaningful real-time instructional adaptation. Recent advances in artificial intelligence (AI) introduce novel capabilities such as dynamic scenario variation, contextual feedback, adaptive pacing, and learner-state modeling that may help address some of these limitations. At the same time, integrating AI into serious games raises important questions related to validity, transparency, system control, and learner trust. This chapter examines how contemporary AI approaches may support real-time instructional adaptation in serious games. It distinguishes between instructional intelligence, defined as a system's capacity to infer learner knowledge and reason about pedagogically appropriate responses, and adaptivity, defined as the ability to modify instructional actions during interaction. A historical synthesis of adaptive learning systems is presented, tracing developments from early computer-assisted instruction through intelligent tutoring systems (ITS), dynamic difficulty adjustment (DDA), authoring platforms, learning analytics, and recent AI-enabled architectures. Building on this perspective, the chapter discusses how large language models (LLMs), reinforcement learning (RL), and agent-based architectures may contribute to more integrated forms of intelligence and adaptivity in serious games. It also highlights practical and research challenges associated with AI-enabled systems, including explainability, validation, computational cost, and the limited empirical evidence regarding long-term learning outcomes in AI-enabled serious games.

</details>


### [231] [Latent-space Attacks for Refusal Evasion in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21706)
*Giorgio Piras,Raffaele Mura,Fabio Brau,Maura Pintor,Luca Oneto,Fabio Roli,Battista Biggio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将语言模型拒绝行为抑制重新解释为潜在空间规避攻击，提出了可控潜在空间规避攻击(CLE)，通过优化置信度将表示投影到决策边界之外的遵从区域，在15种模型上达到最先进的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法缺乏对潜在空间变换的原理性解释及其抑制拒绝行为的机制；同时，规避攻击仅停在决策边界，需要将表示进一步推入遵从区域。

Method: 将拒绝抑制重新建模为针对线性探测器的潜在空间规避攻击。传统方法的差异均值方向自然定义了这样的探测器，其消融等同于向决策边界投影，即最小置信度规避攻击。新方法CLE通过优化置信度将表示投影到决策边界之外，进入遵从区域。

Result: 在15种指令微调、多模态和推理模型上实现最先进的攻击成功率，优于现有拒绝消融基线和专门的越狱攻击。

Conclusion: 提出了一种新的攻击方法CLE，在15种模型上实现了最先进的攻击成功率，超越了现有的拒绝消融基线和专门的越狱攻击。

Abstract: Safety-aligned language models are trained to refuse harmful requests, yet refusal behavior can be suppressed by steering their internal representations. Existing methods do so by ablating a refusal direction from model activations, aiming to remove refusal from the model's residual stream. Despite their empirical success, these methods lack a principled account of the latent-space transformation they induce and why it suppresses refusal. In this work, we recast refusal suppression as a latent-space evasion attack against linear probes trained to separate refused from answered prompts. Under this view, prior work's difference-in-means direction naturally defines such a probe, and its ablation is exactly a projection onto its decision boundary, i.e., a minimum-confidence evasion attack. This perspective not only explains the empirical success of prior work but also admits a key limitation: evasion stops at the decision boundary, motivating the need to push representations further into the compliant region, i.e., where the model answers. We leverage this by proposing a Controlled Latent-space Evasion attack that projects representations past the boundary with an optimized confidence. We achieve state-of-the-art attack success rate across 15 instruction-tuned, multimodal, and reasoning models, outperforming existing refusal-ablation baselines and specialized jailbreak attacks.

</details>


### [232] [Trace2Skill: Verifier-Guided Skill Evolution for Long-Context EDA Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21810)
*Zijian Du,Nathaniel Pinckney*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Trace2Skill是一个测试时扩展框架，通过挖掘rollout轨迹生成任务特定skills和密集verifier反馈来提升硬件LLM agent，无需模型微调即可在复杂Verilog设计问题中实现突破性性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CVDP挑战要求LLM agent在大型代码库中定位verifier相关RTL、testbench、include路径和构建依赖，进行精确编辑，并从稀疏的隐藏verifier失败中恢复——这对现有硬件LLM agent构成重大挑战。

Method: 通过oracle、mutator和selector循环，从rollout轨迹中提取成功/失败模式，生成密集诊断和oracle lesson，进而生成任务特定skills来指导搜索、编辑、验证和恢复；同时引入有界运行时密集verifier反馈机制，返回经清理的功能观察而隐藏reference solution和harness。

Result: 显著提升了CVDP任务的通过率，包括那些同时击败前沿编码agent的困难任务，无需高质量微调数据、RTL专项模型训练或模型权重更新。

Conclusion: Trace2Skill无需模型权重更新或RTL专项微调即可显著提升硬件LLM agent的任务通过率，在CVDP基准测试中实现了突破性进展，并提供了一种可扩展至其他可验证EDA任务的通用测试时扩展策略。

Abstract: Complex Verilog Design Problems (CVDP) challenge hardware LLM agents because solving them requires localizing verifier-relevant RTL, testbenches, include paths, and build dependencies inside large repository snapshots, making precise edits, and recovering from sparse hidden-verifier failures. We present Trace2Skill, a test-time scaling framework that improves a hardware agent without RTL-specialized model fine-tuning. Rather than training a new model or only sampling more candidate solutions, Trace2Skill treats the agent's natural-language skill as an evolvable policy. It mines repeated rollout traces for success and failure modes, converts them into dense diagnostics and oracle lessons, and uses an oracle, mutator, and selector loop to produce task-specific skills that guide later search, editing, validation, and recovery. Because final pass/fail labels are often too coarse for hard failures, Trace2Skill also supports bounded runtime dense verifier feedback that returns sanitized functional observations while keeping hidden harnesses and reference solutions inaccessible to the agent. This feedback helps guide skill evolution and agent execution by connecting skill text, verifier evidence, and downstream behavior. Across hard CVDP tasks that defeat the seed CVDP agent, including tasks that also defeat frontier coding agents, Trace2Skill with dense verifier feedback substantially improves task pass rates and produces breakthrough passes on previously unsolved tasks, without requiring high-quality fine-tuning data, specialized RTL model training, or model weight updates. The same framework provides a general test-time scaling strategy that can extend beyond digital design to other verifiable EDA tasks.

</details>


### [233] [Perception or Prejudice: Can MLLMs Go Beyond First Impressions of Personality?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22109)
*Caixin Kang,Tianyu Yan,Sitong Gong,Mingfang Zhang,Liangyang Ouyang,Ruicong Liu,Bo Zheng,Huchuan Lu,Kaipeng Zhang,Yoichi Sato,Yifei Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了"接地人格推理"（GPR）新任务范式，配套发布MM-OCEAN数据集（含1104视频、5320 MCQ），并通过三层次评估框架和四种失败模式指标揭示了MLLMs在人格感知中的"偏见鸿沟"——模型能给出正确评分却无法提供行为证据支撑，表明当前多模态模型缺乏真正的社会认知推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs人格评估基准仅关注Big Five数值预测，无法区分模型是真正通过行为理解感知人格还是仅依赖表面模式匹配进行预判。这导致即使模型评分准确，其人格推理过程可能缺乏可解释性和可信度。

Method: 研究团队构建了三层评估体系（评分→推理→ grounding）和四种失败模式指标（PR、CR、IR、HR），对27个MLLMs进行了系统测评。通过多智能体pipeline生成结合人类验证的数据集，包含带时间戳的行为观察和证据锚定的人格分析。

Result: 27个MLLMs（13个闭源+14个开源）评测结果显示：51%的正确评分未被检索到的线索所支撑；整体接地率（Holistic-Grounding Rate）仅为0-33.5%，暴露出模型普遍存在"以正确理由得出正确结论"的能力缺陷。

Conclusion: MM-OCEAN benchmark揭示了当前MLLMs在人格感知中存在严重的"偏见鸿沟"——即使给出正确评分，也往往缺乏行为证据支撑。这项研究为MLLMs的社会认知能力评估提供了新范式，强调从单纯的分数预测转向可解释的推理过程，对未来多模态人格推理系统的可信度提升具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed in human-facing roles where personality perception is critical, yet existing benchmarks evaluate this capability solely on numerical Big Five score prediction, leaving open whether models truly perceive personality through behavioral understanding or merely prejudge through superficial pattern matching. We address this gap with three contributions. (i) A new task: we formalize Grounded Personality Reasoning (GPR), which requires MLLMs to anchor each Big Five rating in observable evidence through a chain of rating, reasoning, and grounding. (ii) A new dataset: we release MM-OCEAN (1,104 videos, 5,320 MCQs), produced by a multi-agent pipeline with human verification, with timestamped behavioral observations, evidence-grounded trait analyses, and seven categories of cue-grounding MCQs. (iii) Benchmark and analysis: we design a three-tier evaluation (rating, reasoning, grounding) plus four sample-level failure-mode metrics: Prejudice Rate (PR), Confabulation Rate (CR), Integration-failure Rate (IR), and Holistic-grounding Rate (HR), and benchmark 27 MLLMs (13 closed, 14 open). The analysis uncovers a striking Prejudice Gap: across the field, 51% of correct ratings are not grounded in retrieved cues, and the Holistic-Grounding Rate spans only 0-33.5%. These findings expose a disconnect between getting the right score and reasoning for the right reason, charting a roadmap for grounded social cognition in MLLMs.

</details>


### [234] [AttuneBench: A Conversation-Based Benchmark for LLM Emotional Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21739)
*Kate M. Lubrano,Faisal Sayed,Ankita Rathod,Akshansh,Craver Corbyn Thomas-Smith,Mark E. Whiting,Karina Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AttuneBench基准，用于评估大语言模型的情感智能。该基准基于200个真实的多轮人机对话，涵盖11个模型。核心发现是：情感智能行为分解为可分离的能力，且偏好对齐和响应质量判断比情感标签准确率更具模型区分度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing EI benchmarks rely on synthetic prompts, single-turn cases, or third-party annotation, which do not directly measure how models infer and respond to a participant's emotional state over the course of a real conversation. As LLMs increasingly assume conversational roles in everyday life, accurately assessing their emotional intelligence becomes crucial.

Method: The paper introduces AttuneBench, a benchmark grounded in 200 genuine multi-turn human-model conversations where participants conversed with anonymized LLMs and provided turn-by-turn annotations of their emotional state, the model's behavior, and their preferred responses. The authors evaluate 11 models on emotion recognition, behavioral classification, preference prediction, and judged response quality.

Result: Across 11 evaluated models, model rankings on emotion recognition, behavioral classification, preference prediction, and judged response quality are largely independent, indicating that emotionally intelligent behavior decomposes into separable capabilities. Preference alignment and response-quality judgments are substantially more model-discriminating than emotion-label accuracy.

Conclusion: AttuneBench provides a framework for assessing emotional intelligence capabilities and diagnosing model-specific strengths and failure modes in emotionally salient conversation. The benchmark reveals that emotionally intelligent behavior requires predicting what kind of response a specific user wants in context—a capability that aggregate scoring obscures and single-turn/synthetic formats cannot directly capture across turns.

Abstract: Emotional intelligence (EI), the ability to perceive, understand, and respond appropriately to others' emotional states, is central to human communication, and increasingly important to assess as LLMs assume conversational roles in everyday life. Existing EI benchmarks rely on synthetic prompts, single-turn cases, or third-party annotation. These approaches do not directly measure how models infer and respond to a participant's emotional state over the course of a real conversation. We introduce AttuneBench, a benchmark grounded in 200 genuine multi-turn human-model conversations in which participants conversed with anonymized LLMs and provided turn-by-turn annotations of their emotional state, the model's behavior, and their preferred responses. Across 11 evaluated models, we find that model rankings on emotion recognition, behavioral classification, preference prediction, and judged response quality are largely independent, indicating that emotionally intelligent behavior decomposes into separable capabilities. Preference alignment and response-quality judgments are substantially more model-discriminating than emotion-label accuracy. These results indicate that emotionally intelligent behavior requires predicting what kind of response a specific user wants in context, a distinction that aggregate scoring can obscure and that single-turn or synthetic formats cannot directly capture across turns. AttuneBench provides a framework for assessing each of these capabilities and for diagnosing model-specific strengths and failure modes in emotionally salient conversation.

</details>


### [235] [Search-E1: Self-Distillation Drives Self-Evolution in Search-Augmented Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22511)
*Zihan Liang,Yufei Ma,Ben Chen,Zhipeng Qian,Xuxin Zhang,Huangyu Dai,Lingtao Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Search-E1提出了一种简洁的搜索增强推理自进化方法，仅通过vanilla GRPO与离线自蒸馏即可超越需要复杂外部机制的基线，证明复杂增强机制并非必需。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前后训练Pipeline通常需要引入复杂外部机制（更强的外部系统监督、过程奖励模型、回溯评论器、树搜索、多阶段课程、手工设计的奖励等）来提升搜索增强推理Agent的性能。这些增强虽然带来可衡量的提升，但显著增加了训练Pipeline复杂度，并依赖于未必可用的资源或设计。因此有必要探索这些复杂机制是否真正必要。

Method: Search-E1采用自进化范式，仅依赖vanilla GRPO与离线自蒸馏(OFSD)的结合。具体流程为：每轮GRPO后，策略在训练问题上进行rollout；然后利用token级前向KL目标，将推理时分布对齐到特权上下文下更高效兄弟轨迹的自身分布。这种方式天然地提供了稀疏的每步监督信号。

Result: 在7个QA基准上，Search-E1使用Qwen2.5-3B达到0.440的平均EM，超越了所有同规模的开源基线模型。

Conclusion: Search-E1通过极简设计（仅用GRPO+离线自蒸馏）证明了搜索增强推理不需要复杂的外部监督或辅助模块，为构建高效推理Agent提供了一条简洁可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Post-training has become the dominant recipe for turning a language model into a competent search-augmented reasoning agent. A line of recent work pushes its performance further by adding elaborate machinery on top of this standard pipeline. These augmentations import external supervision from stronger external systems, attach auxiliary modules such as process reward models or retrospective critics, restructure the rollout itself with tree search or multi-stage curricula, or shape the reward with hand-crafted bonuses and penalties. Each addition delivers a measurable gain, but each also inflates the training pipeline and ties the recipe to resources or designs that may not always be available. We take a step back and ask whether any of this machinery is actually necessary, and propose Search-E1, a self-evolution method that lets a search-augmented agent improve through only vanilla GRPO interleaved with offline self-distillation (OFSD). After each GRPO round, the policy rolls out on its own training questions. A token-level forward KL objective then aligns the policy's inference-time distribution to its own distribution under a privileged context that exposes a more efficient sibling trajectory. Despite this simplicity, the procedure naturally provides dense per-step supervision. On seven QA benchmarks, Search-E1 reaches $0.440$ average EM with Qwen2.5-3B, surpassing all open-source baselines at both scales. Code and complete version will be made public soon.

</details>


### [236] [Epicure: Navigating the Emergent Geometry of Food Ingredient Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22391)
*Jakub Radzikowski,Josef Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Epicure是一个多语言食材嵌入模型家族，从头训练于包含7种语言、11个来源、414万菜谱的多语种语料库，通过LLM辅助流程将食材规范化为1,790个标准条目。基于食材共现图（NPMI）和食材-化合物类型图（FlavorDB），训练了三种Metapath2Vec变体，通过不同的随机游走模式在化学特性与烹饪上下文之间取得不同平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing ingredient embeddings lack multilingual scope, unified canonicalization, and fail to balance chemical properties with recipe context. There's a need for embeddings that capture both domain-specific knowledge (chemistry/flavor) and cooking/co-occurrence patterns across diverse culinary traditions.

Method: 1) Aggregated 4.14M multilingual recipes (7 languages, 11 sources); 2) LLM-augmented pipeline normalized ingredients to 1,790 canonical entries; 3) Built two graphs: 203,508-edge ingredient-ingredient NPMI co-occurrence graph and 80,019-edge typed ingredient-compound graph; 4) Trained three Metapath2Vec variants (Cooc, Chem, Core) sharing architecture/hyperparameters but differing in random-walk schema—Cooc uses co-occurrence only, Chem uses compound metapaths only, Core blends both via controlled mixing.

Result: Developed Epicure, a family of three sibling skip-gram ingredient embeddings retrained on a large multilingual recipe corpus, positioned at different points on the chemistry-vs-recipe-context spectrum.

Conclusion: The abstract does not explicitly state a conclusion; however, the paper demonstrates that different random-walk schemas place models at distinct positions on the chemistry-vs-recipe-context spectrum.

Abstract: We present Epicure, a family of three sibling skip-gram ingredient embeddings retrained from scratch on a multilingual recipe corpus. We aggregate 4.14M recipes from 11 sources spanning seven languages, English, Chinese, Russian, Vietnamese, Spanish, Turkish, Indonesian, German, and Indian-English, and normalise the raw ingredient strings to 1,790 canonical entries via an LLM-augmented pipeline. A 203,508-edge ingredient-ingredient NPMI graph and an 80,019-edge typed FlavorDB ingredient-compound graph, 2,247 typed compound nodes across 15 categories, seed three Metapath2Vec variants that share architecture and hyperparameters and differ only in the random-walk schema: Cooc walks the co-occurrence graph only, Chem walks the typed compound metapaths only, and Core blends both via injected ingredient-ingredient walks at controlled mixing, placing each model at a distinct point on the chemistry-vs-recipe-context spectrum.

</details>


### [237] [The Log is the Agent: Event-Sourced Reactive Graphs for Auditable, Forkable Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21997)
*Yohei Nakajima*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ActiveGraph将append-only event log确立为唯一真相来源，工作图为log的确定性投影，组件通过共享图协调而非直接指令，由此获得：确定性重放任意运行、任意事件点廉价分叉、端到端因果血缘追溯三大核心能力，为自我改进型智能体提供了坚实基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统智能体框架以语言模型和对话循环为核心，工具、规则和可观测性层都是事后追加的，状态以检索/摘要式"记忆"持久化。这种架构存在三个根本缺陷：无法确定性重放任意运行、无法廉价地分叉运行轨迹、无法追溯从高层目标到具体模型调用的完整因果链。

Method: 倒置传统架构设计：以append-only event log为真相之源，工作图为log的确定性投影；行为组件（函数、类、LLM例程或类型化边逻辑）响应图变化并发出新事件，组件间无直接指令关系，完全通过共享图协调；通过确定性与Replay合约确保重放的正确性。

Result: 成功设计并实现了ActiveGraph运行时架构，提出了保证重放正确性的确定性合约，通过尽职调查示例完整演示了如何从日志alone重建完整因果结构。理论上论证了该架构对自我改进型智能体的独特优势。

Conclusion: ActiveGraph通过将append-only event log作为唯一真相来源，实现了确定性重放、廉价分叉和端到端血缘追溯三大能力，为自我改进型智能体提供了理想基底，是BabyAGI和相关图记忆研究的重要扩展。

Abstract: Most agent frameworks are built around the language model: a conversation loop comes first, then tools, then rules, and finally a logging layer bolted on for observability, with state persisted as retrievable "memory." We describe ActiveGraph, a runtime that inverts this arrangement. The append-only event log is the source of truth; the working graph is a deterministic projection of that log; and behaviors--ordinary functions, classes, LLM-backed routines, or logic attached to typed edges--react to changes in the graph and emit new events. No component instructs another; coordination happens entirely through the shared graph. This single design decision yields three properties that retrieval-and-summarization memory systems do not provide: deterministic replay of any run from its log, cheap forking that branches a run at any event without re-executing the shared prefix, and end-to-end lineage from a high-level goal down to the individual model call that produced each artifact. We present the architecture, a determinism contract that makes replay sound, and a worked diligence example whose full causal structure is reconstructable from the log alone. We discuss--without claiming to demonstrate--why this substrate is unusually well suited to self-improving agents, and how it extends the BabyAGI lineage and prior graph-memory research.

</details>


### [238] [Who Uses AI? Platforms, Workforce, and AI Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21743)
*Michelle Yin,Burhan Ogut*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了AI平台对话日志测量职业暴露度的关键方法论缺陷：平台用户基础与实际劳动力结构不匹配导致严重的非经典测量误差，使就业弹性估计大幅偏误。同一估计框架下仅更换数据来源即可使结果相差近两倍，且消费者与企业渠道估计结果相互矛盾，表明现有研究结论的可靠性存疑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学界日益广泛采用AI平台对话日志来测度职业暴露度，但这些测度指标究竟反映的是平台用户群体特征还是真实劳动力结构，目前尚无定论。这种测量的有效性直接影响AI对劳动力市场影响评估的可靠性。

Method: 通过固定outcome、样本、控制变量和估计量，仅改变平台输入变量进行敏感性分析；比较同厂商消费者与企业渠道的估计差异；使用BLS劳动力份额进行重新加权调整；建立非经典测量误差的正式框架并推导概率极限和就业弹性的部分识别边界。

Result: 仅更换平台输入数据可使ChatGPT后就业系数变化1.9倍；同厂商消费者与企业渠道估计结果符号相悖；按BLS劳动力份额重新加权后估计值衰减42%至93%。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了AI平台对话日志测量方法存在系统性偏误，平台用户基础与实际劳动力结构存在显著差异，导致就业弹性估计严重失真。偏差特征表现为低估替代效应超过低估增强效应。

Abstract: A growing literature uses artificial intelligence platform conversation logs to measure occupation exposure. We show that these scores partly measure platform user base rather than the workforce. Holding outcome, sample, controls, and estimator fixed while varying only the platform input changes the post-ChatGPT employment coefficient by a factor of 1.9, and within-vendor consumer-versus-enterprise channels produce estimates that disagree in sign. Reweighting to Bureau of Labor Statistics workforce shares attenuates estimates by 42 to 93 percent. We formalize the non-classical measurement error, derive probability limits and partial-identification bounds for employment elasticities. The bias understates substitution more than augmentation.

</details>


### [239] [A Causal Argumentation Method for Explainability of Machine Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21758)
*Henry Salgado,Meagan R. Kendall,Martine Ceberio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合因果推理与论证框架的可解释AI方法，通过因果发现识别变量关系，将其映射为双极论证框架，利用半稳定语义找到可解释决策的特征扩展，从而揭示模型做出特定预测的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI方法只能识别相关特征，但无法解释模型为何做出特定决策，即缺乏对决策原因的因果推理能力。

Method: 1）使用因果发现方法识别变量间的因果关系；2）将因果关系转化为双极论证框架（BAF），表示特征间的支持与反对交互；3）利用半稳定语义寻找解释特定结果的特征扩展集。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上验证了方法有效性，并与标准事后解释方法进行了对比。

Conclusion: 实验表明该方法在两个基准数据集上有效，相比标准事后可解释性方法能提供更深入的解释。

Abstract: Explainable AI (XAI) methods identify which features are relevant to a model's predictions but often fail to clarify why certain decisions are made. In this work, we present a novel method that integrates causality with argument-based reasoning to explain why models may be making predictions. Our approach first identifies causal relationships among variables using causal discovery methods and then translates these into a Bipolar Argumentation Framework (BAF) to represent supportive and opposing interactions among features. By using semi-stable semantics, we find extensions of features that explain why certain outcomes may have been chosen. We demonstrate our method on two benchmark datasets and compare its results against standard post-hoc explainability approaches.

</details>


### [240] [LCGuard: Latent Communication Guard for Safe KV Sharing in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22786)
*Sadia Asif,Mohammad Mohammadi Amiri,Momin Abbas,Prasanna Sattigeri,Karthikeyan Natesan Ramamurthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LCGuard是一个用于多智能体LLM系统中安全KV潜伏通信的框架，通过表示级转换防止敏感信息泄漏，同时保留任务相关语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统通过KV缓存进行潜伏通信虽能提高效率并保留任务相关信息，但KV缓存会编码上下文输入、中间推理状态和智能体特定信息，形成不透明通道，敏感内容可能在没有显式文本披露的情况下跨智能体传播。

Method: 将共享KV缓存视为潜伏工作记忆，在缓存跨智能体传输前学习表示级转换；采用对抗训练方法，敌手学习重建敏感输入，而LCGuard学习保留任务相关语义同时减少可重建信息的转换。

Result: 实验评估表明LCGuard在多个模型家族和多智能体基准测试中一致性地降低了基于重建的泄漏和攻击成功率，同时相比标准KV共享基线保持了具有竞争力的任务性能。

Conclusion: LCGuard为多智能体LLM系统中的KV潜伏通信提供了安全保障，通过在共享缓存传输前学习表示级转换，实现安全性与任务性能的平衡。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems increasingly rely on intermediate communication to coordinate complex tasks. While most existing systems communicate through natural language, recent work shows that latent communication, particularly through transformer key-value (KV) caches, can improve efficiency and preserve richer task-relevant information. However, KV caches also encode contextual inputs, intermediate reasoning states, and agent-specific information, creating an opaque channel through which sensitive content may propagate across agents without explicit textual disclosure. To address this, we introduce \textbf{LCGuard} (Latent Communication Guard), a framework for safe KV-based latent communication in multi-agent LLM systems. LCGuard treats shared KV caches as latent working memory and learns representation-level transformations before cache artifacts are transmitted across agents. We formalize representation-level sensitive information leakage operationally through reconstruction: a shared cache artifact is unsafe if an adversarial decoder can recover agent-specific sensitive inputs from it. This leads to an adversarial training formulation in which the adversary learns to reconstruct sensitive inputs, while LCGuard learns transformations that preserve task-relevant semantics and reduce reconstructable information. Empirical evaluations across multiple model families and multi-agent benchmarks show that LCGuard consistently reduces reconstruction-based leakage and attack success rates while maintaining competitive task performance compared to standard KV-sharing baselines.

</details>


### [241] [What Counts as AI Sycophancy? A Taxonomy and Expert Survey of a Fragmented Construct](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21778)
*Meryl Ye,Lujain Ibrahim,Jessica Y. Bo,Myra Cheng,Ida Mattsson,Daniel Vennemeyer,Robert Kraut,Steve Rathje*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究针对AI sycophancy概念缺乏统一定义的问题，通过文献综述（70篇论文）和专家调查（106位专家）建立了二维行为分类体系，揭示了该领域研究的空白和专家共识的缺乏，为后续研究和治理提供了共同词汇框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI sycophancy目前缺乏一致的定义，不同研究用同一术语描述不同行为（如：同意用户错误观点、过度赞美用户、隐瞒纠正性反馈等），导致评估结果难以比较、缓解策略无法迁移、系统对一种形式的sycophancy免疫但仍表现出其他形式。

Method: 1. 系统文献综述：回顾70篇AI sycophancy相关论文，建立行为分类体系
2. 专家调查：调研106位AI sycophancy及相关领域专家，评估研究界对sycophancy界定的共识程度

Result: 1. 建立二维taxonomy：
   - 维度一：模型迎合的对象（用户立场/信念 vs 用户个人特质/情感）
   - 维度二：迎合的表现形式（显性/直接语言 vs 隐性/微妙行为如框架设置、遗漏、音调）
2. 发现研究空白：现有研究集中于显性sycophancy，对用户信念的迎合研究较多，对更隐性、面向个人的行为研究不足
3. 专家调查发现：94.3%专家认为sycophancy是当前AI系统的重要问题，但对具体哪些行为属于sycophancy存在显著分歧

Conclusion: AI sycophancy是一个包含多种行为类型的广泛概念，不同形式需要不同的测量方法、干预策略和治理方案。研究者的taxonomy为理解和解决这些行为提供了共同词汇基础。

Abstract: AI sycophancy has become a prominent concern in large language model (LLM) research. Yet the term lacks a consistent definition and has been applied to behaviors ranging from agreeing with a user's false claim to excessively praising the user to withholding corrective feedback. When researchers, companies, and policymakers use the same term to describe different behaviors, evaluation results become difficult to compare, mitigation strategies fail to transfer, and systems that are resistant to one form of sycophancy continue exhibiting other forms. To address this, we make two contributions. First, we reviewed 70 papers on AI sycophancy to develop a taxonomy of how the behavior has been defined and measured. The taxonomy distinguishes (1) whether a model is sycophantic toward a user's positions and beliefs, or toward the user's broader personal traits and emotions, and (2) whether this occurs through explicit, direct language or more implicit, subtle behaviors such as framing, omission, or tone. Mapping existing literature to our taxonomy reveals that current research has focused on overt forms of sycophancy toward users' beliefs, leaving more subtle and person-directed behaviors relatively understudied. Second, we surveyed 106 experts in AI sycophancy and related fields to examine whether researchers agree on which model behaviors are sycophantic. While experts are nearly unanimous in believing that sycophancy is a significant problem in current AI systems (94.3% agree), they disagree substantially on which specific behaviors qualify. Together, these findings demonstrate that AI sycophancy is a broad family of behaviors with different measurement challenges, intervention requirements, and governance implications. Our taxonomy provides a shared vocabulary for understanding and addressing these behaviors.

</details>


### [242] [Implicit Safety Alignment from Crowd Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21822)
*Qian Lin,Daniel S. Brown*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Safe Crowd Preference-based RL方法，从人群偏好中层级化提取安全技能并组合用于下游任务，在无显式安全奖励下实现了低安全成本和高效任务完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF能够揭示超越任务完成的安全考虑，但人群偏好数据中不同用户虽有不同偏好或目标，却遵循相似安全原则。当前方法如直接结合偏好奖励模型与任务奖励存在固有局限性，因此需要从人群偏好中发现共享安全标准并转移到下游RL任务中。

Method: 提出Safe Crowd Preference-based RL框架，采用层级结构：1) 从人群偏好数据中提取安全对齐技能；2) 通过高层策略组合这些技能以安全地解决下游任务。该方法避免了直接结合偏好学习奖励模型与下游任务奖励的简单做法。

Result: 在安全RL环境和LLM风格任务的实验中，该方法显著降低了安全成本（无需访问显式安全奖励），同时实现了与使用真值安全信号的oracle方法相当的任务性能。

Conclusion: Safe Crowd Preference-based RL通过从人群偏好中提取安全对齐技能并通过高层策略组合它们，实现了无需显式安全奖励即可有效降低安全成本，同时保持与使用真实安全信号的oracle方法相当的任务性能。验证了从隐式人群偏好中学习安全原则的可行性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) can reveal implicit objectives such as safety considerations that go beyond task completion. In this work, we focus on the common safety criteria embedded in crowd preference datasets, where different users may express distinct preferences or objectives, yet follow similar safety principles. Our aim is to discover shared safety criteria from crowd preferences and then transfer them to downstream RL tasks to regularize agent behavior and enforce safety. We first show that direct reward combination-optimizing a preference-learned reward model together with downstream task rewards-has inherent limitations. Motivated by this, we propose Safe Crowd Preference-based RL, a hierarchical framework that extracts safety-aligned skills from crowd preferences and composes them via a high-level policy to safely solve downstream tasks. Experiments across safe RL environments and a preliminary LLM-style task with diverse user goals and shared safety constraints demonstrate that our approach substantially lowers safety costs without access to explicit safety rewards, while achieving task performance comparable to oracle methods trained with ground-truth safety signals.

</details>


### [243] [Toward AI VIS Co-Scientists: A General and End-to-End Agent Harness for Solving Complex Data Visualization Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21825)
*Haichao Miao,Zhimin Li,Kuangshi Ai,Kaiyuan Tang,Chaoli Wang,Peer-Timo Bremer,Shusen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: The ability to inspect, interpret, and communicate complex data is crucial for virtually any scientific endeavor, but often requires significant expertise outside the core domain ranging from data management and analysis to visualization design and implementation. We present an end-to-end agentic harness that, based on only the data and a high level description of the tasks, independently designs custom visual analysis applications (VIS apps). This represents an important step towards a general AI co-scientist envisioned by many as an autonomous system that can autonomously execute long horizon tasks based on high-level directions. Our proposed VIS co-scientist is an essential component of this broader AI co-scientist vision: a harness that can autonomously analyze data and design visualization solutions using a collection of agents and specialized skills that coordinate exploratory analysis, plan, configure the environment, implement, validate the interface, and most importantly evaluate the overall task completion. Each stage produces document and instruction artifacts that guide downstream work and enable iterative refinement. We validate this approach on IEEE SciVis Contests spanning multiple science and engineering fields. These contests serve as ideal proving grounds because they encode real-world complexity: ambiguous requirements, diverse data modalities, design trade-offs, and task-driven validation. Given only the data and target tasks, our system autonomously produces functional single-page VIS Apps with verified linked-view behavior, highly customized to domain experts' specified tasks and needs.

</details>


### [244] [FLUID: From Ephemeral IDs to Multimodal Semantic Codes for Industrial-Scale Livestreaming Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21832)
*Xinhang Yuan,Zexi Huang,Anjia Cao,Xudong Lu,Zikai Wang,Penghao Zhou,Chang Liu,Wentao Guo,Qinglei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This paper presents FLUID, an ID-free livestreaming recommendation framework that addresses the cold-start problem in traditional ID-based collaborative filtering by using multimodal encoders to generate hierarchical content codes (LUCID), achieving substantial online gains in watch duration, cold-start room visibility, and user engagement.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Livestreaming recommendations suffer from inherent temporal constraints—live rooms typically broadcast for only tens of minutes—resulting in item IDs remaining perpetually in a cold-start state. This causes ID-centric ranking models to fail in generalizing to new or short-lived content.

Method: FLUID combines: (1) A cross-domain multimodal encoder jointly trained on short videos and livestreams to generate discrete hierarchical codes (LUCID); (2) A late-fusion, ID-free design that uses slice-level and room-level LUCID as independent tokens; (3) A staged warmup mechanism with online incremental training for stabilization.

Result: The deployment of FLUID on industrial livestreaming platforms serving over one billion global users yielded significant improvements: +0.55% Quality Watch Duration, +2.05% Cold-Start Room Views, and +0.05% Active Hours.

Conclusion: FLUID represents a paradigm shift in livestreaming recommendation, being the first production-scale system to fully retire candidate-side item IDs from the ranking model.

Abstract: Modern recommender systems rely heavily on ID-based collaborative filtering: each item is represented by a unique ID embedding that accumulates collaborative signals from user interactions. Livestreaming recommendation, however, faces a unique challenge in this paradigm: a live room typically broadcasts for only tens of minutes, so its item ID remains poorly learned in a persistent cold-start state and ID-centric ranking models fail to generalize. We present FLUID, the first framework to fully retire the candidate-side item ID from a production-scale livestreaming ranker. FLUID couples a cross-domain multimodal encoder, jointly trained on short videos and livestreams to produce discrete hierarchical codes (LUCID), with a late-fusion, ID-free design that injects slice-level and room-level LUCID as independent tokens, stabilized by a staged warmup under online incremental training. Deployed on our industrial livestreaming recommenders with a cross-platform combined user base of over one billion globally, FLUID delivers significant online gains of +0.55% Quality Watch Duration, +2.05% Cold-Start Room Views, and +0.05% Active Hours.

</details>


### [245] [Planning in the LLM Era: Building for Reliability and Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21902)
*Michael Katz,Harsha Kokel,Kavitha Srinivas,Shirin Sohrabi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This position paper argues that LLM-based planning is shifting from unreliable single-shot/hybrid methods toward generating symbolic planners at construction time, enabling verified, efficient, and maintainable inference with minimal LLM dependency. The authors examine three categories of planner-generation methods and outline future research directions.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Early LLM planning methods (single-shot generation and hybrid approaches with external search) are unsound and incomplete, requiring substantial resources without yielding better solutions on unseen problems. There is a need for agents that are both reliable and resource-efficient.

Method: Reviews and categorizes three major planner-generation methods, examines their current limitations

Result: Provides a framework for understanding the evolution of LLM-based planning approaches and identifies directions for future research

Conclusion: Outlines research steps toward more reliable and efficient LLM-based planner generation

Abstract: Growing attention to intelligent agents has put a spotlight on one of their central capabilities: planning. Early attempts to leverage large language models (LLMs) for planning relied on single-shot plan generation, followed by hybrid approaches that coupled LLMs with limited external search. These methods, unsound and incomplete by their very nature, often require substantial resources without yielding better solutions on unseen problems. As the limitations of LLMs become clearer, recent work has shifted toward using them at solution construction time -- generating symbolic solvers for a family of problems that can be verified and then used efficiently at inference time. This trend reflects the growing need for agents that are both reliable and resource-efficient. It also offers a path towards generating maintainable planners with minimal dependence on language models at inference time. In this paper, we argue that this shift reflects a broader realignment of the planning field in the LLM era. We examine three major categories of planner-generation methods, discuss their current limitations, and outline research steps towards a more reliable and efficient LLM-based generation of planners.

</details>


### [246] [Format-Constraint Coupling in Knowledge Graph Construction from Statistical Tables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21974)
*Jingxuan Qi,Zhiqiang Ye,Yuxiang Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Schema-format mismatch causes "format-constraint coupling" that catastrophically degrades knowledge graph fidelity from statistical CSVs. A new benchmark (CSVFidelity-Bench) and direct graph access are needed for fidelity-aware evaluation.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Knowledge graph extraction from statistical CSV data is common on open-data portals, but the interaction between serialization format and schema constraints may reduce knowledge graph fidelity in unpredictable ways.

Method: 2x2 factorial design across 6 datasets; controlled experiments across format-schema pairings, GraphRAG hosts, and LLM families; probing and token ablation; bootstrap 95% CIs; comparison of three standard retrieval modes vs. direct graph access.

Result: Joint format-schema effects exceed independent effects by up to +1.180 (2x2 factorial). Schema applied to mismatched format causes "format-constraint coupling" — catastrophic mismatch with fact coverage falling below baseline on 4/6 datasets. Surface-form anchoring (column-name references) explains this phenomenon. Standard retrieval modes mask construction quality (delta <= 1pp), while direct graph access exposes gaps up to +47.6pp (p < 0.0001).

Conclusion: Format-constraint coupling is a real phenomenon that can severely degrade knowledge graph fidelity. Standard retrieval modes mask quality issues, but direct graph access exposes gaps up to +47.6pp. CSVFidelity-Bench (15 datasets, 1,892 Gold Standard facts) is released to support fidelity-aware evaluation.

Abstract: An extraction schema should not reduce knowledge graph fidelity. On statistical CSV, however, it can. We study country-by-year time-series matrices, a common layout on open-data portals. In this setting, serialization format and schema constraints interact super-additively. Their joint effect exceeds the sum of independent effects by up to +1.180 (2x2 factorial, 6 datasets). Bootstrap 95% CIs are strictly positive on 4/6 datasets, with strongest evidence on wide Type-II matrices. More critically, a schema applied to a mismatched format can trigger catastrophic mismatch. Fact coverage falls below the unconstrained baseline on 4/6 datasets through entity inflation or extraction refusal. We call this observed pattern format-constraint coupling. Probing and token ablation support a surface-form anchoring explanation centred on column-name references. Controlled variants across format-schema pairings, GraphRAG hosts, and LLM families show the same direction within the measured scope; one LLM family shows only partial activation. The observation also has a diagnostic consequence. Three standard retrieval modes largely mask construction quality (delta <= 1pp), whereas direct graph access exposes gaps up to +47.6pp (p < 0.0001). To support fidelity-aware evaluation, we release CSVFidelity-Bench. It contains 15 datasets, 11 Type-II matrices, 4 Type-III tables, and 1,892 Gold Standard facts across 6 domains.

</details>


### [247] [Active Evidence-Seeking and Diagnostic Reasoning in Large Language Models for Clinical Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22047)
*Chen Zhan,Xihe Qiu,Xiaoyu Tan,Xibing Zhuang,Gengchen Ma,Yue Zhang,Shuo Li,Peifeng Liu,Xiaoxiao Ge,Liang Liu,Lu Gan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过OSCE风格的模拟器发现，当前大语言模型在交互式诊断场景中的表现显著低于静态基准测试结果，主要受过早诊断闭合和低效提问策略影响，提示现有评估范式存在系统性高估问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在静态医学考试中表现良好，但实际临床诊断需要迭代式的不确定性证据收集过程，现有评估方法无法反映模型在动态交互场景下的真实能力。

Method: 引入OSCE（客观结构化临床考试）风格的标准化患者模拟器，建立了一个可控、可复现的主动诊断交互式基准测试，覆盖468个病例和15个模型。

Result: 多轮证据寻求相比全上下文评估，诊断准确率下降12.75%，支撑证据质量下降24.36%；错误主要与过早诊断闭合和低效提问相关。

Conclusion: 静态全上下文基准测试可能高估模型在实际临床证据收集场景中的表现，需要补充交互式评估方法来支持更安全的临床决策支持。

Abstract: Large language models perform well on static medical examinations, yet clinical diagnosis often requires iterative evidence gathering under uncertainty. Building on prior interactive evaluation efforts, we introduce an OSCE-inspired standardized patient simulator and a controlled, reproducible benchmark for active diagnostic inquiry. Across 468 cases and 15 models in our protocol, we observe that multi-turn evidence seeking reduces diagnostic accuracy by 12.75% and lowers supporting-evidence quality by 24.36% relative to full-context evaluation; error analyses associate these drops with premature diagnostic closure and inefficient questioning. Together, these results suggest that static full-context benchmarks may overestimate performance in interactive evidence-seeking settings, motivating complementary interactive assessment for safer clinical decision support.

</details>


### [248] [Enhancing Visual Token Representations for Video Large Language Models via Training-Free Spatial-Temporal Pooling and Gridding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22078)
*Bingjun Luo,Tony Wang,Hanqi Chen,Xinpeng Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ST-GridPool是一个无需训练的视觉token增强方法，通过金字塔时间网格化(PTG)捕获多粒度时空交互，并利用基于范数的空间池化(NSP)保留高信息量视觉区域，在多个基准测试中一致提升Video LLM性能，且无需重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing visual token compression methods in MLLMs (such as LLaVA family) use simplistic pooling or interpolation techniques that overlook the intricate dynamics of visual tokens, creating a gap between efficient compression and preservation of spatiotemporal interactions.

Method: The method combines two components: Pyramid Temporal Gridding (PTG) for capturing multi-grained spatiotemporal interactions through hierarchical temporal gridding, and Norm-based Spatial Pooling (NSP) for preserving high-information visual regions by leveraging the correlation between token norms and semantic richness.

Result: Extensive experiments on various benchmarks demonstrate that ST-GridPool consistently enhances performance of Video LLMs without requiring costly retraining.

Conclusion: ST-GridPool provides an efficient and plug-and-play solution for improving visual token representations in Video LLMs, offering a training-free approach that can be easily integrated into existing systems without costly retraining.

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have significantly advanced video understanding tasks, yet challenges remain in efficiently compressing visual tokens while preserving spatiotemporal interactions. Existing methods, such as LLaVA family, utilize simplistic pooling or interpolation techniques that overlook the intricate dynamics of visual tokens. To bridge this gap, we propose ST-GridPool, a novel training-free visual token enhancement method designed specifically for Video LLMs. Our approach integrates Pyramid Temporal Gridding (PTG), which captures multi-grained spatiotemporal interactions through hierarchical temporal gridding, and Norm-based Spatial Pooling (NSP), which preserves high-information visual regions by leveraging the correlation between token norms and semantic richness. Extensive experiments on various benchmarks demonstrate that ST-GridPool consistently enhances performance of Video LLMs without requiring costly retraining. Our method offers an efficient and plug-and-play solution for improving visual token representations. Our code is available in https://github.com/bingjunluo/ST-GridPool.

</details>


### [249] [A Camera-Cooperative ISAC Framework for Multimodal Non-Cooperative UAVs Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22090)
*Wenfeng Wu,Luping Xiang,Kun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对非合作无人机检测的资源竞争与感知精度问题，提出CC-ISAC多模态感知框架，通过V2EDA和MMFE两个模块实现视觉与回波域特征对齐及多模态融合估计，在降低波束导向和跟踪开销方面效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非合作式无人机检测对集成感知与通信(ISAC)系统构成重大挑战，主要源于单模态感知的固有局限性以及感知与通信资源之间的竞争关系。

Method: 提出Camera-Cooperative ISAC (CC-ISAC)框架，采用多模态感知策略。框架包含两个关键模块：(1) Vision-to-Echo Data Alignment (V2EDA)模型，通过交叉注意力机制对齐视觉和回波域特征；(2) Multimodal Fusion-Based Estimation (MMFE)模型，融合历史多模态数据与当前观测进行鲁棒状态估计。该框架利用摄像头进行粗粒度空域监控，ISAC进行细粒度高精度感知，形成互补感知环路。

Result: 在DeepSense 6G数据集上的评估表明，该框架实现平均71%的波束导向开销降低和1.69-11.15%的跟踪开销降低，同时保持高角度估计精度，有效缓解了感知与通信间的资源竞争。

Conclusion: CC-ISAC框架有效缓解了感知与通信之间的资源竞争，在释放系统资源用于额外通信任务的同时，实现了可靠的无人机监控，代表了ISAC系统设计中的实用进展。

Abstract: The detection of non-cooperative unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) presents significant challenges for Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC) systems due to the inherent limitations of single-modal perception and the competition for shared communication and sensing resources. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a novel Camera-Cooperative ISAC (CC-ISAC) framework that employs multimodal sensing to enable efficient UAV beam steering and tracking. The proposed framework employs cameras for coarse-grained airspace monitoring and utilizes ISAC for fine-grained, high-precision sensing, forming a complementary perception loop that enhances both sensing accuracy and resource efficiency. Within this framework, two key modules are developed: (1) a Vision-to-Echo Data Alignment (V2EDA) model that aligns visual and echo-domain features through cross-attention mechanisms, and (2) a Multimodal Fusion-Based Estimation (MMFE) model that integrates historical multimodal data with current observations for robust state estimation. Extensive evaluations conducted on the DeepSense 6G dataset demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves an average reduction of 71% in beam steering overhead and 1.69-11.15% in tracking overhead while maintaining high angular estimation accuracy. The CC-ISAC framework effectively mitigates resource contention between sensing and communication, enabling reliable UAV surveillance while freeing substantial system resources for additional communication tasks, thereby representing a practical advancement in ISAC system design.

</details>


### [250] [Knowledge Graph Re-engineering Along the Ontological Continuum (extended version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22093)
*Enrico Daga,Valentina Tamma,Terry Payne*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"本体论连续体"(ontological continuum)作为知识图谱(KG)领域的缺失概念框架。该框架基于两个正交维度——语义vs.语用、属性vs.功能——来描述、比较、导航和转换不同建模实践的知识图谱。采用经验主义方法论，通过形式概念分析(FCA)提供形式化结构，并通过溯源知识案例研究验证其实用性。文章最终提出五个开放研究挑战，呼吁学界将本体论连续体发展为共享研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The diversity of KG modeling practices (from lightweight vocabularies to richly axiomatized ontologies) makes integration and reuse expensive and brittle. This challenge is acute in neuro-symbolic AI where bridging neural and symbolic components requires reengineering KGs for new requirements. GenAI offers unprecedented automation but lacks principled understanding of the KG space.

Method: Introduces the "ontological continuum" theoretical construct with a characterization framework based on two orthogonal distinctions: (1) semantics vs. pragmatics, and (2) properties vs. affordances. Uses an empirical methodology derived from observing real-world KG engineering practices. Employs Formal Concept Analysis (FCA) for formal explicit structure. Demonstrated through a case study on provenance knowledge.

Result: Provides a vocabulary to describe, compare, navigate, and transform KGs across the full range of modeling practices. Demonstrates through a provenance knowledge case study how a single concern manifests differently across the continuum.

Conclusion: The ontological continuum offers a unifying conceptual framework with practical applications (demonstrated through provenance case study) and five open research challenges, positioning itself as a community research agenda for advancing KG engineering practices.

Abstract: Knowledge graphs have become the primary vehicle for data integration and are critical to the success of modern AI, but the diversity of KG modelling practices, from lightweight vocabularies to richly axiomatised ontologies, makes integration and reuse expensive and brittle. This challenge is particularly acute in neuro-symbolic AI, where bridging neural and symbolic components depends on the ability to reengineer KGs to fit new requirements; GenAI now offers unprecedented automation capability, but without a principled understanding of the KG space, such automation remains conceptually ungrounded. We introduce the ontological continuum as that missing conceptualisation, a theoretical construct a theoretical construct whose characterisation framework is defined by two orthogonal distinctions: semantics vs pragmatics, and properties vs affordances; together these define a vocabulary to describe, compare, navigate, and transform KGs across the full range of modelling practices. The methodological stance is empirical: rather than prescribing how KGs should be modelled, the continuum aims to define a theory of the existent, derived from observation of real-world KG engineering practices and whose structure can be made formally explicit, for example, through Formal Concept Analysis (FCA). We ground the vision through a case study on provenance knowledge, showing how a single concern manifests differently across the continuum. We articulate five open research challenges and invite the community to develop the ontological continuum as a shared research agenda.

</details>


### [251] [MPDocBench-Parse: Benchmarking Practical Multi-page Document Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22100)
*Bangbang Zhou,Hangdi Xing,Yifan Chen,Jianjun Xu,Qi Zheng,Feiyu Gao,Zhibo Yang,Shuai Bai,Ming Yan,Jieping Ye,Hongtao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MPDocBench-Parse是一个面向真实场景的多页文档解析基准测试，包含433份标注文档(3246页)，覆盖15种中英文文档类型，支持文档级端到端评估及文本/表格/公式/图表等内容的细粒度评估，为推动文档解析技术向实际应用迈进提供统一基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档解析基准测试存在三大不足：(1)仅聚焦特定任务或单页文本场景，缺乏多页文档评估；(2)缺乏语义连续性、层次结构恢复和视觉内容保留的细粒度评估；(3)难以支撑真实应用场景。

Method: 构建了包含433份文档、3246页的大型基准数据集，覆盖15种文档类型（中英文），支持文档级端到端评估；设计了全面的内容保真度和逻辑结构评估协议，涵盖文本/表格/公式识别、截断内容合并、图表提取、阅读顺序和标题层次结构恢复等任务。

Result: 实验表明，现有模型在基础文本提取上表现良好，但在语义连续性整合、视觉内容解析和层次结构恢复方面存在明显不足。

Conclusion: MPDocBench-Parse为推进文档解析向更真实场景发展提供了统一的基础，有望促进语义连续性集成、视觉内容解析和层次结构恢复等关键技术的研究突破。

Abstract: Document parsing converts visually rich documents into machine-readable structured representations, forming a crucial foundation for information systems. Although many benchmarks have been proposed for document parsing, they remain inadequate for realistic scenarios. Existing benchmarks either focus on specific tasks or assess only single-page, text-centric settings, making them insufficient for practical multi-page parsing. Moreover, they lack fine-grained evaluation of semantic continuity, hierarchical structure recovery, and visual content preservation. To address these gaps, we propose MPDocBench-Parse, a benchmark for multi-page document parsing in real-world applications. It contains 433 manually annotated documents with 3,246 pages, covering 15 document types in English and Chinese, with diverse layout styles, and supports document-level end-to-end evaluation. We further design a comprehensive protocol for content fidelity and logical structure, covering text, table, and formula recognition, truncated text and table merging, figure extraction, reading order, and heading hierarchy recovery. Experiments show that, while existing models perform well on basic text extraction, they still suffer clear limitations in semantic continuity integration, visual content parsing, and hierarchical structure recovery. MPDocBench-Parse provides a unified foundation for advancing document parsing toward more realistic scenarios.

</details>


### [252] [ExComm: Exploration-Stage Communication for Error-Resilient Agentic Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22102)
*Woomin Song,Beomjun Kim,Daewon Choi,Sai Muralidhar Jayanthi,Saket Dingliwal,Jinwoo Shin,Aram Galstyan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ExComm通过检测跨智能体事实冲突并使用软信念更新进行反馈来解决长时域推理中的错误传播问题，同时保留轨迹多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域智能体测试时扩展的一个常见失败模式是错误传播：中间步骤中引入的事实错误或无效推论会持续存在于智能体信念状态中并污染后续推理。现有测试时扩展方法对此过程的控制有限，因为它们依赖智能体自我检测错误、在有缺陷的轨迹中选择，或仅在错误已影响推理路径后才进行修正。

Method: ExComm是一种用于探索阶段智能体测试时扩展的通信协议。其核心机制包括：1) 周期性审计智能体信念状态以检测跨智能体事实冲突；2) 通过专用工具验证循环解决冲突；3) 向涉事智能体返回简洁、有针对性的反馈；4) 采用软信念更新——仅追加已验证反馈而非覆盖现有信念；5) 引入轨迹多样化模块，将冗余轨迹重定向至正交策略以防止通信导致的轨迹多样性崩溃。

Result: 在AIME 2024、AIME 2025和GAIA上使用Gemini-2.5-Flash-Lite和Qwen3.5-4B进行的实验表明，ExComm始终优于强测试时扩展基准方法，分别实现5.7%和5.0%的平均性能提升。进一步分析表明其具有改进的错误恢复能力、良好的扩展行为、强于适应版通信基准的多样性，以及评估方法中最佳的性能-成本权衡。

Conclusion: ExComm在多个基准测试中实现了最佳的性能-成本权衡，显著改善了错误恢复能力，表现出良好的扩展行为，并有效维持了轨迹多样性。

Abstract: A common failure mode in long-horizon agentic test-time scaling is error propagation, where factual errors or invalid deductions introduced at intermediate steps persist in the agent's belief state and contaminate later reasoning. Existing test-time scaling methods provide limited control over this process, as they often rely on agents to detect their own mistakes, select among flawed trajectories, or refine solutions only after errors have already shaped the reasoning path. We propose ExComm, a communication protocol for exploration-stage agentic test-time scaling. ExComm is motivated by the empirical observation that the majority of intermediate errors in parallel agentic reasoning produce detectable cross-agent factual conflicts. Leveraging the iterative structure of agentic workflows, ExComm periodically audits agent belief states to detect such conflicts, resolves them through a dedicated tool-based verification loop, and returns concise, targeted feedback to the involved agents. Corrections are incorporated through soft belief updates, which append verified feedback rather than overwriting existing beliefs. Furthermore, to prevent collapsing trajectory diversity due to communication, ExComm further introduces a trajectory diversification module that redirects redundant trajectories toward orthogonal strategies.
